CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 1

Part 1: Food · Free time · Part 2: Online learning is becoming popular · Part 3: Online learning

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Food

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • healthy foodthức ăn lành mạnh, tốt cho sức khỏe
  • eat outăn ngoài nhà hàng/quán
  • home-cooked mealsbữa ăn nấu tại nhà
  • balanced dietchế độ ăn cân bằng, đủ chất
  • fast foodđồ ăn nhanh (hamburger, pizza...)
  • fresh ingredientsnguyên liệu tươi
  • traditional dishescác món ăn truyền thống
  • street foodđồ ăn vỉa hè, thức ăn đường phố
  • skip a mealbỏ bữa ăn
  • cooking habitthói quen nấu ăn
  • nutritiousgiàu dinh dưỡng
  • favourite cuisineẩm thực yêu thích
  • junk foodđồ ăn vặt không lành mạnh
  • meal prepchuẩn bị bữa ăn trước

Câu 1What kind of food do you like?

ANêu loại/món ăn yêu thích của bạn.

RGiải thích vì sao bạn thích loại đó (hương vị, sức khỏe, kỷ niệm...).

EKể tên một món cụ thể bạn hay ăn hoặc ăn gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I really like Vietnamese traditional dishes, especially pho and broken rice. I prefer them because they use fresh ingredients and are not too heavy for my stomach. As a medical student, I try to choose food that is nutritious and gives me energy for long study sessions. Last week I had a bowl of pho for breakfast and it kept me full for the whole morning.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My favourite kind of food is healthy food such as steamed fish and vegetables. I grew up eating simple home-cooked meals prepared by my mother, so I still prefer that style. I find that eating light, nutritious food helps me stay focused during my studies. For example, I usually have a salad and soup for lunch on busy hospital days.

Câu 2Do you prefer eating at home or eating out? Why?

AChọn rõ: ăn ở nhà hoặc ăn ngoài.

RĐưa ra lý do chính (vệ sinh, tiết kiệm, tiện lợi...).

EChia sẻ thói quen hoặc tình huống cụ thể của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer eating home-cooked meals because I can control what goes into my food. As someone studying medicine, I know how important food safety and hygiene are, so I feel more comfortable cooking at home. I also save money by not eating out every day. For example, I usually cook rice and soup in the evening and bring leftovers to the hospital the next morning.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I prefer to eat out, especially at small local restaurants near the academy. My schedule is very busy, so I rarely have time to cook after a long shift. Street food near the hospital is affordable and convenient for me. For instance, I often grab a banh mi or a bowl of noodles between classes, which saves me a lot of time.

Câu 3Is it important to eat healthy food?

AKhẳng định quan điểm của bạn (có/không và ở mức độ nào).

RGiải thích tác động của việc ăn lành mạnh (sức khỏe, học tập, làm việc).

ELiên hệ với kinh nghiệm học y hoặc cuộc sống hàng ngày.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe that eating healthy food is very important. As a medical student, I have learned that a balanced diet directly affects our immune system and concentration. If we eat too much junk food, we feel tired and find it hard to focus on studying. Personally, I try to eat more vegetables and avoid fried food during exam periods.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think eating healthy food is important, but I also understand it is not always easy, especially for busy students. A balanced diet gives us the energy and nutrients we need to get through long hospital shifts. However, sometimes I do eat fast food when I am short on time. I try to balance it by drinking enough water and eating fruit when I can.

Câu 4How many meals do you eat per day?

ANêu số bữa ăn mỗi ngày của bạn.

RGiải thích lý do dựa trên lịch học/lịch làm việc của bạn.

EMô tả một ngày ăn uống điển hình của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I normally eat three meals a day - breakfast, lunch, and dinner. I think having regular meals is important for keeping my energy levels stable during long study days. I never like to skip a meal, especially breakfast, because it helps me concentrate in the morning classes. A typical day for me starts with rice or bread, then a simple lunch at the canteen, and a light dinner at home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, because of my busy schedule, I sometimes only manage two proper meals a day. I often skip a meal at lunchtime when I have clinical practice in the afternoon. I know this is not the best cooking habit or eating pattern, but I try to make up for it with a bigger, more nutritious dinner. I am trying to improve this by doing some meal prep on weekends.

Câu 5Do you know how to cook? What can you make?

ANói bạn có biết nấu ăn không và ở mức độ nào.

RGiải thích bạn học nấu ăn từ đâu hoặc tại sao bạn nấu/không nấu.

EKể một món bạn có thể tự nấu được.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I can cook basic dishes quite well. I learned from my mother when I was in high school, and I have continued to practice since I moved to the city for university. I enjoy making home-cooked meals like stir-fried vegetables and boiled eggs because they are quick and nutritious. My favourite thing to cook is a simple Vietnamese soup with fresh ingredients from the market.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not a great cook, but I can manage a few simple dishes. Living away from home has pushed me to develop some basic cooking habits so I do not have to eat out every day. I can make instant noodles, fried rice, and steamed vegetables. These are not exciting meals, but they are enough for a student and much cheaper than eating at restaurants.

Câu 6What did you have for breakfast this morning?

AMô tả bữa sáng cụ thể của bạn hôm nay.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn món đó (thói quen, thời gian, sở thích).

ENói thêm về việc bạn ăn sáng thường xuyên hay không.

Trả lời mẫu 1

This morning I had a bowl of pho from a small shop near the academy. I chose it because it is one of my favourite traditional dishes and it fills me up for a long morning of lectures. I try not to skip a meal like breakfast because I know it affects my ability to concentrate. I find that a warm, nutritious breakfast makes a real difference to my energy levels.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I only had a piece of bread and a glass of milk this morning because I was in a hurry. I did not have time to prepare a proper meal before leaving for the hospital. I know that a better balanced diet in the morning would help me perform better, but sometimes convenience wins. On weekends I usually eat a more filling breakfast with rice and soup to compensate.

Câu 7Do you eat a lot of fast food? Why or why not?

ACho biết bạn có ăn nhiều đồ ăn nhanh không.

RGiải thích lý do (lịch bận, sức khỏe, tài chính...).

EKể ví dụ cụ thể về lần bạn ăn hoặc tránh đồ ăn nhanh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not eat a lot of fast food because I am aware of its negative effects on health. As a medical student, I have studied how excessive junk food consumption can lead to obesity and heart disease. I prefer to spend a little more time preparing simple home-cooked meals instead. For example, last month I made a habit of cooking rice every evening to avoid buying fast food on the way home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I must admit I eat fast food more often than I should, mainly because of my packed schedule. After a long day of clinical rounds, I sometimes just grab whatever is quick and available, which is often junk food or instant noodles. However, I am trying to cut down and replace these meals with more nutritious options. I recently started buying salads from a healthier stall near the academy.

Câu 8Have you ever tried food from another country? What was it like?

ANêu tên một món ăn nước ngoài bạn đã thử.

RMô tả cảm nhận của bạn về hương vị hoặc trải nghiệm đó.

ESo sánh với món ăn Việt Nam hoặc chia sẻ bạn có muốn ăn lại không.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have tried Japanese food, specifically sushi and ramen, at a restaurant in Ho Chi Minh City. I found it quite different from our traditional dishes because the flavours were less spicy and the presentation was very beautiful. The ramen had a very rich broth made from fresh ingredients, which I really enjoyed. I would definitely eat it again, although I still prefer Vietnamese food in my daily life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I once tried Korean barbecue with some friends and it was a great experience. The idea of grilling your own meat at the table was very different from how we normally eat out in Vietnam. The food was flavourful but quite heavy and oily compared to our lighter traditional dishes. It was fun for a special occasion, but I think I would not eat it regularly because it is not very nutritious for an everyday meal.

Câu 9What is your favourite Vietnamese dish and why?

ANêu tên món ăn Việt Nam yêu thích của bạn.

RGiải thích điều gì làm bạn thích món đó (hương vị, kỷ niệm, nguyên liệu).

EKể khi nào hoặc ở đâu bạn thường ăn món đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite Vietnamese dish is bun bo Hue, a spicy beef noodle soup from Central Vietnam. I love it because the broth is rich and flavourful, made from fresh ingredients like lemongrass and shrimp paste. It is one of the most satisfying traditional dishes I have ever eaten. Whenever I feel homesick, I find a restaurant that serves it and it always makes me feel better.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I love com tam, or broken rice, which is very popular in the South. It is one of my favourite traditional dishes because it is filling, affordable, and full of variety - you can have grilled pork, a fried egg, and pickled vegetables all on one plate. I usually eat out and order it for lunch when I am near Ben Thanh market. It reminds me of growing up in Saigon and eating it with my family.

Câu 10Do you think your eating habits are healthy? Why or why not?

AĐánh giá thói quen ăn uống của bạn (tốt hay chưa tốt).

RĐưa ra lý do hoặc bằng chứng cụ thể từ cuộc sống của bạn.

ENêu một thói quen tốt hoặc một điều bạn muốn cải thiện.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think my eating habits are fairly healthy food-oriented, but not perfect. I try to eat a balanced diet with vegetables, protein, and rice at most meals, and I rarely eat junk food. However, I sometimes skip a meal when I have back-to-back hospital duties, which is not ideal. I want to improve my meal prep routine so that I always have something good to eat, even on the busiest days.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, my eating habits are not as healthy as I would like them to be. I eat too much fast food and street food because I do not have much time to cook. I know a balanced diet is essential for both my health and my performance as a future doctor, which makes me feel a little guilty. I am planning to start doing meal prep on Sunday evenings to make healthier choices during the week.

Câu 11Do you prefer spicy food or mild food?

ANêu rõ sở thích của bạn: cay hay nhạt.

RGiải thích lý do (thể chất, vùng miền, thói quen từ nhỏ...).

EKể ví dụ về một món bạn yêu thích tương ứng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer mild food because spicy food sometimes upsets my stomach, especially during stressful exam periods. I grew up in the North where traditional dishes tend to be less spicy than those in the South or Central region. I still enjoy a little chilli in my food, but I do not like it when the spice overpowers the other flavours. For example, I love pho with just a small amount of chilli sauce on the side.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I love spicy food and I think it makes meals much more exciting. I am originally from Central Vietnam where almost every dish is quite hot, so I grew up with a high tolerance for spice. My favourite traditional dishes, like bun bo Hue and mi Quang, are both very spicy and very flavourful. Even when I eat out in Hanoi, I always ask for extra chilli because the food here is often too mild for my taste.

Câu 12How often do you eat fruit and vegetables?

ANêu tần suất bạn ăn rau và trái cây.

RGiải thích thái độ của bạn về việc ăn rau quả (thích/không thích/cần thiết).

EKể loại rau/trái cây bạn hay ăn nhất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I try to eat vegetables at every meal because I know they are essential for a balanced diet. I usually have stir-fried or boiled vegetables with rice for lunch and dinner. I also eat fruit at least once a day, usually a banana or some seasonal Vietnamese fruit. Eating these fresh ingredients regularly helps me feel lighter and more energetic during long study days.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I will be honest - I do not eat enough fruit and vegetables. My schedule is so busy that I often rely on fast food or canteen food, which does not always include enough greens. I know this is not part of a balanced diet and could affect my long-term health. I am trying to change this by keeping some fruit like mangoes and dragon fruit in my room for easy healthy food snacking.

Câu 13Is there any food you dislike or cannot eat? Why?

ANêu một món hoặc loại thực phẩm bạn không thích hoặc không ăn được.

RGiải thích lý do (dị ứng, mùi vị, kết cấu...).

EKể một lần bạn phải từ chối món đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I really dislike durian, even though many Vietnamese people love it. The smell is too strong for me and I feel nauseous when I am near it. I know it is actually quite nutritious and high in vitamins, but I just cannot bring myself to eat it. Once at a family gathering, everyone was eating durian desserts and I had to sit at the other end of the table because the smell was too overwhelming for me.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I cannot eat seafood because I am allergic to shrimps and crabs. This makes it quite difficult when I eat out at Vietnamese restaurants, where seafood is very common in many traditional dishes. I always have to check the ingredients carefully before ordering to avoid a reaction. Despite this limitation, I still manage to enjoy a wide variety of healthy food options that do not involve seafood.

Câu 14Do you think the food at your school canteen is good?

AĐưa ra nhận xét của bạn về đồ ăn ở căn-tin trường.

RGiải thích lý do với các chi tiết cụ thể (chất lượng, giá cả, lựa chọn...).

EKể bạn có thường ăn ở đó không và tại sao.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the canteen at our academy is quite good for the price. The food is simple but generally nutritious, with rice, meat, and vegetables available every day. It is much more affordable than eating at restaurants nearby, which is important for students on a tight budget. I eat there for lunch almost every weekday because it saves me both time and money.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The canteen food is acceptable, but I would not say it is great. The menu does not change much, so after a few weeks the traditional dishes start to feel repetitive and a little boring. The portions are decent and the price is low, but I sometimes feel the food lacks enough fresh ingredients and variety. On days when I have more time, I prefer to eat out at small local restaurants where the food tastes more homemade.

Câu 15Do you ever cook for others? How do you feel about it?

ANói bạn có thường nấu ăn cho người khác không.

RChia sẻ cảm xúc của bạn khi nấu cho người khác (vui/căng thẳng/tự hào...).

EKể một lần bạn nấu ăn cho ai đó và kết quả như thế nào.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I sometimes cook for my roommates on weekends when we all have a day off. I enjoy preparing home-cooked meals for others because it is a way to relax after a stressful week. I usually make simple dishes using fresh ingredients from the morning market, like soup and stir-fried vegetables. My friends always seem to appreciate it, which makes me feel very happy and proud.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I rarely cook for others because I am not very confident in my cooking skills. My cooking habit is mostly limited to quick meals just for myself. However, last Tet holiday I helped my mother prepare some traditional dishes for the family, and it was a wonderful experience. Seeing everyone enjoy the food I helped make gave me a sense of accomplishment and made me want to improve my cooking more.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Free time

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • free timethời gian rảnh rỗi
  • relaxthư giãn
  • unwindxả stress, thả lỏng
  • hobbysở thích, thú vui
  • leisure activityhoạt động giải trí
  • go for a walkđi dạo
  • catch up with friendsgặp gỡ, trò chuyện với bạn bè
  • rechargenạp lại năng lượng
  • spend time ondành thời gian cho
  • work-life balancesự cân bằng giữa công việc và cuộc sống
  • outdoor activitieshoạt động ngoài trời
  • keep fitgiữ dáng, duy trì sức khỏe
  • stress reliefgiải tỏa căng thẳng
  • pastimehoạt động tiêu khiển

Câu 1What do you usually do in your free time?

ANêu thẳng hoạt động bạn thường làm khi rảnh.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích hoạt động đó.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về lần gần nhất bạn làm việc đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my free time, I usually go for a walk around the hospital campus or nearby park. I enjoy this because it helps me unwind after long and demanding shifts. For example, last Sunday I spent about an hour walking along the lake near my dormitory and I felt completely refreshed afterward.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I mostly spend time on reading medical journals and sometimes fiction novels when I relax in the evening. Reading helps me keep my mind active even outside of work. Last week, for instance, I finished a short novel about a Vietnamese doctor, which I found both enjoyable and inspiring.

Câu 2Do you prefer spending your free time alone or with friends? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích ở một mình hay với bạn bè.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn có sở thích đó.

EKể một ví dụ cụ thể về thời gian rảnh gần đây của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer spending my free time with friends rather than alone. Being with people helps me recharge emotionally, especially after stressful days in the clinical ward. Last weekend, my study group had a small dinner together and it was a great way to catch up with friends and forget about work for a while.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I prefer spending my free time alone most of the time. I find that quiet time by myself allows me to truly unwind and process everything from the week. For example, I enjoy sitting quietly with a cup of tea and listening to soft music, which is my favourite stress relief routine after a busy week.

Câu 3Do you think people today have enough free time?

ANêu rõ quan điểm của bạn (có/không/tùy trường hợp).

RGiải thích nguyên nhân dẫn đến tình trạng đó trong xã hội hiện đại.

ELấy ví dụ từ cuộc sống của bạn hoặc người xung quanh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think most people today have enough free time, especially those who work in healthcare. The demands of modern life and long working hours leave very little room for leisure activities. For example, many of my fellow residents work overnight shifts several times a week and barely have time to relax properly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think it really depends on the person and their job. Some people manage their schedules well and find time for hobbies and outdoor activities, while others are constantly overwhelmed. Personally, I sometimes struggle with work-life balance, but I try to protect at least a few hours each week just for myself.

Câu 4What hobbies did you have before you started medical school?

AKể tên một hoặc hai sở thích bạn có trước khi học y.

RGiải thích vì sao bạn thích những sở thích đó hồi đó.

ENói về sự thay đổi so với bây giờ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Before medical school, my main hobby was playing badminton with friends every weekend. I loved it because it was a fun way to keep fit while also socialising. Now I rarely have time for it, but I hope to get back into it once my schedule is lighter.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I used to paint watercolour pictures as a pastime when I was in secondary school and university. It was a wonderful way to express creativity and unwind after studying. Unfortunately, since starting my postgraduate programme, I have had very little time to continue this leisure activity.

Câu 5How often do you exercise in your free time?

ANêu tần suất bạn tập thể dục.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn tập hoặc không tập thường xuyên.

EMô tả loại bài tập bạn thường làm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I try to exercise about three times a week, usually in the early morning before my shift starts. I think outdoor activities like jogging are important for both physical health and mental stress relief. My usual routine is a thirty-minute jog around the base followed by some stretching.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I only manage to exercise once or twice a week because of my busy schedule. However, I believe that even a small amount of movement helps me keep fit and recharge for the next day. I usually do simple exercises like push-ups or go for a walk around the ward building during my break.

Câu 6Do you enjoy watching films or series in your free time?

ANói rõ bạn có thích xem phim không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích hoặc không thích.

ENêu một bộ phim hoặc thể loại bạn thích gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy watching medical dramas in my free time because they are both entertaining and relevant to my career. They help me relax while also giving me new perspectives on doctor-patient relationships. Recently I watched a Korean series about an emergency physician, which I found very moving and realistic.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not watch films very often because I find it hard to sit still for that long after a demanding day. I prefer shorter leisure activities like listening to podcasts or music to unwind. But occasionally, when I have a long holiday, I enjoy watching documentaries about nature or history as a pastime.

Câu 7Do you like cooking in your free time?

ANói bạn có thích nấu ăn không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích hoặc không thích nấu ăn.

EKể món bạn thường nấu hoặc muốn học nấu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really like cooking when I have free time, especially on weekends. For me, cooking is a creative hobby that also helps me recharge and think about something other than medicine. I enjoy making simple Vietnamese dishes like pho or stir-fried vegetables, which remind me of home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not very confident in the kitchen so I do not cook often in my free time. I tend to prefer other ways to relax, such as reading or walking. However, I do want to improve my cooking skills because I think preparing your own meals is important for a healthy work-life balance.

Câu 8Do you spend much of your free time using your phone or social media?

ANêu bạn có dùng điện thoại hoặc mạng xã hội nhiều không.

RGiải thích tác động tích cực hoặc tiêu cực của thói quen đó.

EMô tả cụ thể bạn thường làm gì trên điện thoại.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I must admit that I do spend time on my phone quite a lot in my free time, mainly scrolling through news and medical education pages. It helps me stay informed, but sometimes I feel it stops me from truly unwinding. I am trying to limit my screen time at night so I can sleep better.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to keep my phone use limited during leisure activities because I think it is important to disconnect from screens in order to recharge. Instead of social media, I prefer to catch up with friends in person or read a book. I believe this habit is better for my mental health and overall work-life balance.

Câu 9Have you ever taken up a new hobby as an adult?

AKể một sở thích mới mà bạn đã bắt đầu khi trưởng thành.

RGiải thích điều gì thúc đẩy bạn thử sở thích đó.

EMô tả trải nghiệm của bạn với sở thích mới đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I took up jogging as a hobby when I started my residency two years ago. I began because I needed a simple and affordable way to keep fit and deal with stress. Now I run about four kilometres every other morning and it has become one of my favourite forms of stress relief.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I recently started learning to play the guitar in my free time and it has been a wonderful experience. I wanted a creative pastime that was completely different from medicine to help me unwind. I am still a beginner, but even practising for fifteen minutes makes me feel calm and happy.

Câu 10Do you prefer indoor or outdoor activities in your free time?

ANêu bạn thích hoạt động trong nhà hay ngoài trời hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn có sở thích đó.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về hoạt động bạn thích.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I definitely prefer outdoor activities in my free time because I spend most of my working hours indoors in the hospital. Being outside in fresh air is the best way for me to recharge and clear my mind. I especially enjoy going for a walk in the park or cycling along the river on my days off.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I tend to prefer indoor leisure activities because the weather here can be very hot and humid. My favourite ways to relax at home include reading, listening to music, or doing light yoga in my room. I find these activities perfect for helping me unwind without needing to go anywhere.

Câu 11How do you usually spend your days off?

AMô tả một ngày nghỉ điển hình của bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích cách bố trí ngày nghỉ như vậy.

EKể về một ngày nghỉ gần đây đáng nhớ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

On my days off, I usually start with a slow morning and then spend time on something creative like reading or sketching. I value this unstructured time because it lets me fully unwind without any schedule or pressure. Last month, I spent an entire Sunday afternoon at a coffee shop reading a novel and it was one of the most relaxing days I have had in months.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to make my days off as active as possible by planning outdoor activities or visiting friends. I believe a good work-life balance means using days off well, not just sleeping all day. For example, last week I joined a group of colleagues to hike a small mountain near Hanoi, which was great fun and excellent stress relief.

Câu 12Do you think free time is important for your health as a medical student?

ANêu quan điểm rõ ràng về tầm quan trọng của thời gian rảnh.

RGiải thích lý do bằng kiến thức y khoa hoặc kinh nghiệm thực tế.

EChia sẻ điều gì xảy ra khi bạn không có đủ thời gian rảnh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I absolutely believe that free time is essential for our health, especially in medicine. Without time to relax and recharge, burnout becomes a very real risk for doctors and students alike. I noticed this myself last year when I had no days off for three weeks and my concentration and mood both suffered significantly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, I think free time is vital for both physical and mental health. As medical students, we learn about the importance of stress relief for patients, so we should apply the same principles to ourselves. Even short periods of leisure activities during the week help me stay focused and perform better in clinical settings.

Câu 13Is there any activity you would like to try in your free time but have not done yet?

ANêu một hoạt động bạn muốn thử nhưng chưa làm.

RGiải thích lý do bạn muốn thử hoạt động đó.

ENói về kế hoạch hoặc thời điểm bạn muốn thực hiện.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I have always wanted to try swimming as a regular hobby but I have not started yet. I think it is one of the best outdoor activities for keeping the whole body healthy without putting stress on the joints. I plan to join the swimming club at the academy next semester when my schedule becomes a little easier.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would really love to try meditation and mindfulness practice in my free time. I have read a lot about how it can improve focus and act as a powerful form of stress relief, which is something I definitely need. I hope to join a beginner class this summer so I can learn the basics properly.

Câu 14Do you listen to music in your free time? What kind?

ANêu bạn có nghe nhạc không và thể loại nhạc bạn thích.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích thể loại nhạc đó.

EKể về một bài hát hoặc nghệ sĩ bạn yêu thích gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, listening to music is one of my favourite ways to unwind in my free time. I mostly enjoy soft acoustic or classical music because it helps me calm down after a busy shift. I recently discovered a Vietnamese acoustic artist whose songs make me feel peaceful and nostalgic, and I have been listening to his album every evening.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do listen to music quite often, especially when I am doing other leisure activities like cooking or exercising. I prefer upbeat pop music because it gives me energy and makes everyday tasks feel more enjoyable. Music is a simple but effective stress relief tool that I would recommend to anyone who needs a quick mood boost.

Câu 15How has your idea of free time changed since you became a postgraduate student?

ASo sánh quan niệm về thời gian rảnh của bạn trước và sau khi học sau đại học.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân dẫn đến sự thay đổi đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ cụ thể về sự thay đổi trong thói quen của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My idea of free time has changed a lot since I started my postgraduate programme. Before, I thought leisure activities meant long trips or going out with friends, but now even thirty minutes to relax quietly feels precious. I appreciate small moments much more now, such as having a peaceful breakfast or going for a walk without checking my phone.

Trả lời mẫu 2

As a postgraduate student, I have come to see free time as something I must protect and plan for, not just hope will happen naturally. I now actively try to maintain a healthy work-life balance by scheduling at least one activity each week that helps me recharge. This mindset shift has genuinely improved my overall wellbeing and made me a more focused and effective student.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Online learning is becoming popular

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • online learninghọc trực tuyến
  • e-learning platformnền tảng học điện tử
  • study anywherehọc ở bất cứ đâu
  • flexible schedulelịch học linh hoạt
  • self-paced learninghọc theo tốc độ của bản thân
  • tuition feehọc phí
  • commute to schoolđi lại đến trường
  • digital devicethiết bị kỹ thuật số
  • internet connectionkết nối internet
  • video lecturebài giảng video
  • interactive contentnội dung tương tác
  • save time and moneytiết kiệm thời gian và tiền bạc
  • access course materialstruy cập tài liệu học
  • improve skillsnâng cao kỹ năng

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

Today I would like to talk about why online learning is becoming more and more popular, especially among students like us. There are several reasons why I think this trend is growing so fast. First of all, online learning allows students to study anywhere they want. You do not need to be in a classroom. For example, I can watch a video lecture on my phone while I am sitting at home or even on the bus. This is very helpful for busy students. Another reason is that it is much more convenient. With a digital device and a good internet connection, you can access course materials at any time you like. You can follow a flexible schedule and enjoy self-paced learning, so you do not feel too much pressure. In addition, online learning helps students save time and money. You do not have to commute to school every day, and the tuition fee for many online courses is much lower than at traditional schools. On top of that, I personally think that interactive content, such as quizzes and games on e-learning platforms, makes studying more fun and helps us improve skills faster. To sum up, online learning is a great choice because it is flexible, affordable, and enjoyable for modern students.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Online learning (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • online learninghọc trực tuyến
  • face-to-face learninghọc trực tiếp / học mặt đối mặt
  • flexible schedulelịch học linh hoạt
  • self-disciplinetính tự giác, kỷ luật bản thân
  • interactive activitieshoạt động tương tác
  • blended learninghọc kết hợp (trực tuyến + trực tiếp)
  • digital skillskỹ năng kỹ thuật số
  • internet connectionkết nối internet
  • practical trainingđào tạo thực hành
  • learning platformnền tảng học tập
  • technical issuessự cố kỹ thuật
  • study at your own pacehọc theo tốc độ của bản thân
  • access to resourcestiếp cận tài nguyên / nguồn học liệu
  • virtual classroomlớp học ảo / lớp học trực tuyến

Câu 1Why do many people choose online learning today?

ANêu lý do chính: sự tiện lợi và tính linh hoạt.

RGiải thích: tiết kiệm thời gian đi lại, có thể học mọi lúc mọi nơi.

EVí dụ: nhân viên văn phòng hoặc bác sĩ học thêm ngoài giờ làm việc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people choose online learning because it offers a very flexible schedule. They do not need to travel to a classroom, so they save a lot of time and money. For example, busy professionals like doctors can study at your own pace after work without missing important shifts. This makes it much easier to balance study and personal life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people prefer online learning today mainly because of the wide access to resources available on the internet. Students can find lectures, videos, and materials from top universities around the world for free or at low cost. A medical student, for instance, can watch surgery demonstrations on a learning platform that would not be available in a local library. This is a huge advantage compared to traditional study.

Câu 2Is online learning suitable for all students? Why or why not?

ANêu quan điểm: không phù hợp với tất cả học sinh/sinh viên.

RGiải thích: một số người thiếu tính tự giác hoặc không có điều kiện kỹ thuật.

EVí dụ: học sinh nhỏ tuổi cần giáo viên hướng dẫn trực tiếp; sinh viên y khoa cần thực hành.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think online learning is suitable for all students. Some learners, especially younger children, need direct guidance and interactive activities that are hard to do through a screen. Without enough self-discipline, many students may lose focus easily and fall behind. For example, a ten-year-old child needs a teacher nearby to stay on task and ask questions immediately.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe online learning works well for motivated adult learners but not for everyone. Students who lack a stable internet connection or the necessary digital skills will struggle to keep up. Medical students, in particular, also need practical training that cannot be replaced by watching videos online. So while online study is useful for theory, it cannot fully replace the classroom for all types of learners.

Câu 3Should schools combine online and face-to-face learning?

ANêu quan điểm: nên kết hợp cả hai hình thức.

RGiải thích: tận dụng ưu điểm của cả hai, bù đắp nhược điểm của từng hình thức.

EVí dụ: học lý thuyết online, thực hành kỹ năng trực tiếp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe schools should combine online learning and face-to-face learning through blended learning models. Online lessons give students the freedom to review content at home at their own pace, while classroom sessions allow real discussion and hands-on practice. For medical schools, students can watch recorded lectures online but must attend hospital wards in person for practical training. This balance gives the best results for both knowledge and skills.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that schools should use a blended learning approach. Relying only on online learning can make students feel isolated and reduce opportunities for teamwork. On the other hand, only face-to-face learning limits flexibility for busy students. By mixing both, for example using a virtual classroom for theory and real labs for experiments, schools can meet the needs of different types of learners more effectively.

Câu 4How do you think technology has changed the way students learn?

ANêu quan điểm: công nghệ đã thay đổi việc học theo hướng tích cực.

RGiải thích: học sinh có thể tiếp cận thông tin dễ dàng hơn và học theo tốc độ riêng.

EVí dụ: ứng dụng học trực tuyến, video bài giảng, bài kiểm tra online.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think technology has completely changed how students learn by making access to resources much easier. In the past, students had to rely only on textbooks and library books, but now they can find information on any learning platform within seconds. For instance, a nursing student can watch a video on how to take blood pressure correctly as many times as needed. This ability to study at your own pace is a major improvement over traditional methods.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, technology has made learning more interactive activities and engaging than before. Students can now join virtual classroom sessions, take online quizzes, and collaborate with classmates from different cities. However, technology also brings challenges such as technical issues and distractions from social media. Overall, I think the benefits outweigh the problems if students use technology wisely and with clear goals.

Câu 5Do you think online learning is more effective than traditional classroom learning?

ANêu quan điểm: không hẳn hiệu quả hơn, phụ thuộc vào môn học và người học.

RGiải thích: học trực tiếp giúp tương tác và thực hành tốt hơn, đặc biệt với ngành y.

EVí dụ: kỹ năng khám bệnh cần thực hành với bệnh nhân thật, không thể học qua màn hình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think online learning is always more effective than face-to-face learning. For subjects that require a lot of memorization or reading, online study can be very convenient. But for medical training, practical training with real patients and equipment is essential and cannot be replaced by a screen. A doctor who has only studied online without clinical practice would not be ready to treat patients safely.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe the effectiveness depends on the student and the subject. Online learning allows students to study at your own pace and revisit difficult content many times, which can improve understanding. However, interactive activities in a physical classroom, such as group discussions and role plays, help develop communication skills better. For my field of medicine, I think a combination of both is the most effective approach.

Câu 6What are the main disadvantages of online learning?

ANêu quan điểm: nhược điểm chính là thiếu tương tác và đòi hỏi tính tự giác cao.

RGiải thích: học sinh dễ mất tập trung, thiếu cơ hội thực hành và giao tiếp trực tiếp.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa thiếu giờ thực hành lâm sàng nếu chỉ học online.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my opinion, one of the biggest disadvantages of online learning is the lack of interactive activities and real human connection. Students miss out on spontaneous discussions, peer support, and direct feedback from teachers. Another major problem is the need for strong self-discipline, because without a fixed schedule it is easy to delay studying. For example, a student with many home responsibilities may keep postponing online lessons until it is too late.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the main drawbacks of online learning are technical issues and the difficulty of practical training. A poor internet connection can interrupt a lecture at a critical moment, causing students to miss important information. More seriously, medical skills like physical examinations and emergency procedures simply cannot be learned effectively through a screen. These limitations mean that online study must always be supported by real-world practice for medical professionals.

Câu 7How can teachers make online classes more engaging for students?

ANêu quan điểm: giáo viên nên dùng các hoạt động tương tác và công cụ đa dạng.

RGiải thích: học sinh tập trung hơn khi có câu hỏi thảo luận, bài tập nhóm và phản hồi kịp thời.

EVí dụ: dùng poll, breakout rooms, video clip ngắn trong giờ học online.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think teachers can make online learning more engaging by including more interactive activities such as live polls, group discussions, and short quizzes during the lesson. When students are asked questions frequently, they are more likely to stay focused and participate. For example, a teacher can use a virtual classroom tool with a whiteboard feature so students can write answers together in real time. This makes the session feel more like a real classroom.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, the key to engaging online learning is variety and regular feedback. Teachers should mix short video clips, reading tasks, and discussions rather than just lecturing for a long time. Using a good learning platform that allows students to message the teacher easily also helps learners feel supported. For medical students, virtual case studies where they have to diagnose a patient online can be both educational and genuinely interesting.

Câu 8Do you think students need strong self-discipline to succeed in online learning?

ANêu quan điểm: có, tính tự giác là yếu tố quan trọng nhất.

RGiải thích: không có giáo viên giám sát trực tiếp, học sinh phải tự quản lý thời gian.

EVí dụ: sinh viên không có lịch cố định dễ trì hoãn và bỏ lỡ bài học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe that self-discipline is the most important quality for success in online learning. Without a fixed classroom time and a teacher watching over you, it is very easy to get distracted or delay your studies. For example, many students start with good intentions but gradually stop logging in to the learning platform because there is no one to remind them. Students who cannot manage their own time will struggle greatly in an online environment.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that self-discipline is essential, but I also think a good flexible schedule and a supportive environment help. Even a motivated student can lose focus if they study in a noisy place or if they have too many family responsibilities. I know some medical students who set a fixed time every day to attend their virtual classroom and treat it as seriously as a hospital shift. This kind of routine makes online learning much more manageable and effective.

Câu 9Should the government provide free internet access to support online learning?

ANêu quan điểm: có, chính phủ nên hỗ trợ internet miễn phí.

RGiải thích: đảm bảo bình đẳng giáo dục cho học sinh ở vùng nông thôn và gia đình khó khăn.

EVí dụ: học sinh miền núi không có internet sẽ không thể tham gia học trực tuyến.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think the government should definitely provide free or affordable internet connection to support online learning for all students. Without reliable internet, students from poor or rural areas simply cannot participate in virtual classroom sessions or access any learning platform. This creates a serious gap in education quality between rich and poor students. Equal access to resources should be a basic right in modern society, and the government has a responsibility to ensure this.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that governments should support internet connection for students, but I also think schools and communities should share this responsibility. The government alone cannot solve the digital gap immediately, so local businesses and international organizations can also help by donating devices and data packages. In the meantime, schools should design online learning materials that work on low data, such as downloadable audio files, so that more students can benefit even with limited connectivity.

Câu 10How do you think online learning affects students' social skills?

ANêu quan điểm: học online có thể làm giảm kỹ năng xã hội nếu không có sự bù đắp.

RGiải thích: thiếu giao tiếp trực tiếp hằng ngày khiến học sinh kém tự tin và ít kỹ năng làm việc nhóm.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa cần kỹ năng giao tiếp với bệnh nhân, không thể phát triển qua màn hình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe that too much online learning can negatively affect students' social skills over time. When students only communicate through a screen, they miss the natural practice of reading body language, making eye contact, and responding quickly in conversation. For medical professionals especially, communicating clearly and warmly with patients is a critical skill that must be developed through real face-to-face learning interactions. If students spend all their time in a virtual classroom, they may become less confident in real-life situations.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the effect on social skills depends on how the online learning is designed. If teachers include group projects, peer discussions, and interactive activities in their online courses, students can still develop teamwork and communication skills to some extent. However, there is no perfect substitute for meeting classmates and teachers in person. I think students should be encouraged to join clubs, study groups, or volunteering activities outside their learning platform to maintain and improve their social abilities.

Câu 11Do you think online learning is better for adults than for young children?

ANêu quan điểm: có, người lớn thích nghi với học online tốt hơn trẻ nhỏ.

RGiải thích: người lớn có tính tự giác cao hơn và rõ mục tiêu học tập, trẻ nhỏ cần hướng dẫn trực tiếp.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ học thêm chứng chỉ online dễ hơn nhiều so với học sinh tiểu học học qua máy tính.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think online learning is generally more suitable for adults than for young children. Adults have a clearer purpose for studying and stronger self-discipline, so they can manage a flexible schedule more effectively. A doctor who wants to earn a new medical certificate, for example, can follow an online course independently without needing constant supervision. Young children, on the other hand, need a teacher physically present to guide them, encourage them, and manage their attention.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that adults benefit more from online learning, but I think the gap is getting smaller as technology improves. Modern learning platform tools now include cartoons, games, and fun interactive activities that can hold a young child's attention better than in the past. However, children still need social interaction with peers to develop language and emotional skills that screens cannot provide. So while adults can thrive fully online, children should have face-to-face learning as the main method with online tools used only as a supplement.

Câu 12How can students improve their digital skills to get the most from online learning?

ANêu quan điểm: học sinh cần chủ động học và thực hành các kỹ năng kỹ thuật số.

RGiải thích: biết dùng phần mềm, tìm kiếm thông tin đáng tin cậy và bảo mật trực tuyến giúp học hiệu quả hơn.

EVí dụ: tham gia khóa học ngắn về Word, PowerPoint hoặc cách tìm tài liệu y khoa online.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think students can improve their digital skills by starting with short practical courses on how to use common tools like word processors, video call apps, and learning platform software. It is also important to learn how to find reliable information online, especially for medical students who need accurate and up-to-date sources. For example, learning to search medical databases like PubMed is a valuable digital skills that helps students do better research for their studies and future careers.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, the best way to build digital skills is simply through regular practice and not being afraid to try new tools. Students should explore the features of their virtual classroom platform fully, such as recording sessions, sharing screens, and using annotation tools. Peer learning also helps a lot: when a classmate knows how to do something on a learning platform, they can teach the others quickly. Over time, these small steps add up to real confidence and efficiency in online learning.

Câu 13Should companies invest in online training programmes for their employees?

ANêu quan điểm: có, đầu tư đào tạo trực tuyến mang lại lợi ích cho cả công ty lẫn nhân viên.

RGiải thích: giúp nhân viên cập nhật kiến thức mà không cần nghỉ việc, tiết kiệm chi phí đào tạo.

EVí dụ: bệnh viện có thể đào tạo kỹ năng mới cho y tá và bác sĩ qua các module online.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe companies should invest in online learning programmes for their staff. It allows employees to update their knowledge and digital skills without taking long breaks from work, which saves both time and money for the organisation. In the medical field, for example, a hospital can create short online modules to train nurses on new procedures or updated guidelines, ensuring that the whole team stays current without needing to hire external trainers every time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that workplace online learning is a smart investment, but companies must also make sure the training is practical and relevant. Simply giving employees access to resources on a learning platform is not enough if those resources do not match their daily tasks. The best programmes combine online theory with short face-to-face learning workshops where staff can practise skills together. This blended learning approach ensures that employees actually apply what they learn and that the company gets a real return on its investment.

Câu 14Compare online learning and studying abroad. Which do you think is more beneficial?

ANêu quan điểm: du học có lợi ích toàn diện hơn nhưng học online phù hợp hơn với nhiều người.

RGiải thích: du học phát triển ngôn ngữ và văn hóa sâu hơn, nhưng học online tiết kiệm hơn và linh hoạt hơn.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ có gia đình khó bỏ việc đi du học, nhưng vẫn học được qua các khoá online quốc tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Studying abroad is an amazing experience because it develops language skills, cultural understanding, and independence all at once, which online learning simply cannot replicate. Living in another country forces you to communicate in a foreign language every single day in real situations. However, studying abroad is extremely expensive and not possible for most Vietnamese medical professionals who have family and work commitments. In comparison, a high-quality international online learning course gives access to resources from top universities at a fraction of the cost, making it the more realistic choice for many people.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think both options have clear strengths depending on a person's situation. Studying abroad offers deep cultural immersion and strong face-to-face learning interaction with international peers and experts, which is very valuable for developing a global perspective. On the other hand, online learning provides a flexible schedule that lets professionals continue working while they study. For a military doctor in Vietnam who cannot leave the country easily, completing a foreign specialist course through a virtual classroom is not just more practical but may be the only real option available.

Câu 15Do you think online learning will completely replace traditional schools in the future?

ANêu quan điểm: không, trường học truyền thống sẽ không bị thay thế hoàn toàn.

RGiải thích: trường học cung cấp môi trường xã hội, thực hành và kỷ luật mà học online không thể thay thế.

EVí dụ: trường y khoa luôn cần phòng thí nghiệm, bệnh viện thực hành và thầy cô hướng dẫn trực tiếp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think online learning will ever completely replace traditional schools, even with rapid advances in technology. Schools provide much more than just knowledge: they offer social environments where children learn to cooperate, resolve conflict, and build friendships that shape their character. For medical education especially, no virtual classroom can replace the experience of standing beside a patient in a real hospital ward and learning from a senior doctor in person. Practical training in medicine will always require a physical setting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe online learning will continue to grow and take on more of the roles that traditional schools currently play, but a full replacement is very unlikely. Many students need the structure, routine, and social contact that a physical school provides to stay motivated and develop as people. While blended learning will probably become the standard model in most institutions, the classroom as a space for human connection, hands-on experiments, and direct mentorship will remain essential. Face-to-face learning offers something that no screen or learning platform can fully reproduce.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 2

Part 1: Your hometown · Music · Part 2: Exercise is important for health · Part 3: Exercise

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Your hometown

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • hometownquê hương, thành phố/làng quê nơi sinh ra và lớn lên
  • local marketchợ địa phương
  • peaceful atmospherekhông khí yên bình
  • grow uplớn lên
  • close-knit communitycộng đồng gắn bó, thân thiết
  • traditional foodmón ăn truyền thống
  • move backchuyển về (nơi cũ)
  • sceneryphong cảnh
  • public transportphương tiện giao thông công cộng
  • miss homenhớ nhà
  • local festivallễ hội địa phương
  • childhood memorieský ức tuổi thơ
  • city lifecuộc sống thành thị
  • rural areavùng nông thôn

Câu 1Where is your hometown?

ANói tên tỉnh/thành phố và vị trí địa lý của nơi đó.

RMô tả ngắn gọn đặc điểm nổi bật của nơi đó (biển, núi, đô thị...).

EKể một điều cụ thể mà nơi đó nổi tiếng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown is Hue, a city in central Vietnam. It is famous for its historical sites and old royal palaces. The city sits along the Perfume River, which makes the scenery very beautiful. I was born there and grew up surrounded by its rich culture.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I come from a small rural area in Ha Giang province, in the northern mountains of Vietnam. It is quite far from Hanoi, about six hours by bus. The local community there is very close-knit community and the mountains are stunning. Not many tourists know about it, but I think it is a hidden gem.

Câu 2What do you like about your hometown?

ANêu thẳng điều bạn thích nhất ở quê hương.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó quan trọng hoặc có ý nghĩa với bạn.

EKể một kỷ niệm hoặc thói quen cụ thể liên quan đến điều đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

What I like most about my hometown is the peaceful atmosphere. Life there moves at a slower pace than in Hanoi, so people are less stressed. I always feel relaxed when I visit and enjoy walking through the local market in the early morning. The fresh air and scenery also make me feel very happy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I really love the traditional food in my hometown. Every region in Vietnam has its own dishes, and mine are truly special. For example, the local market sells a type of rice cake that I cannot find anywhere else. Whenever I miss home, I think about that food first.

Câu 3Would you like to live there in the future? Why or why not?

ATrả lời rõ có muốn sống ở quê hay không.

RĐưa ra lý do chính (công việc, gia đình, lối sống...).

EMô tả kế hoạch hoặc điều kiện cụ thể liên quan đến quyết định đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would love to move back to my hometown in the future. After finishing my medical training in Hanoi, I hope to work as a doctor in my home province. The local community there needs more qualified doctors, and I feel a responsibility to help them. Living near my family would also be a great benefit.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I am not sure I want to live in my hometown permanently. I grew up there and I have many childhood memories, but the job opportunities for a specialist doctor are limited. I think I will settle in a larger city where I can develop my career. However, I definitely plan to visit often because I miss home when I am away for too long.

Câu 4How far is your hometown from Hanoi?

ANói khoảng cách hoặc thời gian di chuyển cụ thể.

RGiải thích phương tiện giao thông thường dùng.

EChia sẻ cảm nhận về chuyến đi về quê.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown is about 300 kilometres from Hanoi, which takes around four hours by train. I usually travel home for local festival holidays and long weekends. The train journey is actually quite pleasant because I can enjoy the scenery along the way. I always feel excited when the train gets closer to home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My hometown is quite close to Hanoi, only about 80 kilometres away. I can catch a bus and arrive in less than two hours, so I go home quite often. Because it is so convenient, I never really feel too far from my close-knit community. Using public transport is easy and affordable for a student like me.

Câu 5What is the most famous thing about your hometown?

ANêu một điều nổi tiếng nhất của quê hương bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó nổi tiếng hoặc đặc biệt.

EKể một trải nghiệm cá nhân liên quan đến điều đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown is most famous for its local festival called the Hue Festival, which happens every two years. During the festival, the whole city fills with performances, lights, and traditional costumes. I grew up watching these performances and they always felt magical. Many tourists travel from all over Vietnam and the world to see it.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The most famous thing about my hometown is definitely its traditional food, especially the noodle soup called 'bun bo Hue'. It has a spicy and rich flavour that is completely different from pho in Hanoi. Every time a friend visits me, I take them to a local market to try it. They always say it is the best thing they have eaten in Vietnam.

Câu 6What did you enjoy doing as a child in your hometown?

AKể một hoạt động yêu thích hồi nhỏ ở quê.

RGiải thích điều gì làm cho hoạt động đó thú vị hoặc đáng nhớ.

ENêu một chi tiết cụ thể về ký ức đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

When I was a child, I loved cycling around my hometown with my friends after school. The streets were quiet and safe, so our parents allowed us to go quite far. We often stopped at a small shop near the local market to buy sweet snacks. Those childhood memories are still very clear in my mind today.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I used to love swimming in the river near my village. It was a favourite activity for all the children during the hot summers. The rural area where I grew up had very clean rivers at that time. It was simple fun, but it gave me some of my happiest childhood memories.

Câu 7Has your hometown changed much since you were young?

ANói rõ quê hương bạn đã thay đổi hay không.

RGiải thích những thay đổi cụ thể (giao thông, nhà cửa, dân số...).

ENêu cảm nhận cá nhân về sự thay đổi đó - tích cực hay tiêu cực.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my hometown has changed a lot since I was a child. Many new roads and buildings have been built, and public transport is much better now. The city life there feels busier and more modern. I think the changes are mostly positive, but I do miss the quieter peaceful atmosphere of my childhood.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My hometown has not changed very much, and that is actually something I appreciate. The rural area still looks similar to when I grew up there. The same families still run the same shops at the local market. It feels like a place that the rest of the world has forgotten, which I find very comforting.

Câu 8What is the weather like in your hometown?

AMô tả đặc điểm khí hậu chính của quê hương.

RGiải thích mùa nào bạn thích nhất và tại sao.

EKể một hoạt động thường làm vào mùa đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown in central Vietnam has quite hot and sunny weather for most of the year. However, the rainy season from October to December brings very heavy floods. I personally prefer the spring months when the scenery is green and beautiful. During that time, the local festival season also begins, which is always exciting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The weather in my hometown in the north is very different from Hanoi. It is cooler because of the mountains, and in winter it can actually get quite cold. I love the cool weather because it makes the rural area look misty and romantic. Going home in winter always feels like visiting a completely different world.

Câu 9Do you have many friends or relatives still living in your hometown?

ANói rõ bạn còn bao nhiêu người thân/bạn bè ở quê.

RGiải thích mối quan hệ của bạn với họ hiện tại.

EKể điều bạn thường làm khi gặp lại họ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, most of my family still lives in my hometown, including my parents and grandparents. I have a large extended family there, which is typical of our close-knit community. When I go back, we always have a big family meal together with lots of traditional food. These gatherings are the highlight of every visit for me.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Most of my old friends have actually moved away from my hometown to study or work in bigger cities. A few school friends still live there, and I always try to meet them when I visit. We usually walk around the old streets and share childhood memories. It is always a mix of happiness and a little sadness to see how much we have all changed.

Câu 10What do you miss most about your hometown when you are in Hanoi?

ANói thẳng điều bạn nhớ nhất khi xa quê.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó có ý nghĩa đặc biệt với bạn.

EKể bạn thường làm gì để vơi bớt nỗi nhớ quê.

Trả lời mẫu 1

When I am in Hanoi, I miss home most because of my mother's cooking. She makes traditional food from our region that I simply cannot find in any restaurant here. The taste brings back so many childhood memories that I feel very emotional sometimes. To cope, I sometimes call her and ask for the recipe so I can try to cook it myself.

Trả lời mẫu 2

What I miss home most for is the peaceful atmosphere of my hometown. Hanoi is very noisy and crowded, especially the traffic. In my hometown, I can wake up early and hear birds singing instead of motorbikes. I deal with this by going to a quiet park near my dormitory whenever I need to feel calm.

Câu 11Is your hometown a city or a smaller town or village?

AMô tả rõ quy mô và loại hình nơi bạn lớn lên.

RGiải thích ưu và nhược điểm của việc sống ở đó.

ESo sánh ngắn với cuộc sống hiện tại ở Hà Nội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown is a small provincial town, not a big city. It has a relaxed pace of life and a very friendly close-knit community. Compared to city life in Hanoi, everything is simpler and quieter. I sometimes miss the simplicity, but I also appreciate the opportunities that a big city offers for my medical studies.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am from a rural area, actually a small village with about a thousand people. There are no big shopping centres or cinemas there, just farmland, a local market, and a school. Growing up in such a small place taught me to be independent and creative with free time. Now that I experience city life in Hanoi, I value both environments for different reasons.

Câu 12What kind of food is your hometown known for?

AGiới thiệu một hoặc hai món ăn đặc trưng của quê hương.

RMô tả hương vị hoặc cách làm đặc biệt của món đó.

EKể một kỷ niệm hoặc thói quen ăn uống liên quan đến món đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My hometown is well known for its grilled pork rice, which is a traditional food that locals eat for breakfast. The pork is marinated overnight and cooked over charcoal, giving it a wonderful smoky flavour. Every Sunday morning, my family used to walk to the local market together to eat it. That weekly routine is one of my most treasured childhood memories.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The traditional food my hometown is most famous for is a type of fermented fish sauce used in cooking. It sounds unusual, but it gives dishes a very deep and unique flavour. I grew up eating food made with it every day and now I find ordinary food a little bland. I always bring a jar back to Hanoi after each visit home.

Câu 13How do people in your hometown usually get around?

AMô tả phương tiện đi lại phổ biến nhất ở quê hương.

RGiải thích tại sao phương tiện đó phù hợp với nơi bạn sống.

EKể thói quen đi lại của bản thân hồi còn sống ở quê.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my hometown, most people travel by motorbike because the town is too spread out to walk everywhere. Public transport is quite limited there, with only a few bus routes. When I grew up, I used to cycle to school every day because we lived quite close to it. The roads were not too busy, so cycling felt safe and enjoyable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My village is so small that most people simply walk or cycle to get around. There is no public transport at all, so a motorbike is considered essential for adults. As a child, I walked everywhere with my friends and knew every corner of the rural area. Now I find it strange to be in Hanoi where everyone is always rushing and traffic is chaotic.

Câu 14Are there any interesting places to visit in your hometown?

AGiới thiệu một hoặc hai địa điểm thú vị ở quê hương.

RGiải thích điều gì làm cho địa điểm đó đặc biệt hoặc đáng tham quan.

EKể một lần bạn đến đó hoặc đưa ai đó đến tham quan.

Trả lời mẫu 1

There is a beautiful old pagoda in my hometown that is over three hundred years old. It sits on a small hill surrounded by pine trees, which makes the scenery absolutely stunning. I often visit it during the local festival to pray and enjoy the atmosphere. Last year, I brought a classmate from Hanoi there and she was amazed by how peaceful it was.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My hometown has a wonderful weekend market that I think is the most interesting place to visit. It is not a typical local market because people come from many nearby villages to sell handmade crafts, fresh produce, and street food. I used to go every week with my mother when I was young. Whenever friends visit my hometown, I always take them there first because it shows the real character of the place.

Câu 15Do you think young people prefer to stay in their hometown or move to a big city?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về xu hướng của người trẻ ở quê hương.

RĐưa ra lý do tại sao người trẻ thường có lựa chọn như vậy.

ELiên hệ với quyết định hoặc hoàn cảnh cá nhân của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think most young people from my hometown prefer to move to a big city for better opportunities. City life offers more jobs, better universities, and more entertainment. I am a good example of this, since I left my hometown to study medicine in Hanoi. However, I notice that many of my friends hope to move back one day after they have built a career.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my experience, most young people want to leave their hometown at least for a few years. They want to experience city life and see if the world beyond their rural area suits them better. But after some years in the city, many start to miss home and think about returning. I believe that growing up in a close-knit community gives people strong roots that they always come back to.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Music

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • listen to musicnghe nhạc
  • favourite genrethể loại nhạc yêu thích
  • relax / unwindthư giãn, xả stress
  • lyricslời bài hát
  • melodygiai điệu
  • play an instrumentchơi một nhạc cụ
  • live music / live concertnhạc sống / buổi biểu diễn trực tiếp
  • background musicnhạc nền
  • music streamingnghe nhạc trực tuyến (streaming)
  • upbeat / upliftingsôi động / nâng cao tinh thần
  • concentrate / focustập trung
  • moodtâm trạng
  • sing alonghát theo
  • Vietnamese pop (V-pop)nhạc pop Việt Nam

Câu 1What kind of music do you like?

ANêu thể loại nhạc yêu thích của bạn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích thể loại đó (cảm xúc, giai điệu, lời bài hát...).

ECho ví dụ về ca sĩ hoặc bài hát cụ thể bạn hay nghe.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I mostly enjoy Vietnamese pop (V-pop) and soft melody|melodies. I like it because the lyrics are easy to understand and the songs often express real emotions. For example, I often listen to music by Son Tung M-TP when I want to relax after a busy shift.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My favourite genre is classical music, especially piano pieces. I find the melody very calming and it helps me concentrate when I study. Composers like Chopin always put me in a good mood before exams.

Câu 2When do you usually listen to music?

ANêu thời điểm bạn thường nghe nhạc.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn thời điểm đó.

EMô tả thói quen cụ thể của bạn lúc nghe nhạc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually listen to music in the evening after work. At that time I need to relax / unwind and music helps me forget the stress of the hospital. I normally use a music streaming app and listen for about thirty minutes before sleeping.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I often put on background music while I am studying or reviewing medical notes. It helps me concentrate / focus better, especially upbeat songs without heavy lyrics. I also listen to music during my morning commute on the bus.

Câu 3Do you prefer listening to music alone or with other people? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích nghe một mình hay cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do cho sở thích đó.

EĐưa ra tình huống cụ thể khi bạn nghe nhạc theo cách đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer listen to music|listening to music alone because I can fully focus on the melody and lyrics without distraction. When I am alone, I can also sing along freely without feeling shy. For example, I enjoy listening to slow ballads by myself late at night.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually enjoy listening with friends, especially at a live concert or a small gathering. Sharing music creates a great mood and we often sing along together, which is very fun. Last month my classmates and I went to a live music event near the academy.

Câu 4Do you often listen to music while studying or working?

ANêu bạn có nghe nhạc khi học/làm việc không.

RGiải thích ảnh hưởng của nhạc đến việc học hoặc làm việc của bạn.

ECho ví dụ về loại nhạc bạn chọn khi học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I sometimes play background music when I study, but only instrumental pieces. Lyrics can distract me from reading medical texts, so I choose soft piano or classical music. I find it helps me concentrate / focus for longer periods.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I rarely listen to music while studying because I lose concentration easily. I prefer complete silence when I need to concentrate on complex topics. However, I do play uplifting music for a few minutes before I start studying to put myself in a good mood.

Câu 5Have you ever learned to play a musical instrument?

ANêu bạn có học nhạc cụ nào không (hoặc chưa từng học).

RGiải thích lý do bạn học hoặc lý do bạn chưa học.

EChia sẻ kỷ niệm hoặc kế hoạch liên quan đến nhạc cụ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I learned to play an instrument - the guitar - when I was in secondary school. I wanted to play an instrument because it seemed like a great way to relax and impress my friends. I still remember the first song I learned was a simple Vietnamese folk melody.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have never play an instrument|played an instrument because my schedule has always been very busy with medical studies. However, I would love to learn the piano someday because I find the melody very beautiful. I think playing an instrument would be a wonderful way to unwind after work.

Câu 6Does music affect your mood? How?

AKhẳng định nhạc có ảnh hưởng đến tâm trạng của bạn hay không.

RGiải thích cơ chế ảnh hưởng đó như thế nào.

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể về tình huống nhạc làm thay đổi tâm trạng bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, music strongly affects my mood. When I feel stressed after a long day at the hospital, upbeat songs help me feel more energetic and positive. On the other hand, slow ballads sometimes make me feel a little sad, so I choose my music carefully depending on how I feel.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Definitely, music changes my mood very quickly. If I listen to uplifting music in the morning, I feel more motivated for the whole day. For example, I always play energetic songs before a difficult exam to calm my nerves and build my confidence.

Câu 7What is your favourite song or singer at the moment?

ANêu tên bài hát hoặc ca sĩ yêu thích của bạn hiện tại.

RGiải thích điều gì khiến bạn thích bài hát/ca sĩ đó.

EMô tả cảm xúc khi bạn nghe bài hát hoặc ca sĩ đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

At the moment my favourite singer is a Vietnamese pop (V-pop) artist named My Tam. I love her powerful voice and meaningful lyrics that touch real-life emotions. Every time I hear her songs, I feel a strong emotional connection and my mood immediately improves.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Right now I really enjoy a song called 'Chay Ve Khoc' by Den Vau. The melody is gentle and the lyrics talk about everyday struggles in a very honest way. I find it very comforting to listen to music like this when I feel tired after clinical practice.

Câu 8Did you listen to a lot of music when you were a child?

ANêu bạn có nghe nhiều nhạc hồi nhỏ không.

RGiải thích loại nhạc bạn nghe và hoàn cảnh khi đó.

EChia sẻ kỷ niệm cụ thể về âm nhạc thời thơ ấu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I listened to a lot of music as a child because my parents always played music at home. My mother loved traditional Vietnamese folk songs and she often sing along|sang along while cooking. Those melodies are still very familiar and comforting to me today.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really - as a child I was more interested in outdoor activities than music. However, my school had a music class where I first learned about different genres and simple melodies. That experience made me appreciate music more as I grew older.

Câu 9Have you ever been to a live concert or music performance?

ANêu bạn đã từng đi xem buổi biểu diễn nhạc sống chưa.

RMô tả cảm nhận của bạn về trải nghiệm đó.

EKể chi tiết về một buổi biểu diễn cụ thể bạn nhớ nhất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I went to a live concert last year with some friends from the academy. The atmosphere was amazing - everyone was singing along and the energy was incredible. That experience made me realize that live music feels completely different from just using a music streaming app.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have not been to a big live concert yet because tickets are quite expensive and I have limited free time. However, I once watched a small live music performance at a coffee shop near my university. The singer played guitar and the melody filled the whole room beautifully.

Câu 10Do you think music is important in everyday life? Why?

AĐưa ra quan điểm của bạn về tầm quan trọng của âm nhạc.

RGiải thích lý do âm nhạc có hoặc không có vai trò quan trọng.

ELiên hệ đến cuộc sống hàng ngày của bạn như một sinh viên y khoa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think music is very important in everyday life because it helps people relax / unwind and manage their emotions. As a medical student, I face a lot of pressure, and listening to music is one of the easiest ways to improve my mood quickly. Without music, life would feel much more stressful.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe music is important but not essential for everyone. Some people find silence more helpful for concentration, while others need background music to feel comfortable. Personally, music plays a moderate role in my life - I use it mainly to unwind rather than as a daily habit.

Câu 11Do you prefer Vietnamese music or foreign music?

ANêu rõ bạn thích nhạc Việt hay nhạc nước ngoài hơn.

RGiải thích lý do cho sở thích đó (ngôn ngữ, giai điệu, cảm xúc...).

ECho ví dụ về loại nhạc hoặc ca sĩ bạn hay nghe.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer Vietnamese pop (V-pop) because I can understand the lyrics directly without translation. The stories in Vietnamese songs feel closer to my own life and emotions. However, I also enjoy some English pop songs for their upbeat rhythm, especially during exercise.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually enjoy foreign music, particularly Korean pop and English pop, more than Vietnamese music. I love the polished melody and energetic upbeat style of these songs. Even though I do not always understand all the lyrics, the music still improves my mood greatly.

Câu 12Does your taste in music change depending on your mood?

ANêu sở thích nghe nhạc của bạn có thay đổi theo tâm trạng không.

RGiải thích bạn chọn loại nhạc nào trong tâm trạng khác nhau.

EMiêu tả cụ thể một tình huống bạn chọn nhạc theo tâm trạng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my choice of music definitely depends on my mood. When I feel energetic and happy, I choose upbeat pop songs and sometimes sing along. But when I feel sad or tired after work, I prefer soft ballads with gentle melodies that help me relax.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really - I tend to listen to music from my favourite genre regardless of how I feel. I find that uplifting music actually helps me when I am in a bad mood, so I do not switch to sad songs. I believe choosing positive music is a good habit for mental health, especially in the medical field.

Câu 13Do you think music can help reduce stress? Why?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về việc nhạc có giảm stress không.

RGiải thích cơ chế hoặc lý do bạn tin như vậy.

ELiên hệ đến kinh nghiệm thực tế của bạn khi dùng nhạc để giảm stress.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe music can help relax / unwind and reduce stress. When I listen to music with a gentle melody, my breathing slows down and I feel calmer almost immediately. After a stressful day at the hospital, ten minutes of soft music can completely change my mood.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think music can reduce stress for many people, but it depends on the type of music. Uplifting or soft instrumental music is helpful, but very loud or heavy music might increase anxiety for some. I personally use background music with no lyrics when I want to concentrate and feel less stressed.

Câu 14Do you ever listen to music using headphones or earphones?

ANêu bạn có dùng tai nghe để nghe nhạc không và dùng khi nào.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn dùng tai nghe hoặc không dùng.

EChia sẻ thói quen cụ thể khi bạn dùng tai nghe.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I use earphones almost every day, especially when I commute to the academy. Earphones allow me to listen to music privately without disturbing others, and I can use music streaming apps easily on my phone. I find that wearing earphones also helps me concentrate / focus in noisy environments.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to avoid using earphones for long periods because I worry about my hearing health. Instead, I prefer to play music through a small speaker at a comfortable volume when I am at home. I only use earphones occasionally in very noisy places where background music through a speaker is not practical.

Câu 15Would you like to learn more about music in the future?

ANêu bạn có muốn tìm hiểu thêm về âm nhạc trong tương lai không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn muốn hoặc không muốn học thêm về âm nhạc.

EĐề cập đến loại nhạc hoặc kỹ năng âm nhạc cụ thể bạn muốn khám phá.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would love to learn more about music in the future. I have always wanted to play an instrument, and I think learning the guitar would be a wonderful hobby after I finish my medical training. Music would give me a healthy way to relax / unwind and express my feelings outside of work.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, music is not my top priority for future learning since my focus is on medical studies and clinical skills. However, I am curious about music therapy and how certain melodies and rhythms are used to support patient recovery. Learning about that connection between music and health seems very useful for a doctor.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Exercise is important for health

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • regular exercisetập thể dục đều đặn
  • physical healthsức khỏe thể chất
  • keep fitgiữ dáng / duy trì thể lực
  • strengthen musclestăng cường cơ bắp
  • boost the immune systemtăng cường hệ miễn dịch
  • reduce stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • release endorphinsgiải phóng endorphin (chất giảm đau tự nhiên)
  • feel more relaxedcảm thấy thư giãn hơn
  • improve sleep qualitycải thiện chất lượng giấc ngủ
  • fall asleep more easilydễ ngủ hơn
  • social interactiongiao tiếp xã hội
  • mental well-beingsức khỏe tinh thần
  • work outtập luyện thể dục
  • stay activeduy trì hoạt động thể chất
  • energy levelsmức năng lượng cơ thể
  • healthy lifestylelối sống lành mạnh

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I would like to talk about why exercise is important for our health. I strongly believe that regular exercise is one of the best things we can do for our bodies and minds.
