There are several reasons why I think this, and I would also like to add my own idea. First of all, exercise helps us keep fit and maintain good physical health. When we work out, we strengthen muscles and boost the immune system, so we get sick less often. For example, people who jog or swim three times a week tend to have stronger bodies than those who stay active very little. Another reason is that exercise can reduce stress. Physical activity causes the brain to release endorphins, which help us feel more relaxed. For instance, after a long day of studying, a short walk can quickly improve our mood. In addition, exercise helps us improve sleep quality. When our body is tired from physical activity, we fall asleep more easily and sleep more deeply. On top of that, I think exercise also increases our social interaction. Joining a sports team or gym class gives us a chance to meet new friends, which is great for our mental well-being and keeps our energy levels high. To sum up, regular exercise supports a healthy lifestyle in many ways, and I encourage everyone to make it a daily habit.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Exercise (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • exercise regularlytập thể dục đều đặn
  • physical activityhoạt động thể chất
  • stay fitduy trì vóc dáng / sức khỏe tốt
  • sedentary lifestylelối sống ít vận động
  • work outtập luyện thể dục
  • gym membershipthẻ tập phòng gym
  • outdoor activitieshoạt động ngoài trời
  • team sportsthể thao đồng đội
  • mental healthsức khỏe tâm thần
  • physical educationgiáo dục thể chất
  • motivate someonetạo động lực cho ai
  • build healthy habitsxây dựng thói quen lành mạnh
  • reduce stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • sports facilitiescơ sở vật chất thể thao

Câu 1Why do some people not exercise regularly?

ANêu lý do chính: thiếu thời gian hoặc thiếu động lực.

RGiải thích vì sao yếu tố đó cản trở việc tập luyện.

ELấy ví dụ cụ thể như lịch làm việc bận rộn hoặc cảm thấy mệt mỏi sau ca trực.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the main reason is that many people are too busy to exercise regularly. They have long working hours and when they come home, they feel too tired to work out. For example, doctors and nurses often finish late shifts and just want to rest. This makes it very hard to build a routine.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, a lot of people do not exercise regularly because they lack motivation. They may want to stay fit, but without a clear goal or a friend to join them, they give up quickly. For instance, someone might start going to the gym but stop after two weeks because they do not see results fast enough.

Câu 2Is it better to exercise alone or with others?

AĐưa ra lựa chọn rõ ràng: tập một mình hoặc tập cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lợi ích chính của lựa chọn đó.

EVí dụ về tình huống cụ thể hoặc loại hình thể thao phù hợp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe it is better to exercise with others because it helps you stay motivated. When you have a friend or a group, you are less likely to skip a session. For example, joining a running club means you have a fixed schedule and people who expect you to show up, which makes you more consistent.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Personally, I think exercising alone has more advantages for some people. You can choose your own time and pace without depending on anyone else. For instance, going for a morning jog alone allows you to reduce stress quietly and focus on your own thoughts, which is very helpful for mental health.

Câu 3How can schools encourage students to exercise more?

AĐề xuất một biện pháp cụ thể mà trường học có thể thực hiện.

RGiải thích tại sao biện pháp đó hiệu quả.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về hoạt động hoặc chương trình cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think schools can encourage students to exercise more by improving sports facilities and making physical education classes more enjoyable. When students have access to good equipment and fun activities, they are more likely to participate. For example, schools could offer a variety of outdoor activities like football, badminton, or yoga so every student can find something they enjoy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Schools should organise more team sports competitions between classes or year groups. This creates excitement and gives students a reason to train regularly. For instance, an inter-class football tournament motivates even students who normally avoid exercise to stay active and build healthy habits from a young age.

Câu 4Do you think exercise is more important for young people or for older people? Why?

AChọn một nhóm hoặc nói cả hai đều quan trọng nhưng theo cách khác nhau.

RGiải thích lý do dựa trên sức khỏe hoặc lợi ích lâu dài.

EVí dụ về loại bài tập phù hợp hoặc lợi ích cụ thể của từng nhóm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think physical activity is especially important for older people because their bodies become weaker with age. Regular exercise can help them stay fit, improve balance, and reduce the risk of diseases like diabetes or heart problems. For example, older adults who walk every day tend to have better mental health and live longer than those who have a sedentary lifestyle.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, exercise is more important for young people because this is the time to build healthy habits that last a lifetime. If young people work out regularly, they develop stronger bones and better cardiovascular health. For instance, medical students who do physical activity between study sessions also perform better academically because exercise improves concentration.

Câu 5Should the government spend more money on public sports facilities? Why?

AĐồng ý hoặc không đồng ý và nêu lập trường rõ ràng.

RGiải thích lợi ích đối với cộng đồng hoặc sức khỏe cộng đồng.

EVí dụ về loại cơ sở hoặc khu vực cần đầu tư.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe the government should invest more in sports facilities. When parks, swimming pools, and gyms are free or affordable, more people can exercise regularly regardless of their income. For example, in countries where public parks have outdoor fitness equipment, the rate of physical activity among residents is much higher.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that more funding for sports facilities would benefit society, but the government should also focus on awareness campaigns. Having good facilities is useless if people do not know how to use them or do not feel motivated. For instance, a new community sports centre combined with free fitness classes would encourage more families to stay active together.

Câu 6How does exercise affect a person's mental health?

ANêu tác động tích cực của tập thể dục lên sức khỏe tâm thần.

RGiải thích cơ chế hoặc lý do khoa học đơn giản.

EVí dụ từ cuộc sống thực hoặc kinh nghiệm của bản thân/người khác.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Exercise has a very positive effect on mental health. When we work out, our body releases chemicals that help us feel happier and less anxious. For example, many medical students say that going for a run after a stressful exam helps them reduce stress and feel more relaxed and focused.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think regular exercise can also improve sleep and reduce feelings of depression. When people stay physically active, they feel more confident and energetic throughout the day. For instance, nurses who do yoga or light exercise before their shifts report feeling less emotionally exhausted and better able to cope with difficult situations at work.

Câu 7Why do you think many people prefer watching sports to playing sports?

AGiải thích lý do tại sao xem thể thao hấp dẫn hơn đối với nhiều người.

RNêu sự khác biệt về nỗ lực hoặc khả năng tiếp cận.

EVí dụ về một môn thể thao phổ biến để xem nhưng khó để chơi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people prefer watching sports because it requires no effort but still gives excitement. They can enjoy the thrill of competition without needing sports facilities or physical fitness. For example, millions of people watch football on television every weekend, but very few of them actually play in a team sport themselves.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Watching sports is also more social and convenient than playing sports. You can enjoy it at home with family or friends without worrying about the weather or your skill level. For instance, watching the Olympics brings people together as a community, which is something very different from the personal effort needed to train regularly and compete.

Câu 8Do you think workplaces should provide exercise facilities for employees? Why?

AĐồng ý hoặc không đồng ý và đưa ra quan điểm rõ ràng.

RGiải thích lợi ích đối với nhân viên hoặc người sử dụng lao động.

EVí dụ về loại cơ sở hoặc chương trình sức khỏe tại nơi làm việc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think workplaces should definitely provide exercise facilities. When employees can work out during lunch breaks or after work, they are healthier and reduce stress more easily. For example, hospitals that have a small gym or relaxation area for staff tend to have lower rates of burnout among doctors and nurses.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that workplace sports facilities are a good idea, but not all companies can afford them. A more realistic option might be to give employees flexible hours so they can exercise regularly before or after work. For instance, allowing staff to start one hour later a few days a week would give them time for morning physical activity without expensive equipment.

Câu 9How has technology changed the way people exercise?

ANêu một thay đổi tích cực hoặc tiêu cực do công nghệ mang lại.

RGiải thích cơ chế tác động của công nghệ đến thói quen tập luyện.

EVí dụ cụ thể về ứng dụng, thiết bị hoặc video tập luyện trực tuyến.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Technology has made it much easier for people to exercise regularly without going to a gym. Online workout videos and fitness apps allow people to work out at home at any time. For example, during the pandemic many people used YouTube or apps to follow home exercise routines, which helped them stay fit even when sports facilities were closed.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On the other hand, technology can also encourage a more sedentary lifestyle. People spend more time on phones and computers, which reduces the time they spend on physical activity. For instance, many young people today would rather play video games than go outside for outdoor activities, which is a growing public health concern.

Câu 10Should children have more physical education classes at school? Why?

AĐồng ý và nêu lý do tại sao giáo dục thể chất quan trọng với trẻ em.

RGiải thích lợi ích về sức khỏe hoặc sự phát triển toàn diện.

EVí dụ về tần suất hoặc loại hoạt động cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe children should have more physical education classes because regular exercise is essential for their development. It helps them stay fit, develop social skills, and improve concentration in other subjects. For example, schools that have daily PE lessons report that students are less likely to be overweight and have better behaviour in class.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, more physical education time would definitely benefit children. However, the quality of the lessons is also important. If classes are fun and include a variety of team sports and outdoor activities, children are more likely to build healthy habits that they keep for the rest of their lives.

Câu 11Do you think it is harder to exercise regularly in a city or in the countryside? Why?

AChọn môi trường khó hơn và giải thích lý do.

RSo sánh điều kiện cơ sở vật chất hoặc lối sống ở hai nơi.

EVí dụ về loại hình tập luyện phổ biến ở mỗi nơi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think it can be harder to exercise regularly in a city, even though cities have more sports facilities. The problem is that city life is very busy and people often have long commutes, leaving little time for physical activity. For example, someone who works in Hanoi may spend two hours a day on the road, making it very difficult to find time to work out.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, exercising in the countryside can be harder because there are fewer sports facilities like gyms or swimming pools. However, the natural environment offers great opportunities for outdoor activities like hiking or cycling. For instance, farmers and rural residents are often naturally more physically active because of their daily work, even without formal exercise.

Câu 12How can people stay motivated to exercise when they feel tired or busy?

AĐề xuất một chiến lược cụ thể để duy trì động lực tập luyện.

RGiải thích tại sao chiến lược đó có hiệu quả.

EVí dụ thực tế về cách áp dụng chiến lược đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the best way to stay motivated is to set small, achievable goals. Instead of planning to work out for an hour every day, people can start with just 15 minutes. For example, a busy medical student could commit to a short walk after dinner, which is easy to do even on a tiring day, and this small habit can grow into regular exercise over time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Another effective strategy is to find an exercise partner or join a group. When someone is waiting for you, you feel more responsible and are less likely to give up. For instance, agreeing to work out with a colleague three times a week creates a routine and makes physical activity feel like a social event rather than a chore.

Câu 13Should doctors and healthcare workers be required to exercise regularly? Why?

AĐồng ý hoặc không đồng ý và nêu quan điểm về trách nhiệm nghề nghiệp.

RGiải thích liên hệ giữa sức khỏe của bác sĩ và chất lượng chăm sóc bệnh nhân.

EVí dụ về lợi ích cụ thể khi bác sĩ có thói quen tập luyện tốt.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think healthcare workers should be strongly encouraged to exercise regularly, though I am not sure it should be required by law. A doctor who stays fit and maintains good health is better able to handle long working hours and high stress. For example, a surgeon who does regular physical activity has better stamina, focus, and hand coordination during complex operations.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, doctors and nurses who exercise regularly also set a better example for their patients. When a healthcare professional advises a patient to stay active, it is more convincing if they practise what they preach. For instance, a physician who jogs every morning can speak about the benefits of physical activity with personal experience and genuine motivation.

Câu 14Do you think competitive sports or recreational exercise is better for health? Why?

AChọn một loại hình và giải thích lý do dựa trên lợi ích sức khỏe.

RSo sánh tác động của hai loại hình lên thể chất và tâm thần.

EVí dụ cụ thể về môn thể thao cạnh tranh hoặc hình thức tập luyện giải trí.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe recreational exercise is better for health in the long run. Competitive sports can lead to injuries or burnout if people push themselves too hard. For example, someone who swims or does yoga for fun is more likely to exercise regularly over many years compared to an athlete who trains intensely and eventually suffers from physical exhaustion or injuries.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think competitive team sports can actually be very good for both physical and mental health. The excitement of competition keeps people motivated and pushes them to train harder than they would alone. For instance, playing basketball in a local league is not only great physical activity but also builds teamwork, discipline, and social connections.

Câu 15How can communities encourage inactive people to start exercising?

AĐề xuất một hoặc hai biện pháp cộng đồng cụ thể.

RGiải thích tại sao biện pháp đó tiếp cận được người không thích tập thể dục.

EVí dụ về sự kiện, chương trình hoặc sáng kiến cộng đồng cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think communities can encourage inactive people by organising free, fun events that do not feel like exercise. Many people avoid working out because they think it is boring or too difficult. For example, a community could organise weekend dance classes, cycling tours, or group walks in the park, which make physical activity enjoyable and social without pressure.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Communities should also focus on removing barriers that stop people from exercising regularly, such as cost and lack of information. Offering free gym membership trials or setting up sports facilities in public areas can make a big difference. For instance, when a neighbourhood installs outdoor fitness equipment in a park, people who pass by every day are naturally motivated to try it and may build healthy habits gradually.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Shopping

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • shopping onlinemua sắm trực tuyến
  • shopping malltrung tâm thương mại
  • daily necessitiesđồ dùng thiết yếu hàng ngày
  • compare pricesso sánh giá cả
  • on sale / on discountđang giảm giá
  • try onthử (quần áo)
  • branded itemshàng có thương hiệu

  • impulse buymua hàng không có kế hoạch
  • window shoppingđi xem hàng mà không mua
  • second-hand / second-hand shopđồ cũ / cửa hàng đồ cũ

Câu 1How often do you go shopping?

ANêu tần suất bạn đi mua sắm (hàng tuần, hàng tháng, hiếm khi...).

RGiải thích lý do (bận rộn, thích mua online, ít có nhu cầu...).

EKể một lần đi mua sắm gần đây hoặc loại hàng thường mua.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I go shopping about once a week, usually on weekends. Because I work long hours at the hospital during the week, I do not have much free time on weekdays. Last Saturday, for example, I went to the shopping mall near my house to buy some daily necessities like shampoo and laundry detergent.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I only go shopping about twice a month because I am quite busy with my studies. I prefer to plan my purchases carefully so that I do not waste time or money. The last time I went was two weeks ago, and I mainly bought some stationery and a new medical textbook.

Câu 2Do you prefer shopping online or in shops? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích hình thức nào (online hay tại cửa hàng).

RGiải thích lý do tại sao (tiện lợi, tin tưởng hơn, thích thử hàng...).

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về loại hàng bạn mua theo hình thức đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer shopping online because it saves me a lot of time. I can compare prices easily and place an order any time, even after midnight when I finish studying. For example, I often buy books and phone accessories online because they are cheaper and delivered to my door.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually prefer shopping in physical stores, especially when I buy clothes. I like to try on items before I pay so that I know they fit well. Last month I went to a shop downtown and tried on three jackets before choosing the one I liked most.

Câu 3What do you usually buy?

ALiệt kê những loại hàng bạn hay mua nhất.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn hay mua những thứ đó (nhu cầu thiết yếu, sở thích...).

ENêu một ví dụ cụ thể về món đồ gần đây bạn mua.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually buy daily necessities such as food, toiletries, and cleaning products. These are things I need every week, so I cannot avoid buying them. Last weekend, for instance, I spent most of my shopping budget on groceries and some vitamins for my health.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Most of the time I buy medical books and stationery because I am a postgraduate student and I always need study materials. I also buy comfortable clothes suitable for working in a hospital. Recently I bought a new pair of shoes that are both professional and easy to wear during long shifts.

Câu 4Do you enjoy shopping? Why or why not?

ACho biết bạn có thích đi mua sắm không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích hoặc không thích.

EKể một trải nghiệm mua sắm tích cực hoặc tiêu cực.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I enjoy shopping quite a lot, especially when items are on sale. I find it relaxing to walk around a shopping mall after a stressful week at the hospital. One time I found a great jacket at half price, and that made me very happy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I do not enjoy shopping very much because it takes a long time. I usually know exactly what I need, so I just go in, buy it, and leave. However, I do enjoy window shopping with friends on weekends, even if I do not buy anything.

Câu 5Where do you usually go shopping?

ANêu địa điểm bạn thường mua sắm (siêu thị, chợ, trung tâm thương mại, mạng...).

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn nơi đó (gần nhà, giá tốt, nhiều lựa chọn...).

EKể tên một cửa hàng hoặc trang web cụ thể bạn hay dùng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually go to the supermarket near my accommodation because it is very convenient. The prices are reasonable and the quality is reliable. I also sometimes order from online platforms when I need things delivered quickly without leaving the house.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to shop at a large shopping mall in the city centre because it has everything in one place. I can buy groceries, clothes, and household items without going to multiple stores. The mall also has a food court, so I can have lunch there after shopping.

Câu 6Do you make a shopping list before you go shopping?

ACho biết bạn có lập danh sách mua sắm không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn làm vậy hoặc không làm vậy.

ECho ví dụ điều gì xảy ra khi bạn có hoặc không có danh sách.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always make a shopping list before I leave home. This helps me stay within my budget and avoid wasting money on things I do not need. Once I forgot my list and ended up making an impulse buy - I bought a bag of snacks I did not plan to get.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not usually write a list because I tend to remember what I need quite well. However, I sometimes regret this when I get home and realise I forgot something important. I think making a list is a good habit and I should do it more often.

Câu 7How much time do you usually spend shopping?

ANêu khoảng thời gian bạn thường dành cho mỗi lần mua sắm.

RGiải thích tại sao mất nhiều hoặc ít thời gian như vậy.

EKể về một lần mua sắm mất nhiều hoặc ít thời gian hơn bình thường.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually spend about one hour shopping because I try to be efficient. I know which aisles to go to and I rarely browse for long. Last Sunday, though, I spent nearly two hours because the shopping mall was very crowded and the queues at the checkout were long.

Trả lời mẫu 2

When I go shopping online, I sometimes spend two or three hours browsing different websites to compare prices. I want to make sure I get the best deal before I pay. But when I shop in a physical store, I try to finish in thirty minutes so I can get back to studying.

Câu 8Do you like buying branded items or cheaper alternatives?

ANêu bạn thích hàng có thương hiệu hay hàng rẻ hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn như vậy (chất lượng, giá cả, thói quen...).

ECho ví dụ một loại hàng cụ thể bạn mua theo tiêu chí đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer to buy branded items when it comes to shoes and medical equipment because quality is very important for my work. Poor-quality shoes can cause pain after standing all day in the hospital. However, for everyday groceries I always choose cheaper alternatives to save money.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I usually choose cheaper alternatives because I am a student and my budget is limited. I believe that many non-branded products have similar quality to famous brands. For example, I buy store-brand vitamins and they work just as well as the expensive ones.

Câu 9Have you ever bought something and then regretted it?

ANêu rõ có hay không và đó là món hàng gì.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn hối tiếc khi mua món đó.

ENêu bạn đã làm gì sau đó (trả lại, tặng người khác, học được bài học...).

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I once made an impulse buy and bought an expensive smartwatch I did not really need. I regretted it because the money could have been used for textbooks or a medical course. After that experience, I always wait a day before buying anything that costs more than a million dong.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, I remember buying a jacket on sale that looked great in the shop but did not fit well when I got home. I had not try on the jacket properly because the shop was busy. Unfortunately the store did not accept returns for sale items, so it was a wasted purchase.

Câu 10Do you think shopping is a good way to relax?

ACho biết bạn có đồng ý mua sắm là cách thư giãn tốt không.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao mua sắm có hoặc không có tác dụng thư giãn.

EKể về một lần bạn đi mua sắm để giải tỏa căng thẳng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think window shopping can be a pleasant way to relax, especially when I walk around a shopping mall with a friend. It does not feel like a chore when there is no pressure to buy anything. Last month after a difficult exam, I went to a mall just to walk around and it helped me feel much better.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Personally, I do not find shopping very relaxing because large crowds and noise make me feel more stressed. I prefer to relax by reading or exercising. However, I understand that many people enjoy go shopping as a social activity and a way to treat themselves after a hard week.

Câu 11Do you ever buy second-hand items?

ANêu bạn có mua đồ cũ không và loại đồ gì.

RGiải thích lý do bạn mua hoặc không mua đồ cũ.

ECho ví dụ về một món đồ cũ bạn từng mua hoặc lý do bạn tránh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I sometimes buy second-hand textbooks because they are much cheaper than new ones. Medical books are very expensive, so buying used copies saves me a lot of money. I bought a second-hand anatomy atlas last semester and it was in perfect condition.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I rarely buy second-hand items because I worry about hygiene and quality. I feel more comfortable knowing that clothing or equipment is brand new. However, I think buying second-hand furniture or electronics is a smart idea if you are on a tight budget.

Câu 12Do you think people spend too much money on shopping these days?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về vấn đề chi tiêu quá mức hiện nay.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn nghĩ vậy (quảng cáo, cám dỗ, thói quen xã hội...).

ECho ví dụ về người quen hoặc xu hướng xã hội bạn quan sát thấy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I believe many people spend too much money shopping, especially because of online advertising. It is very easy to make an impulse buy when attractive deals appear on your phone screen. I have friends who use loyalty card points from multiple stores because they shop so frequently.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think it depends on the person. Some people are very disciplined and only buy what they need, while others tend to overspend on branded items or fashion. In my opinion, setting a clear budget each month is the best way to avoid spending too much.

Câu 13What do you think about shopping malls? Do you like going to them?

ANêu ý kiến của bạn về trung tâm thương mại và bạn có thích đến không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích hoặc không thích đến đó.

EKể về một lần bạn đến trung tâm thương mại và trải nghiệm của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I like going to shopping malls because they offer everything I need under one roof. I can buy clothes, groceries, and even have a meal without travelling to different places. Last week I spent a whole afternoon there and it was very convenient and enjoyable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I find shopping malls a bit overwhelming because they are usually very crowded and noisy. I prefer quieter places like small markets or specialist shops. However, I do appreciate that malls have a wide variety of products, which makes compare prices much easier.

Câu 14Has shopping changed since you were a child? How?

ANêu sự thay đổi chính trong thói quen mua sắm từ thời thơ ấu đến nay.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân của sự thay đổi đó (công nghệ, xã hội...).

EKể một ví dụ cụ thể về sự khác biệt giữa mua sắm ngày xưa và bây giờ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Shopping has changed a lot since I was a child. When I was young, my family always went to the local market to buy fresh food and daily necessities. Now, shopping online is very common and we can get almost anything delivered to our door the next day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, it has changed significantly. As a child, I remember going with my mother to small neighbourhood shops where the sellers knew us by name. Today, I mostly use apps to go shopping, which is faster but less personal. I sometimes miss the friendly atmosphere of those old markets.

Câu 15Do you prefer to go shopping alone or with others?

ANêu bạn thích đi mua sắm một mình hay cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích hình thức đó hơn.

EKể một trải nghiệm mua sắm cùng bạn bè hoặc một mình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer to go shopping alone because I can move quickly and focus on what I need. When I shop with others, we often spend too much time in stores I am not interested in. Last time I went alone I finished all my shopping in forty minutes and felt very satisfied.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy shopping with friends because it makes the experience more fun and social. My friends often give helpful opinions when I am choosing clothes or trying to compare prices on electronics. Last month we went to a shopping mall together and had a great time trying on different outfits and having coffee afterwards.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Your daily routine

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • daily routinethói quen hằng ngày
  • wake upthức dậy
  • get up earlydậy sớm
  • have breakfastăn sáng
  • work shiftca làm việc
  • unwindthư giãn, giải tỏa căng thẳng
  • keep fitgiữ sức khỏe, tập thể dục đều đặn
  • go to bedđi ngủ
  • skip a mealbỏ bữa ăn
  • stick to a scheduletuân thủ lịch trình
  • free timethời gian rảnh
  • take a breaknghỉ ngơi một chút
  • morning exercisetập thể dục buổi sáng
  • wind downthư giãn trước khi ngủ

Câu 1What do you usually do in the morning?

ANêu rõ bạn thường làm gì đầu tiên vào buổi sáng.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn có thói quen đó.

EĐưa ra một hoạt động cụ thể bạn làm mỗi sáng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually wake up at around five-thirty and do some morning exercise before work. I think starting the day with physical activity helps me feel more energetic and focused. For example, I often go for a jog around the hospital campus for about twenty minutes. After that, I quickly have breakfast and get ready for my shift.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My mornings are quite rushed because my work shift starts early. I get up early at five o'clock, wash my face, and immediately prepare for the hospital. I normally grab a quick meal like a bowl of pho on the way to work. I do not have much time to relax, but I try to stick to a schedule so I am never late.

Câu 2What do you do after work or classes?

ANêu hoạt động bạn thường làm sau khi tan ca hoặc hết lớp.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn hoạt động đó.

EMô tả một ví dụ cụ thể về buổi tối của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

After my shift, I usually need some time to unwind because working in the hospital can be very tiring. I often go home, take a shower, and then cook a simple meal at home. I find that home-cooked meals help me relax and save money. Sometimes I also review my medical notes or read a short book before I go to bed.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I like to take a break and spend some free time with my family after classes. We usually have dinner together and talk about the day, which I think is very important for mental health. After dinner, I sometimes go for a short walk in the neighbourhood to keep fit. I try to wind down by ten o'clock so I can sleep well.

Câu 3Do you think your daily routine is busy?

ATrả lời thẳng là lịch của bạn có bận hay không.

RGiải thích lý do khiến lịch bận hoặc không bận.

EĐưa ra một ngày điển hình để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think my daily routine is quite busy, especially on weekdays. I have morning rounds, clinical training, and sometimes evening lectures all in one day. For example, last Tuesday I was at the hospital from six in the morning until eight at night without a proper take a break. I sometimes feel exhausted, but I know it is part of training to become a good doctor.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, my routine is busy but manageable because I have learned to stick to a schedule. I plan my week in advance and try not to skip a meal or lose sleep. For instance, I always block out one hour in the evening for personal study and another thirty minutes to keep fit. Having a clear plan makes even a full day feel less overwhelming.

Câu 4What time do you usually wake up?

ACho biết giờ bạn thường thức dậy.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn dậy vào giờ đó.

ENêu điều đầu tiên bạn làm sau khi thức dậy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually wake up at five-fifteen every morning because my hospital duty begins at six-thirty. Getting up early gives me enough time to prepare without feeling rushed. The first thing I do is drink a glass of water and then do a short morning exercise routine. I believe starting early helps me stick to a schedule for the rest of the day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On most days I wake up at six o'clock, which I think is not too early and not too late. I need about forty-five minutes to have breakfast and get dressed before leaving home. However, on the days when I have early ward rounds, I set my alarm for five o'clock. I have learned that getting up early consistently makes my whole daily routine feel more organised.

Câu 5Do you have a regular exercise routine?

ANêu rõ bạn có tập thể dục thường xuyên hay không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn tập hoặc không tập.

EMô tả loại bài tập và tần suất bạn thực hiện.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I try to keep fit by doing morning exercise three or four times a week. I usually go for a jog for about twenty minutes before breakfast. I started this habit because sitting or standing for long hours during hospital duty is tiring for my body. Regular exercise helps me unwind and also improves my concentration during lectures.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not have a very regular exercise routine because my work shift changes from week to week. I try to keep fit by cycling on weekends and taking the stairs instead of the elevator at the hospital. Sometimes I do simple stretching exercises before go to bed to relax my muscles. I think even small activities count as part of a healthy daily routine.

Câu 6What do you usually eat for breakfast?

ANêu món ăn sáng bạn thường ăn.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn món đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về một buổi sáng cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually have breakfast with rice or bread and eggs because they are quick to prepare and give me enough energy for a long shift. I prefer home-cooked meals in the morning because they are cheaper and healthier than buying food outside. For example, yesterday I made a simple omelette with vegetables in about ten minutes. I never skip a meal in the morning because it affects my focus at work.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I often grab a quick meal from a street stall near my dormitory because I do not have much time to cook. I usually buy a bowl of pho or a banh mi sandwich. I know it is not the healthiest option, but it is fast and filling. I hope to build a better habit of preparing home-cooked meals once my schedule becomes less busy.

Câu 7How do you relax after a long day?

ANêu cách bạn thư giãn sau một ngày dài.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó giúp bạn thư giãn.

EMô tả một buổi tối cụ thể khi bạn thư giãn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

After a tiring day at the hospital, I like to unwind by watching a short video or listening to music. I find that spending about thirty minutes on something enjoyable helps me wind down and forget about work stress. For example, last night I watched a comedy clip with my roommate and laughed a lot. Simple activities like this really help me recharge for the next day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to take a break by going outside for a short walk in the evening. Fresh air and a change of environment help me unwind after being in the hospital all day. Sometimes I call my parents back home and talk for about fifteen minutes, which always makes me feel better. After that, I usually wind down with a cup of herbal tea before go to bed.

Câu 8What time do you usually go to bed?

ACho biết giờ bạn thường đi ngủ.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn ngủ vào giờ đó.

ENêu điều bạn thường làm ngay trước khi ngủ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually go to bed at around ten-thirty on weeknights because I need to wake up early the next morning. I think getting enough sleep is very important for a medical student, so I try to sleep at least seven hours. Before sleeping, I usually wind down by reading a few pages of a book or reviewing my notes. Going to bed at a fixed time helps me stick to a schedule and feel rested.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I go to bed quite late, usually around midnight, because I have a lot of studying to do after dinner. I know this is not the best habit, but the workload during postgraduate training is very heavy. I try to take a break from the screen for ten minutes before sleeping to help my eyes rest. I am trying to stick to a schedule and move my bedtime to eleven o'clock.

Câu 9Do you prefer mornings or evenings? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích buổi sáng hay buổi tối hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích khoảng thời gian đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về điều bạn thường làm vào thời điểm đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer mornings because I feel most alert and productive during that time. Getting up early gives me quiet time to study or do morning exercise before the day gets busy. For example, I often review medical terms or practice English for about thirty minutes after breakfast. I find that what I study in the morning stays in my memory longer than what I study at night.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually prefer evenings because that is when I have more free time to do things I enjoy. After my work shift ends, I can unwind without worrying about any immediate tasks. For example, I like to cook a proper home-cooked meal in the evening and eat slowly while watching the news. The evening feels more relaxing and personal compared to the rushed daily routine of the morning.

Câu 10Do you have enough free time in your daily routine?

ANêu ý kiến về lượng thời gian rảnh bạn có.

RGiải thích lý do bạn có nhiều hoặc ít thời gian rảnh.

EMô tả bạn thường làm gì trong thời gian rảnh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Honestly, I do not have much free time during the week because postgraduate training keeps me very busy. My work shift at the hospital and study sessions take up most of the day. However, I usually have a couple of hours on Sunday afternoon that I use to unwind and do personal activities. I try to make the most of that limited free time by meeting friends or exercising.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think I have just enough free time if I manage my schedule carefully. I usually finish my duties by seven in the evening and that gives me about two hours before go to bed. I use that time to keep fit, cook, or simply take a break and browse the internet. I am grateful for even a short period of rest in an otherwise packed daily routine.

Câu 11Do you think having a routine is important? Why?

ANêu ý kiến về tầm quan trọng của việc có thói quen hằng ngày.

RGiải thích lý do thói quen có lợi hoặc không cần thiết.

EĐưa ra ví dụ từ cuộc sống của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe that having a daily routine is very important, especially for medical students. When I stick to a schedule, I waste less time deciding what to do next and I feel more in control of my day. For example, since I set fixed times for studying and sleeping, my grades have improved and I rarely skip a meal. A good routine also helps me keep fit and manage stress better.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think a routine is helpful, but it should not be too rigid. Having a general daily routine helps me remember important tasks like eating well and getting enough sleep. However, in the hospital, unexpected situations come up all the time, so I need to be flexible. I try to stick to a schedule for basic habits like morning exercise and go to bed at a fixed time, while staying adaptable for everything else.

Câu 12Has your daily routine changed since you started postgraduate study?

ANêu lịch của bạn có thay đổi hay không kể từ khi học sau đại học.

RGiải thích điều gì đã thay đổi và tại sao.

ESo sánh một ngày điển hình trước và sau khi bắt đầu học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my daily routine has changed a lot since I started postgraduate training. Before, I had more free time in the evenings and could unwind easily. Now, I have longer work shift hours and more reading to do at night, so I often go to bed much later than before. I have had to stick to a schedule more strictly to manage everything without skip a meal or losing too much sleep.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My routine has definitely become busier, but I think I have adapted well. I now wake up thirty minutes earlier than I used to so I have time for morning exercise before the hospital rounds begin. I also cook less and tend to grab a quick meal more often, which I want to change. Overall, the biggest shift is that I now treat every hour of free time as precious and try not to waste it.

Câu 13Do you ever skip meals because of your schedule?

ACho biết bạn có thường bỏ bữa vì lịch bận không.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân khiến bạn bỏ bữa hoặc không.

EKể một tình huống cụ thể liên quan đến bữa ăn của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Unfortunately, I do skip a meal sometimes, especially lunch, when the ward is very busy. There are days when I am in the middle of a procedure and simply cannot stop to eat. For example, last week I missed lunch two days in a row because of emergency cases. I try to have breakfast every day without fail so that I have enough energy even if I miss lunch.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try very hard not to skip a meal because I know it is bad for my concentration and health. I always have breakfast before leaving home, and I pack a small snack to eat during short take a break moments. Even on very busy days, I make sure to eat something small between patient consultations. I believe a regular eating schedule is just as important as any other part of a good daily routine.

Câu 14Do you spend time on hobbies in your daily routine?

ANêu sở thích bạn có trong thói quen hằng ngày.

RGiải thích tại sao sở thích đó quan trọng với bạn.

EMô tả khi nào và bao lâu bạn dành cho sở thích đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always try to spend at least a little time on my hobby even on busy days. I enjoy drawing and I usually sketch for about fifteen to twenty minutes before go to bed to help me wind down. It gives me a sense of free time that belongs only to me, separate from work and study. I think having a hobby is essential for mental health and helps me unwind after long hospital days.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not have much time for hobbies during the week, but I protect my weekend mornings for things I enjoy. I like playing the guitar, so every Saturday morning I play for about half an hour instead of doing morning exercise. It helps me unwind and feel more balanced as a person. I think finding even a small window in your daily routine for a hobby makes you more productive overall.

Câu 15How do you manage your time between work and personal life?

ANêu cách bạn cân bằng giữa công việc và cuộc sống cá nhân.

RGiải thích chiến lược hoặc nguyên tắc bạn áp dụng.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về cách bạn sắp xếp thời gian.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I manage my time by planning my week every Sunday evening and trying to stick to a schedule as much as possible. I set clear boundaries, for example, I stop studying by ten-thirty so I have time to wind down before go to bed. I also protect at least one evening per week as personal time to call family or enjoy some free time. This balance helps me avoid burnout and keep fit both physically and mentally.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, balancing work shift demands and personal life is still a challenge for me. My main strategy is to use small pockets of time wisely, like listening to podcasts during my commute or doing morning exercise instead of scrolling on my phone. I also try not to skip a meal, because eating properly gives me enough energy to be productive without overworking. I am still learning, but I think a realistic daily routine is better than a perfect one I cannot follow.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Travelling helps people learn about life

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • travellingdu lịch, đi đây đó
  • learn about lifehọc hỏi về cuộc sống
  • reduce stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • recharge your energynạp lại năng lượng
  • local customsphong tục địa phương
  • traditional cuisineẩm thực truyền thống
  • broaden your horizonsmở rộng tầm nhìn
  • meet new peoplegặp gỡ người mới
  • make lifelong friendskết bạn lâu dài
  • exchange ideastrao đổi ý kiến
  • become more independenttrở nên tự lập hơn
  • solve problemsgiải quyết vấn đề
  • step out of your comfort zonebước ra khỏi vùng an toàn
  • valuable experiencetrải nghiệm quý giá
  • open-mindedcởi mở, phóng khoáng

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

Today I would like to talk about the topic 'Travelling helps people learn about life.' I strongly agree with this idea because travelling offers many valuable experiences that help us grow as people. First of all, travelling helps us reduce stress and relax. When we leave our daily routine behind, we can recharge your energy and feel much better. For example, after a short trip to Da Lat, I felt completely refreshed and ready to study again. Another reason is that travel lets us learn about life through different cultures. We can discover local customs and try traditional cuisine, which broaden your horizons and makes us more open-minded. For instance, when I visited Hoi An, I learned about traditional crafts that I had never seen before. In addition, travelling gives us the chance to meet new people from different backgrounds. We can exchange ideas and sometimes even make lifelong friends. I once met a student from France on a bus tour, and we still keep in touch today. On top of that, I think travelling helps us become more independent. When we face unexpected situations on the road, we learn to solve problems on our own and step out of your comfort zone. This builds real confidence in everyday life. To sum up, travelling is one of the best ways to learn about life, and I think everyone should travel as often as possible.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Travelling (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • travel broadens the mindđi du lịch mở rộng tầm nhìn
  • explore new placeskhám phá những nơi mới
  • local culturevăn hóa địa phương
  • affordable flightschuyến bay giá rẻ
  • budget traveldu lịch tiết kiệm chi phí
  • gain new experiencestích lũy trải nghiệm mới
  • expand your horizonsmở rộng tầm hiểu biết
  • tourist attractionsđiểm tham quan du lịch
  • solo traveldu lịch một mình
  • cultural exchangegiao lưu văn hóa
  • broaden perspectivesmở rộng quan điểm, cách nhìn
  • public transportphương tiện giao thông công cộng
  • eco-tourismdu lịch sinh thái
  • travel on a tight budgetdu lịch với ngân sách hạn hẹp

Câu 1Why do people like travelling to new places?

ANêu lý do chính: du lịch giúp khám phá và thư giãn.

RGiải thích: cuộc sống hàng ngày căng thẳng, cần thay đổi môi trường và mở mang kiến thức.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ hoặc sinh viên y khoa cần nghỉ ngơi sau thời gian dài học tập, làm việc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think people like travelling because it gives them a chance to escape from daily stress and explore new places. When we visit somewhere new, we can learn about different local cultures, try new food, and meet people with different backgrounds. For example, as a medical student with a busy schedule, I always feel refreshed after a short trip somewhere I have never been before.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people enjoy travelling mainly because they want to gain new experiences that they cannot have at home. Travelling allows us to broaden perspectives and understand the world better. For instance, visiting a rural area can show a doctor how people in different regions live and what health challenges they face, which is very useful knowledge.

Câu 2Is travelling more popular now than in the past?

AKhẳng định: du lịch phổ biến hơn nhiều so với trước đây.

RGiải thích: [[affordable flights]], công nghệ đặt vé online, thu nhập tăng.

EVí dụ: so sánh số lượng du khách quốc tế ngày nay với 20-30 năm trước.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe travelling is much more popular now than in the past. One main reason is that affordable flights and online booking platforms have made it much easier and cheaper for ordinary people to travel. For example, in Vietnam, many more families take domestic holidays now compared to twenty or thirty years ago, when most people could not afford to travel far.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think travelling has definitely become more popular in recent decades. Thanks to the rise of budget travel options and the internet, people can plan trips quickly and at low cost. However, I also think social media plays a big role because seeing friends share beautiful travel photos motivates others to explore new places as well.

Câu 3Should young people travel more?

AĐồng ý: người trẻ nên đi du lịch nhiều hơn.

RGiải thích: giúp phát triển kỹ năng sống, tự lập và [[broaden perspectives]].

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa có thể tham gia các chương trình y tế tình nguyện ở nước ngoài.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I definitely think young people should travel more because it helps them expand your horizons and become more independent. When you travel, especially alone, you have to solve problems by yourself, which builds confidence and life skills. As a medical student, I believe even a short trip to a different province or country can teach you things that no textbook can.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that young people should travel more, but I also think they need to do it responsibly. Travelling helps young people develop cultural exchange skills and understand diversity, which is very important in today's world. For example, young doctors who have travelled abroad often communicate better with patients from different backgrounds, so the benefits go beyond just personal enjoyment.

Câu 4How does travelling help people improve their language skills?

AKhẳng định: du lịch là cách hiệu quả để luyện ngoại ngữ thực tế.

RGiải thích: buộc phải giao tiếp thực tế với người bản ngữ trong tình huống thật.

EVí dụ: hỏi đường, đặt phòng, mua sắm đều là cơ hội luyện tiếng Anh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think travelling is one of the best ways to improve language skills because it forces you to use the language in real situations. When you are abroad, you have to communicate with locals to ask for directions, order food, or find your hotel, so you practise every day. In my experience, even a two-week trip can improve your confidence in speaking English much more than months of studying in a classroom.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Travelling definitely helps with language learning, but I think the improvement depends on how open you are to interact with local people. Some travellers only stay with groups of their own nationality, so they do not practise much. However, if you make the effort to speak the local language or at least use English with local culture guides and shopkeepers, your language skills can improve very quickly.

Câu 5Do you think travelling alone is better than travelling with friends or family?

ANêu quan điểm rõ ràng: thích du lịch một mình hoặc theo nhóm, và lý do.

RGiải thích: du lịch một mình giúp tự lập; du lịch nhóm vui hơn và an toàn hơn.

EVí dụ: so sánh hai trải nghiệm cụ thể hoặc tình huống phù hợp với mỗi loại.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I personally think solo travel has some great advantages because you can plan everything according to your own interests and move at your own pace. You also meet more new people when you travel alone because you are more open to talking to strangers. However, I admit that travelling with friends is more fun and safer, especially in an unfamiliar country.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, travelling with family or friends is generally better, especially for first-time travellers, because you can share costs and look after each other. Solo travel can be rewarding but also stressful if something goes wrong, like losing your bag or getting sick. As someone studying medicine, I know how important it is to have support when you are unwell, so I prefer travelling with at least one companion.

Câu 6Should governments spend more money to develop tourism in their country?

AĐồng ý có điều kiện: chính phủ nên đầu tư du lịch nhưng cần cân bằng với các ưu tiên khác.

RGiải thích: du lịch tạo ra việc làm và nguồn thu ngoại tệ cho đất nước.

EVí dụ: Việt Nam đã phát triển ngành du lịch thành công nhờ đầu tư vào cơ sở hạ tầng và quảng bá.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think governments should invest in tourism because it can create many jobs and bring in significant income for the country. Better tourist attractions, roads, and services benefit both visitors and local residents. For example, Vietnam has developed its tourism industry successfully by improving airports and promoting its beautiful natural scenery, which has helped the economy grow.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I partly agree that governments should invest in tourism, but I think they must balance this with other important sectors like health and education. Eco-tourism and sustainable tourism projects are especially worth funding because they protect the environment while still attracting visitors. However, spending too much on luxury resorts for foreign tourists while local people lack basic services would be a wrong priority.

Câu 7How has technology changed the way people travel?

ANêu rõ: công nghệ đã thay đổi hoàn toàn cách lên kế hoạch và trải nghiệm du lịch.

RGiải thích: ứng dụng điện thoại, đặt vé online, bản đồ GPS giúp du lịch dễ dàng hơn nhiều.

EVí dụ: so sánh cách du lịch có bản đồ giấy ngày xưa với dùng Google Maps ngày nay.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Technology has completely transformed the way people travel. With smartphones, we can book flights and hotels online within minutes, use GPS maps to navigate, and even translate signs in a foreign language using a camera app. In the past, travellers had to rely on paper maps and travel agents, which was much slower and sometimes more expensive.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the biggest change that technology has brought to travel planning is the ability to read reviews and compare prices instantly. Websites and apps like Booking.com or Google Maps allow travellers to make informed decisions and travel on a tight budget more easily than ever. However, I also think technology can make people less adventurous because they always follow a pre-planned route instead of getting pleasantly lost and discovering something unexpected.

Câu 8Why do some people prefer to travel to natural places rather than cities?

ANêu lý do: thiên nhiên mang lại sự bình yên và giúp phục hồi tinh thần.

RGiải thích: thành phố ồn ào và đông đúc, thiên nhiên giúp giảm stress tốt hơn.

EVí dụ: so sánh chuyến đi biển hoặc núi với chuyến đi thành phố lớn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people prefer natural destinations because they want to escape from the noise and crowds of city life. Spending time in nature, whether at the beach, in the mountains, or in a forest, has proven health benefits such as reducing stress and improving mood. As a medical student, I can confirm that a weekend trip to a quiet natural area can restore your energy far better than visiting a busy city.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, natural places attract travellers because they offer a completely different experience from urban environments. People who live and work in cities every day find that visiting eco-tourism destinations helps them slow down and reconnect with the environment. However, I think cities also have unique cultural and historical attractions, so the best choice really depends on what the traveller is looking for.

Câu 9Do you think international travel is more beneficial than domestic travel?

ANêu quan điểm cân bằng: cả hai đều có lợi ích riêng, tuỳ vào mục đích.

RGiải thích: du lịch quốc tế cho [[cultural exchange]] sâu hơn; trong nước tiết kiệm và dễ tiếp cận hơn.

EVí dụ: một bác sĩ tương lai có thể học được nhiều từ chuyến đi nước ngoài lẫn từ vùng nông thôn trong nước.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe both international and domestic travel have their own benefits, but international travel gives you a much deeper experience of cultural exchange and exposes you to truly different ways of life. Visiting another country forces you to adapt to a new language, currency, and social norms, which builds resilience and open-mindedness. That said, not everyone can afford international trips, so domestic travel is a great alternative to explore new places closer to home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Personally, I think domestic travel is often undervalued. Vietnam is a diverse country with many beautiful landscapes, historic sites, and ethnic cultures that most Vietnamese people have never visited. While international travel certainly helps you broaden perspectives on a global scale, I think understanding your own country deeply is equally important, especially for medical professionals who serve local communities.

Câu 10How can travelling affect a person's health, both positively and negatively?

ANêu cả hai mặt: tích cực (giảm stress, tăng vận động) và tiêu cực (mệt mỏi, bệnh).

RGiải thích: du lịch giúp thư giãn tinh thần nhưng có thể gây kiệt sức, mắc bệnh lạ hoặc chấn thương.

EVí dụ: góc nhìn của sinh viên y khoa về các rủi ro sức khỏe khi đi du lịch.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Travelling can have many positive effects on health. It reduces stress, encourages physical activity like walking and hiking, and improves mental well-being by giving people new positive travel experiences. As a medical student, I know that rest and a change of environment can do wonders for a person's immune system and mood.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On the other hand, travelling also carries some health risks that people should not ignore. Long international flights can cause deep vein thrombosis, and visiting tropical countries exposes travellers to diseases like dengue fever or food poisoning. As someone studying medicine, I always recommend that travellers get the necessary vaccines, carry a basic medical kit, and travel with travel insurance to stay safe.

Câu 11Should children be taken on international trips? Why or why not?

AĐồng ý có điều kiện: trẻ em nên được đi du lịch quốc tế nhưng cần lên kế hoạch cẩn thận.

RGiải thích: trẻ học hỏi nhanh từ môi trường thực tế, nhưng cần phù hợp với lứa tuổi và sức khoẻ.

EVí dụ: trẻ em đi du lịch tiếp xúc với ngôn ngữ và văn hóa mới từ sớm, giúp phát triển tư duy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think taking children on international trips is a wonderful idea because children learn through experience much faster than through books. Seeing different cultures, trying new foods, and hearing foreign languages at a young age helps children become more open-minded and adaptable. Of course, parents need to plan carefully and choose destinations that are safe and suitable for children.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that children can benefit from international travel, but I think it should not be rushed. Very young children may find long journeys stressful, and some destinations may not be safe in terms of hygiene or local health risks. For school-age children, a well-planned trip abroad can be an amazing learning experience that teaches empathy, curiosity, and the value of cultural exchange in a way no classroom can.

Câu 12Do you think people should be responsible tourists? How can they do this?

AKhẳng định: du khách cần có trách nhiệm với môi trường và cộng đồng địa phương.

RGiải thích: du lịch thiếu trách nhiệm gây ô nhiễm, phá hoại văn hóa và cơ sở hạ tầng địa phương.

EVí dụ: không xả rác, tôn trọng phong tục, mua sản phẩm địa phương thay vì hàng nhập khẩu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, I think being a responsible tourist is very important in today's world. Irresponsible tourism can damage natural environments, harm local culture, and put pressure on resources that local communities depend on. Simple actions like not littering, respecting sacred sites, and buying from local businesses instead of large chains can make a big positive difference.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I strongly believe people should practise eco-tourism principles whenever they travel. This means choosing environmentally friendly accommodation, avoiding single-use plastics, and learning about local customs before you visit. As medical students, we are trained to do no harm, and I think the same principle should apply to the places we travel to - we should leave them in better or at least the same condition as we found them.

Câu 13How does travelling to different countries help people understand global health issues?

AKhẳng định: du lịch giúp thấy trực tiếp các vấn đề y tế toàn cầu mà sách giáo khoa không truyền đạt được.

RGiải thích: tiếp xúc với hệ thống y tế, môi trường sống và bệnh tật khác nhau mở rộng nhận thức.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa thấy tình trạng suy dinh dưỡng hoặc bệnh nhiệt đới khi đi các nước đang phát triển.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Travelling to different countries is incredibly valuable for understanding global health challenges. When you visit low-income countries, you see first-hand the impact of poor sanitation, limited access to medicine, and malnutrition, which deepens your understanding as a future doctor. This kind of real-world exposure cannot be replaced by reading textbooks, and it motivates medical students to think about healthcare on a wider scale.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think international travel helps medical students broaden perspectives on public health in a very powerful way. For example, visiting a country with a different healthcare system shows you alternative approaches to patient care, disease prevention, and community health. Experiences like these encourage doctors to question assumptions and explore new places of knowledge that ultimately make them better clinicians.

Câu 14Compare the experience of travelling for leisure and travelling for work or study.

ASo sánh rõ ràng: du lịch vui chơi giúp thư giãn; du lịch công tác/học tập có mục tiêu cụ thể hơn.

RGiải thích: cả hai đều mở rộng tầm nhìn nhưng theo cách khác nhau và mang lại lợi ích khác nhau.

EVí dụ: tham dự hội nghị y khoa ở nước ngoài so với đi du lịch biển.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my view, leisure travel and work or study travel are quite different experiences. Leisure travel allows you to relax, recharge, and explore new places with no particular goal in mind, which is great for mental health. On the other hand, travelling for a medical conference or study programme has clear objectives, and while it can be exhausting, it provides professional growth and networking opportunities that leisure travel cannot.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think both types of travel are valuable but in different ways. When you travel for leisure, you are free to follow your own interests and pace, which makes the experience enjoyable and stress-free. However, travelling for work or study, such as attending an international medical seminar, forces you to engage deeply with new knowledge and build professional connections, which can have a lasting impact on your career as a doctor.

Câu 15Do you think the rise of tourism has more advantages or disadvantages for local communities?

ANêu quan điểm cân bằng: du lịch có lợi về kinh tế nhưng cũng gây nhiều vấn đề xã hội và môi trường.

RGiải thích: tạo việc làm và phát triển hạ tầng là tích cực; nhưng ô nhiễm, tăng giá và mất bản sắc văn hóa là tiêu cực.

EVí dụ: Hội An hoặc Phú Quốc phát triển nhanh nhờ du lịch nhưng cũng đối mặt với vấn đề rác thải và đô thị hóa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the rise of tourism brings more advantages than disadvantages when it is well-managed. Tourism creates jobs, improves local infrastructure like roads and hospitals, and helps communities preserve their local culture by sharing it with visitors. For example, many small towns in Vietnam have seen significant economic growth thanks to domestic and international tourists spending money on food, accommodation, and crafts.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On the other hand, I believe mass tourism can seriously harm local communities if it is not controlled properly. Overcrowding, rising costs of living, pollution, and the erosion of local culture are real problems in many popular destinations. I think governments and communities need to promote eco-tourism and limit the number of visitors to sensitive areas, so that local people and the environment are protected for future generations.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank
