Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Your best friend

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • best friendngười bạn thân nhất
  • hang outđi chơi cùng nhau, tụ tập
  • get along wellhợp nhau, hòa hợp tốt
  • trusttin tưởng, sự tin tưởng
  • support each otherhỗ trợ nhau, giúp đỡ lẫn nhau
  • share interestscó chung sở thích
  • keep in touchgiữ liên lạc
  • childhood friendbạn thời thơ ấu
  • similar personalitytính cách tương đồng
  • have a lot in commoncó nhiều điểm chung
  • close bondmối quan hệ gắn bó chặt chẽ
  • loyal friendngười bạn trung thành
  • make memoriestạo nên những kỷ niệm
  • catch upnói chuyện để cập nhật tin tức cho nhau sau thời gian xa cách

Câu 1Who is your best friend?

ANêu tên và cho biết bạn quen người đó ở đâu hoặc từ khi nào.

RGiải thích tại sao người đó là bạn thân nhất của bạn.

EKể một chi tiết cụ thể về người bạn đó để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My best friend is Minh, and we have known each other since secondary school. He is my closest friend because he always listens to me and gives honest advice. For example, when I was stressed about my medical exams, he stayed up late to help me review the material.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My best friend is Lan, a colleague I met during my first year at the military medical academy. She became my closest friend because we have a lot in common, including our passion for medicine and our sense of humour. I remember how she helped me feel welcome when I first arrived and did not know anyone.

Câu 2What do you usually do together?

ANêu một hoặc hai hoạt động thường xuyên làm cùng nhau.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích làm những hoạt động đó cùng nhau.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về lần gần nhất bạn làm hoạt động đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

We usually hang out at a cafe near the hospital on weekends to catch up on each other's lives. I enjoy this because it helps us relax after a long week of clinical work. Last Saturday, we spent two hours there talking about our new rotation and sharing funny stories from the ward.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We often go jogging together in the park early in the morning before our shifts start. This is great because it keeps us both fit and gives us time to talk. Just last week we ran five kilometres together and planned our next short trip during the national holiday.

Câu 3Why do you like spending time with this person?

ANêu lý do chính tại sao bạn thích ở cùng người bạn đó.

RGiải thích cảm xúc hoặc lợi ích bạn nhận được khi ở cùng họ.

EMô tả một tình huống cụ thể cho thấy điều đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I love spending time with Minh because he makes me feel calm and happy even on stressful days. We get along well because we have a lot in common and never run out of things to talk about. Once, after a very difficult night shift, he brought me food and sat with me for an hour, which really lifted my mood.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy being with Lan because she always support each other and never judges me when I make mistakes. Her positive energy is very encouraging, especially during hard periods of study. I remember when I failed a practical test, she encouraged me to try again and even practised with me every evening until I passed.

Câu 4How did you first meet your best friend?

ANêu rõ bạn gặp người đó ở đâu và trong hoàn cảnh nào.

RGiải thích điều gì khiến lần gặp đó trở nên đáng nhớ hoặc đặc biệt.

EKể một chi tiết nhỏ về cuộc gặp gỡ đầu tiên đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I first met my best friend on the first day of university when we were placed in the same study group. I remember feeling nervous, but he immediately introduced himself and offered to share his notes with me. That small gesture made me feel that he was a genuinely kind person.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We met during military training camp before our academic year officially began. We were assigned to the same dormitory room, so we got to know each other very quickly. I noticed we both kept medical textbooks on our desks, and that shared passion became the starting point of our close bond.

Câu 5How long have you known your best friend?

ANêu khoảng thời gian bạn đã quen biết người đó.

RGiải thích tại sao tình bạn đó vẫn bền vững qua thời gian.

ENhắc đến một kỷ niệm quan trọng trong quá trình đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I have known my best friend for almost eight years now, since we were in our first year of secondary school. Our friendship has lasted so long because we always keep in touch even when we are busy. One of my favourite memories is the trip we took together to celebrate finishing our high school exams.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We have been friends for about four years, since we started at the military medical academy together. Our close bond has grown stronger because we went through the same difficult training and examinations side by side. It feels like a very long time when I think about how much we have experienced together.

Câu 6Do you and your best friend share the same interests?

ANêu bạn có cùng sở thích không và đó là sở thích gì.

RGiải thích những điểm chung đó ảnh hưởng thế nào đến tình bạn.

ENêu một hoạt động cụ thể mà cả hai cùng thích.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, we definitely share interests in medicine and outdoor activities, which makes spending time together very enjoyable. Having common hobbies means we always have something fun to plan together. For example, we both love hiking, and last month we climbed a small mountain near the city on a day off.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We actually have quite different hobbies - he loves video games while I prefer reading - but we both enjoy watching films and cooking. I think having some differences makes the friendship more interesting because we learn new things from each other. He recently introduced me to a game I ended up really enjoying.

Câu 7How do you usually keep in touch with your best friend?

ANêu cách thức liên lạc chính mà hai bạn dùng.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó phù hợp với lịch bận rộn của hai bạn.

EMô tả một cuộc trò chuyện hoặc tình huống gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

We mainly keep in touch by sending voice messages on Zalo because it is quicker than typing when we are busy on the ward. This works well for us because we can listen to each other's messages between tasks. Just yesterday, she sent me a funny message about a patient who spoke in an unusual dialect.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We try to meet in person at least once a week, and on days when that is not possible, we video call in the evening. I prefer face-to-face contact because I can read her expressions and it feels more personal than texting. Last Tuesday we had a long video call where we discussed our career plans after graduation.

Câu 8Has your best friend ever helped you through a difficult time?

ANêu ngắn gọn tình huống khó khăn đó là gì.

RGiải thích người bạn đó đã giúp bạn như thế nào.

ENêu cảm xúc của bạn sau khi nhận được sự giúp đỡ đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, when my grandfather was seriously ill last year, I was very distracted and could not focus on my studies. My best friend visited me regularly and helped me revise for my exams by explaining topics I had missed. Thanks to his loyal support, I managed to pass that semester without failing any subjects.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Definitely. When I first started my clinical rotation, I felt very overwhelmed and doubted myself. She spent several evenings with me practising patient communication skills and calming my nerves. Her encouragement gave me the confidence to get through the rotation, and I am very grateful for that.

Câu 9What qualities do you most admire in your best friend?

ANêu một hoặc hai phẩm chất nổi bật nhất mà bạn ngưỡng mộ.

RGiải thích tại sao những phẩm chất đó quan trọng với bạn.

EKể một ví dụ cụ thể thể hiện phẩm chất đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I most admire his patience and honesty, because these are qualities that are hard to find in a friend. He always tells me the truth even when it is not what I want to hear, and that makes me trust him completely. Once, he politely told me that my presentation skills needed improvement, and that feedback helped me a lot.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I really admire her ability to stay calm under pressure, especially during demanding shifts at the hospital. She never panics, and her composure helps me feel more confident too. I remember watching her handle a difficult emergency case with total focus, and I thought to myself that I wanted to develop that same quality.

Câu 10Do you think it is important to have a best friend? Why?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn: có hay không có bạn thân là quan trọng.

RGiải thích lý do bằng một lợi ích cụ thể của tình bạn thân.

ELiên hệ với kinh nghiệm cá nhân để làm rõ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think having a best friend is very important because life can be very stressful, especially in the medical field, and having someone you trust makes it easier to cope. A true friend helps you stay emotionally healthy and motivated. Without my best friend's support during hard exam periods, I think I would have felt much more isolated.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, I believe it is important, but the quality of the friendship matters more than simply having one. A genuine best friend gives you honest advice and supports each other through challenges, which is more valuable than having many casual friends. From my own experience, one truly close friend has helped me more than a large group of acquaintances.

Câu 11Have you and your best friend ever had an argument? How did you resolve it?

AThành thật nêu có hay không và ngắn gọn nêu tình huống đó.

RGiải thích cách hai bạn giải quyết mâu thuẫn đó.

ENêu điều bạn học được từ tình huống đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, we had a small argument once about a group project where we disagreed on how to divide the work. We resolved it by sitting down calmly and talking about each person's strengths. That experience actually made our close bond stronger because we learned to communicate more openly with each other.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We have had a few minor disagreements, but we always manage to work things out quickly because we both value the friendship too much to let misunderstandings grow. Usually, one of us will send a message to apologise first, and then we talk it through. I learned that being willing to say sorry first is a sign of maturity, not weakness.

Câu 12Do you prefer having one best friend or a large group of friends?

ANêu rõ sở thích của bạn: một người bạn thân hay nhiều bạn bè.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn như vậy dựa trên tính cách hoặc lối sống.

ESo sánh ngắn với lựa chọn còn lại để làm rõ quan điểm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I personally prefer having one best friend whom I can really trust rather than a large group of acquaintances. This is because I am quite introverted and find deep one-on-one conversations more satisfying than large social gatherings. I feel I can be completely honest with my best friend in a way that would not be possible in a big group.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think a balance is ideal - I enjoy having a small circle of good friends as well as one best friend I am especially close to. A larger group gives me variety and fun social experiences, while my best friend provides the deeper emotional support I sometimes need. As a medical student with limited free time, this combination works well for me.

Câu 13Has your friendship changed since you started medical school?

ANêu tình bạn đã thay đổi như thế nào kể từ khi học y.

RGiải thích tại sao việc học y khoa lại tác động đến tình bạn đó.

ENêu một ví dụ cụ thể về sự thay đổi đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Our friendship has definitely grown stronger since we started medical school because we now face the same pressures and understand each other's struggles much better. We support each other through long study sessions and difficult clinical rotations, which has created a deeper level of trust. Before university, our friendship was more casual, but now we rely on each other in a much more meaningful way.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, it has changed a little because we are both so busy that we can no longer hang out as often as before. However, the time we do spend together has become more valuable and meaningful. We make memories during the rare moments we share, like celebrating together after finishing a tough exam.

Câu 14What is one memorable experience you have shared with your best friend?

ANêu tên hoặc mô tả ngắn gọn về kỷ niệm đáng nhớ đó.

RGiải thích tại sao kỷ niệm đó đặc biệt với bạn.

EChia sẻ một chi tiết cụ thể giúp kỷ niệm đó sống động.

Trả lời mẫu 1

One of my most memorable experiences with my best friend was a weekend trip we took to the coast after finishing our third-year exams. It was the first time we had really relaxed together without thinking about medicine. We make memories by watching the sunrise on the beach, and I still look back on that trip as one of the happiest moments of my university life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I remember a night when we stayed up until 3 a.m. helping a classmate prepare for an important presentation the next morning. The three of us worked together, and our classmate performed very well. That experience showed me what a loyal friend my best friend is, because he gave up his own sleep to help someone else without being asked.

Câu 15Do you think your best friend will be an important part of your life in the future?

ANêu quan điểm về tương lai của tình bạn này.

RGiải thích lý do bạn nghĩ như vậy dựa trên tình bạn hiện tại.

ENêu một kế hoạch hoặc mong muốn cụ thể cho tương lai của hai bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I am sure my best friend will remain an important part of my life for many years to come. Our close bond has been built on shared experiences and mutual trust, and I believe that kind of friendship does not fade easily. I hope that even after we graduate and work in different hospitals, we will still keep in touch and visit each other regularly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I definitely hope so, although I know that life after graduation may take us to different cities or even different countries. What gives me confidence is that we have a lot in common and genuinely care about each other, so I think we will make the effort to stay connected. I would love for us to one day work together in the same hospital or even collaborate on medical research.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Your favourite teacher

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • favourite teachergiáo viên yêu thích nhất
  • inspiringtruyền cảm hứng
  • patientkiên nhẫn (với học sinh)
  • explain clearlygiải thích rõ ràng
  • teaching stylephong cách giảng dạy
  • supportivehỗ trợ, luôn sẵn sàng giúp đỡ
  • passionate aboutđam mê, nhiệt tình với
  • make a differencetạo ra sự thay đổi tích cực
  • strict but fairnghiêm khắc nhưng công bằng
  • pay attentionchú ý, tập trung
  • memorable lessonbài học đáng nhớ
  • give feedbackđưa ra phản hồi, nhận xét
  • keep in touchgiữ liên lạc
  • role modelhình mẫu, người để noi gương

Câu 1Who is your favourite teacher?

ANêu tên hoặc mô tả người giáo viên yêu thích.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn chọn người đó.

EĐưa ra một kỷ niệm hoặc chi tiết cụ thể về giáo viên đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite teacher is Mr Hung, who taught me biology in secondary school. I choose him because he was always very patient and never made students feel embarrassed when they asked questions. I still remember how he stayed after class to help me understand difficult topics before my exams.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My favourite teacher is Dr Lan, my anatomy professor at the academy. She stands out because she is truly passionate about her subject and that enthusiasm spreads to the whole class. Even now I think of her whenever I study a challenging medical concept.

Câu 2What subject does he or she teach?

ANêu tên môn học mà giáo viên đó dạy.

RGiải thích tại sao môn học đó quan trọng với bạn.

ECho biết bạn học môn đó ở đâu hoặc vào thời điểm nào.

Trả lời mẫu 1

She teaches anatomy, which is one of the core subjects in our medical programme. I find this subject very important because a strong understanding of the human body is the foundation of all clinical work. I first studied it in my second year at the academy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

He teaches biology at secondary school level. This subject was crucial for me because it helped me decide to pursue a career in medicine. Without his engaging lessons, I might not have developed such a strong interest in the sciences.

Câu 3Why do you like this teacher?

ANêu trực tiếp lý do bạn thích giáo viên đó.

RGiải thích cụ thể điều gì ở họ khiến bạn ấn tượng.

EKể một tình huống cụ thể minh họa lý do đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I like her because she always explains clearly and uses real-life medical cases to illustrate her points. Her teaching style makes even the most complicated topics feel manageable. For example, she once used a simple drawing to help me finally understand the structure of the heart.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I like him because he is strict but fair, which pushed me to study harder. He always gave feedback on our work in a kind but honest way. Once he pointed out a mistake in my assignment and helped me correct it step by step, which I really appreciated.

Câu 4How long have you known this teacher?

ANêu khoảng thời gian bạn đã biết giáo viên đó.

RGiải thích bạn gặp họ trong hoàn cảnh nào.

ECho biết mối quan hệ của bạn với họ hiện tại như thế nào.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I have known Mr Hung for about ten years now, since he taught me in secondary school. We first met when I was thirteen years old and he was my biology teacher. We still keep in touch occasionally and he always encourages me in my medical studies.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have known Dr Lan for around three years, since I started studying at the military medical academy. She became my professor in the second semester of my first year. Although I no longer attend her classes, I still see her at academic events on campus.

Câu 5What do you remember most about this teacher?

ANêu điều bạn nhớ nhất về giáo viên đó.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó in sâu trong trí nhớ bạn.

EMô tả một tình huống hoặc khoảnh khắc cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

What I remember most is a memorable lesson about blood circulation that she turned into a kind of role-play activity. It was so creative that the whole class was fully engaged. That lesson made me realise how inspiring a good teacher can be.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I remember most how he always greeted every student by name when we entered the classroom. This small gesture made us feel valued and ready to pay attention. It taught me that respect between teacher and student is very important in education.

Câu 6Did this teacher influence your choice of career?

ACho biết giáo viên đó có ảnh hưởng đến lựa chọn nghề nghiệp của bạn không.

RGiải thích cách họ ảnh hưởng đến quyết định của bạn.

EMô tả khoảnh khắc hoặc lời khuyên cụ thể đã thay đổi suy nghĩ của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, she definitely influenced my career choice. She was so passionate about medicine and healthcare that her enthusiasm was contagious. After attending her lectures, I became certain that I wanted to become a doctor and make a difference in people's lives.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, his influence was indirect but powerful. He showed me how much I enjoyed science, which led me to study biology more seriously. Eventually that passion guided me toward a career in medicine, so I think he made a difference in my life without even knowing it.

Câu 7What was this teacher's teaching style like?

AMô tả phong cách giảng dạy của giáo viên đó.

RGiải thích tại sao phong cách đó phù hợp với bạn.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ về cách họ dạy trong lớp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Her teaching style was very interactive. She asked lots of questions and encouraged students to discuss ideas rather than just listen. For example, she would present a clinical case and ask us to diagnose it as a group, which made learning feel like real medical practice.

Trả lời mẫu 2

His teaching style was structured and clear. He always started the lesson with a short review, then explained clearly the new content, and ended with a quick quiz. This predictable pattern helped me feel confident and prepared during every class.

Câu 8Was this teacher strict or easy-going?

ANêu giáo viên đó nghiêm khắc hay thoải mái.

RGiải thích điều đó ảnh hưởng đến lớp học như thế nào.

EKể một ví dụ thể hiện tính cách đó của giáo viên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

She was strict but fair. She had high expectations and did not accept late homework, but she always explained the reasons behind her rules. Because of her high standards, I worked harder and my results improved a lot by the end of the year.

Trả lời mẫu 2

He was quite easy-going in the sense that he created a relaxed atmosphere in class. However, he was still very serious about learning outcomes and always gave feedback when our work was not up to standard. It was a good balance that kept us both comfortable and motivated.

Câu 9How did this teacher treat students?

AMô tả cách giáo viên đó đối xử với học sinh.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đối xử đó có ý nghĩa với bạn.

ECho biết một khoảnh khắc cụ thể bạn cảm nhận được điều đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

She treated all students equally and was very supportive of those who struggled. She never singled anyone out in a negative way. I remember one time I failed a test and she quietly encouraged me after class, which motivated me to study harder instead of giving up.

Trả lời mẫu 2

He treated students like young adults and always respected our opinions. He was patient when we made mistakes and used them as teaching moments rather than reasons to scold us. That respectful attitude made me feel more confident to speak up and ask questions in class.

Câu 10Do you still keep in touch with this teacher?

ACho biết bạn có còn liên lạc với giáo viên đó không.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao vẫn giữ hay đã mất liên lạc.

EMô tả lần cuối cùng bạn gặp hoặc liên lạc với họ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I still keep in touch with Dr Lan through email occasionally. I usually write to her when I have a question about my research or need advice. She always replies quickly and I am grateful for her continued supportive role in my academic life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Unfortunately, I have lost contact with Mr Hung since I moved to a different city for university. However, I follow his updates on social media and I know he is still teaching and inspiring new students. I hope to visit him one day and thank him in person.

Câu 11What is one lesson from this teacher that you will never forget?

ANêu một bài học cụ thể bạn không bao giờ quên.

RGiải thích tại sao bài học đó quan trọng với bạn.

EMô tả hoàn cảnh mà bài học đó được dạy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

One memorable lesson she taught me is that understanding is more important than memorising. She said that in medicine, you must know the 'why' behind every fact. This advice has shaped the way I approach my studies and I apply it every day as a medical student.

Trả lời mẫu 2

He once told us that failure is just the first step to success, and that lesson stayed with me. He shared his own story of failing an important exam before eventually becoming a teacher. That moment of honesty made him a true role model in my eyes.

Câu 12Is this teacher a role model for you? Why?

ACho biết giáo viên đó có là hình mẫu của bạn không.

RGiải thích phẩm chất nào khiến họ trở thành hình mẫu.

ECho biết bạn cố gắng học theo họ như thế nào.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, she is definitely a role model for me. She combines professional excellence with genuine kindness, which is exactly the kind of doctor and educator I want to become. I try to copy her habit of always giving feedback in a constructive and encouraging way when I help my juniors.

Trả lời mẫu 2

He is a role model for me, though not in the traditional sense. What I admire most is his dedication and consistency over many years of teaching. He showed me that being passionate about your work, no matter how routine it seems, is the key to doing it well.

Câu 13Would you like to become a teacher like him or her one day?

ANêu ý kiến của bạn về việc trở thành giáo viên như họ.

RGiải thích lý do bạn muốn hoặc không muốn điều đó.

ELiên hệ với công việc hoặc mục tiêu tương lai của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would love to become a teacher like her one day. As a military medical officer, I will likely have opportunities to train younger colleagues and I want to inspire them the way she inspired me. I hope to adopt her clear teaching style and her ability to explain clearly complex ideas.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I am not sure I could be as dedicated as he was to full-time teaching. However, I do want to be a supportive senior colleague and make a difference to junior doctors in my unit. I think mentoring in the clinic is a form of teaching that fits my career path well.

Câu 14Do you think it is easy to be a good teacher? Why?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về việc làm giáo viên giỏi có dễ không.

RGiải thích những thách thức hoặc yêu cầu của nghề giáo.

EDùng ví dụ từ giáo viên yêu thích của bạn để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think it is easy to be a good teacher at all. A good teacher needs to be patient, knowledgeable, and creative at the same time. My favourite teacher made it look effortless, but I know she spent many hours preparing her lessons and thinking about how to help each student.

Trả lời mẫu 2

No, I think it is quite difficult to be a genuinely good teacher. You need not only subject knowledge but also the ability to explain clearly to students with different learning speeds. Watching my teacher manage a class of thirty students with different levels of understanding made me realise how skilled and inspiring he truly was.

Câu 15What qualities do you think a good teacher should have?

ANêu những phẩm chất bạn cho là quan trọng ở một giáo viên giỏi.

RGiải thích tại sao những phẩm chất đó quan trọng.

ELiên hệ với giáo viên yêu thích của bạn để làm ví dụ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think a good teacher must be patient, supportive, and passionate about the subject they teach. Patience helps students feel safe to make mistakes, while passion makes lessons more engaging. My favourite teacher had all these qualities, which is exactly why I learned so much from her.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, the most important quality is the ability to explain clearly and adapt to different students. A good teacher also needs to give feedback regularly so students know how to improve. My favourite teacher was a perfect example of this because he always made sure no student was left behind.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Watching TV has both benefits and problems

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • television programmechương trình truyền hình
  • stay informedcập nhật thông tin
  • current eventscác sự kiện thời sự hiện tại
  • news channelkênh tin tức
  • unwindthư giãn, giải tỏa căng thẳng
  • favourite seriesbộ phim / chương trình yêu thích
  • waste timelãng phí thời gian
  • watch too much TVxem TV quá nhiều
  • affect concentrationảnh hưởng đến sự tập trung
  • educational contentnội dung có tính giáo dục
  • broaden knowledgemở rộng kiến thức
  • sedentary lifestylelối sống ít vận động
  • screen timethời gian nhìn màn hình
  • documentaryphim tài liệu
  • balance TV habitscân bằng thói quen xem TV
  • physical activityhoạt động thể chất

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

Today I would like to talk about the topic: watching TV has both benefits and problems. In my opinion, TV can be very useful, but it also has some drawbacks if we are not careful. First of all, watching TV helps us stay informed about current events. By turning on a news channel, we can quickly learn about what is happening in the world. For example, during the COVID pandemic, many people relied on TV to get the latest health updates every day. Another benefit is that TV is a great way to unwind after a busy day. After long hours of studying or working, watching a favourite series or a comedy show helps us relax and forget our stress. For example, I often watch a short television programme in the evening to feel better before bed. However, if we watch too much TV, it can waste time that we could use for exercise or studying. Spending hours in front of the screen can also affect concentration and lead to a sedentary lifestyle. On top of that, one idea I want to add is that TV can also broaden knowledge through educational content like documentary programmes about science or history. These shows make learning more interesting and enjoyable. To sum up, TV has real benefits, but we should balance TV habits and limit our screen time to stay healthy and productive.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Watching TV (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • watch TVxem tivi
  • TV programmechương trình truyền hình
  • streaming servicedịch vụ phát trực tuyến (Netflix, YouTube...)
  • screen timethời gian nhìn vào màn hình
  • binge-watchxem nhiều tập liên tục không ngừng
  • educational contentnội dung có tính giáo dục
  • news channelkênh thời sự / tin tức
  • documentaryphim tài liệu
  • on-demand TVxem truyền hình theo yêu cầu (bất cứ lúc nào)
  • media literacykhả năng đọc hiểu và đánh giá nội dung truyền thông
  • passive entertainmenthình thức giải trí thụ động
  • social mediamạng xã hội
  • limit screen timegiới hạn thời gian xem màn hình
  • reliable source of informationnguồn thông tin đáng tin cậy

Câu 1Why do people enjoy watching TV?

ANêu lý do chính: TV mang lại giải trí và thư giãn.

RGiải thích: sau một ngày làm việc mệt mỏi, con người cần thư giãn dễ dàng.

EVí dụ: xem phim tài liệu y khoa hoặc tin tức để vừa thư giãn vừa học hỏi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think people enjoy watching TV mainly because it is a simple way to relax after a long day. You do not need to go anywhere or spend much money, so it is very convenient. For example, many of my colleagues watch TV programmes about medicine or science in the evening to unwind and still learn something useful. It gives them a comfortable feeling of passive entertainment.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people enjoy TV because it connects them with the world. News channels and documentaries help people stay informed about what is happening around them. For instance, during the COVID-19 pandemic, many Vietnamese families gathered to watch TV together every night to follow the latest health news. This habit created a sense of closeness and shared experience.

Câu 2Do you think people watch too much TV today?

AĐưa ra quan điểm rõ ràng: có hoặc không, hoặc tùy đối tượng.

RGiải thích: [[screen time]] tăng cao ảnh hưởng đến sức khỏe và năng suất.

EVí dụ: trẻ em hoặc người lớn binge-watch đến khuya, ảnh hưởng giấc ngủ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I do think many people watch too much TV these days, especially with the rise of streaming services like Netflix. It is very easy to binge-watch several episodes in one sitting and forget about other responsibilities. For example, some of my students stay up very late watching series and then feel tired during morning classes. We really need to limit screen time to stay healthy and productive.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I partly agree, but I think it depends on the type of content. If someone spends hours watching low-quality shows, that is a waste of time. However, watching educational content or documentaries in moderation can be very beneficial. For example, medical students can learn a lot from health-focused TV programmes, so it is not always a bad habit as long as you choose wisely.

Câu 3Is TV still important in the age of the Internet?

AKhẳng định: TV vẫn còn vai trò quan trọng dù Internet phát triển.

RGiải thích: TV vẫn là nguồn tin tức đáng tin cậy và tiếp cận người cao tuổi tốt hơn.

EVí dụ: người cao tuổi ở nông thôn vẫn dùng TV để nhận thông tin y tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I believe TV is still very important even in the age of the Internet. Many people, especially older generations, still rely on news channels and government TV programmes as their main reliable source of information. For example, in rural areas of Vietnam, elderly patients often learn about health campaigns and vaccination drives through TV rather than through social media. So TV still plays a key role in public communication.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think TV is becoming less dominant, but it has not disappeared. The Internet offers on-demand TV and streaming services, which have partly replaced traditional TV. However, live television still provides a shared national experience, like watching the SEA Games or a live press conference about a disease outbreak. So TV still has a unique value that the Internet has not fully replaced.

Câu 4How has the way people watch TV changed in recent years?

ANêu sự thay đổi: từ TV truyền thống sang xem trực tuyến theo yêu cầu.

RGiải thích: điện thoại và Internet cho phép xem mọi lúc mọi nơi.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa xem lại bài giảng hoặc phim tài liệu y tế trên điện thoại.

Trả lời mẫu 1

The way people watch TV has changed a great deal in recent years. In the past, everyone had to sit in front of a television set at a fixed time to see their favourite show. Now, thanks to streaming services and on-demand TV, people can watch what they want, when they want, on any device. For example, many of my peers watch documentaries about surgery or disease on their phones during their lunch break.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the biggest change is the shift from watching TV as a family activity to watching alone on personal devices. Before, families would gather together every evening around one TV set. Today, each family member might binge-watch different series on their own phone or tablet. This change has made viewing more flexible, but it has also reduced the social aspect of TV programmes.

Câu 5Do you think TV programmes have a positive or negative influence on young people?

AĐưa ra quan điểm cân bằng: cả tích cực lẫn tiêu cực tùy nội dung.

RGiải thích: nội dung giáo dục tốt, nhưng nội dung bạo lực hoặc quảng cáo có hại.

EVí dụ: phim tài liệu khoa học giúp học sinh yêu khoa học, nhưng quảng cáo đồ ăn nhanh gây thói quen xấu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think TV programmes can have a very positive influence if parents choose the right content. Educational content such as science programmes or health documentaries can inspire young people to learn and think critically. For example, I became interested in biology as a child after watching a nature documentary on television. So the key is media literacy and careful selection of programmes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On the negative side, I believe some TV programmes can harm young people by showing unrealistic lifestyles or promoting unhealthy products. Many commercials target children and encourage them to eat junk food or buy unnecessary things. Without proper media literacy, young viewers may not be able to judge what is good or bad for them. Parents and teachers should guide children on how to watch TV responsibly.

Câu 6Should the government control what is shown on TV? Why or why not?

AĐưa ra quan điểm: có kiểm soát ở mức độ nhất định là cần thiết.

RGiải thích: bảo vệ trẻ em và duy trì thông tin chính xác trên [[news channels]].

EVí dụ: quy định giờ phát sóng cho nội dung người lớn hoặc kiểm duyệt tin giả.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think some level of government control over TV programmes is necessary and reasonable. Without any regulation, broadcasters might show violent or misleading content during hours when children are watching TV. For example, many countries have rules about watershed hours, which means adult content can only be shown late at night. This kind of regulation protects young viewers and helps maintain quality educational content on public channels.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While I understand the need for basic standards, I think too much government control can be harmful to freedom of information. If news channels are heavily censored, people may not get accurate or complete information about important issues like public health. A better approach is to encourage media literacy so that viewers themselves can critically evaluate what they watch on TV, rather than relying only on government censorship.

Câu 7How do you think TV is different from social media as a source of information?

ASo sánh: TV có kiểm duyệt biên tập, mạng xã hội nhanh hơn nhưng kém tin cậy hơn.

RGiải thích: [[news channels]] có đội ngũ phóng viên chuyên nghiệp, còn mạng xã hội thiếu kiểm chứng.

EVí dụ: tin giả về vaccine lan truyền nhanh trên mạng xã hội nhưng hiếm khi xuất hiện trên TV chính thống.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think news channels on TV are generally more reliable than social media as sources of information. Television stations usually have professional journalists and editorial teams who check facts before broadcasting. For example, during the pandemic, official health guidelines were first announced on national TV, which helped prevent confusion. Social media, on the other hand, often spreads rumours and unverified claims very quickly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

That said, social media has some advantages over TV. It is much faster and more interactive, allowing ordinary people to share and comment on news in real time. For instance, community groups on Facebook often share local health alerts before they appear on TV programmes. However, because there is no editorial filter, viewers need strong media literacy skills to judge which information from social media is trustworthy.

Câu 8Do you think children should be allowed to watch TV freely, or should parents set limits?

AKhuyến nghị: cha mẹ nên đặt giới hạn [[screen time]] hợp lý cho trẻ.

RGiải thích: quá nhiều [[screen time]] ảnh hưởng sức khỏe mắt, giấc ngủ và việc học.

EVí dụ: giới hạn 1-2 tiếng mỗi ngày và chọn kênh [[educational content]].

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe that parents should set clear limits on children's screen time when it comes to watching TV. Young children do not have the self-control to stop on their own, especially when they enjoy a show. Too much TV can affect their sleep, eyesight, and concentration at school. For example, paediatricians recommend no more than one or two hours of TV programmes per day for school-age children.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that limits are important, but I also think the quality of content matters as much as the quantity. If a child is watching a high-quality educational content programme about nature or science, a little extra screen time may not be harmful. Parents should guide their children to choose good documentaries or learning shows rather than simply switching off the TV. The goal should be smart and balanced viewing habits.

Câu 9Why do some people prefer streaming services over traditional TV?

ANêu lý do: [[streaming services]] linh hoạt hơn, không có quảng cáo và có nhiều lựa chọn.

RGiải thích: người dùng tự chọn nội dung và thời gian xem theo lịch của mình.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ bận rộn có thể xem [[on-demand TV]] vào cuối tuần thay vì theo lịch phát sóng cố định.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people prefer streaming services over traditional TV because of the flexibility they offer. With on-demand TV, you can watch TV programmes at any time that suits you, and you can pause or rewind whenever you like. For example, a busy medical professional might not be able to watch a show at its broadcast time, but they can easily catch up on a streaming service over the weekend. This freedom makes the experience much more convenient.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Another reason is that streaming services usually offer a much wider variety of content than traditional TV channels. You can find documentaries, international series, and niche programmes that would never appear on mainstream television. For example, platforms like Netflix have detailed medical documentaries that are very useful for healthcare students. This variety, combined with fewer advertisements, makes binge-watching quality content a much more attractive option.

Câu 10How does watching TV affect family relationships?

ANêu cả hai mặt: TV có thể gắn kết gia đình hoặc làm giảm giao tiếp trực tiếp.

RGiải thích: xem chung [[TV programmes]] tạo chủ đề trò chuyện, nhưng mỗi người xem riêng trên thiết bị làm gia đình xa cách.

EVí dụ: gia đình cùng xem tin tức buổi tối hoặc phim vào cuối tuần giúp gắn kết.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think watching TV together can actually strengthen family bonds when done in moderation. Sharing a favourite TV programme gives family members something to talk about and laugh over together. For example, many Vietnamese families I know have a tradition of watching the evening news channel together before dinner, which helps them stay connected and discuss current events. This shared routine creates a warm family atmosphere.

Trả lời mẫu 2

However, I also see a negative side. When every family member binge-watches different content on their own devices, there is less real conversation and quality time together. Screen time can become a barrier rather than a bridge. For instance, if parents are always watching TV in the living room while children are on their tablets, the family physically shares a space but is not truly together. So it is important to balance TV time with face-to-face interaction.

Câu 11Should TV be used more in schools and hospitals for education? Why?

AỦng hộ: TV và video [[educational content]] hỗ trợ học tập trực quan hiệu quả.

RGiải thích: hình ảnh động giúp người học hiểu các khái niệm phức tạp dễ hơn sách giáo khoa.

EVí dụ: phim phẫu thuật hoặc [[documentaries]] về bệnh lý giúp sinh viên y khoa học trực quan.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think TV programmes and video educational content should be used more widely in medical schools and hospitals. Visual media can illustrate complex procedures and conditions far more clearly than a textbook. For example, watching a documentary about cardiac surgery gives students a much better understanding of the steps involved than reading a description alone. Using TV screens in ward rounds or classrooms can make learning more engaging and effective.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree, but we should be careful about quality and relevance. Not all TV programmes are accurate or suitable for professional training. It is important to select well-produced educational content from trusted sources. For instance, hospitals could use approved medical documentaries from institutions like the WHO or major universities. When used thoughtfully, television can be a powerful tool to complement traditional teaching methods in healthcare education.

Câu 12Do you think watching the news on TV is more trustworthy than reading news online?

ASo sánh độ tin cậy: TV có quy trình biên tập chặt chẽ hơn, nhưng cần xem đài uy tín.

RGiải thích: [[news channels]] lớn có đội ngũ kiểm chứng thông tin, còn website trực tuyến thiếu kiểm soát.

EVí dụ: tin tức y tế trên VTV hoặc BBC thường đáng tin hơn bài đăng ẩn danh trên mạng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think watching established news channels on TV tends to be more reliable than reading random news online. Major television broadcasters follow strict editorial standards and are legally responsible for what they broadcast. For example, health announcements on national TV during the pandemic were carefully verified by the Ministry of Health before airing, which made them a reliable source of information for the public. Online sources do not always have this level of accountability.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On the other hand, I think it depends on which online source you use. Reputable newspapers and official health websites can be just as trustworthy as TV news channels, and they often provide more detail and data. The real problem is media literacy, because many people cannot distinguish between credible online journalism and unreliable blogs. Whether you watch TV or read online, the key is to check the source carefully and compare multiple outlets.

Câu 13How do you think TV content has changed compared to 20 years ago?

AMô tả sự thay đổi: nhiều thể loại hơn, chất lượng hình ảnh tốt hơn, nhưng nhiều quảng cáo và nội dung thương mại hơn.

RGiải thích: cạnh tranh với [[streaming services]] buộc TV phải đa dạng hóa nội dung.

EVí dụ: [[documentaries]] về y tế và khoa học ngày nay có chất lượng sản xuất vượt trội so với 20 năm trước.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think TV programmes have improved enormously in terms of production quality over the past 20 years. Documentaries today use high-definition cameras, animation, and expert interviews that make complex topics like human biology very engaging and easy to understand. For example, medical series produced now look completely different from what was available when I was in secondary school. The visual quality alone makes watching TV a much richer educational experience today.

Trả lời mẫu 2

However, I also think there is more low-quality and commercial content on TV today than 20 years ago. Many channels are filled with reality shows and advertisements rather than genuine educational content or serious news channels. The competition from streaming services has pushed some broadcasters to chase ratings instead of quality. So while the best TV is better than ever, the average content has arguably become less meaningful.

Câu 14Do you think people who watch a lot of TV are less active or social?

AThừa nhận một phần: [[binge-watching]] có thể làm giảm hoạt động thể chất và giao tiếp xã hội.

RGiải thích: ngồi lâu trước màn hình thay thế thời gian vận động và gặp gỡ bạn bè.

EVí dụ: người xem TV nhiều giờ mỗi ngày thường ít tập thể dục và ít gặp bạn bè hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think there is some truth to this. People who binge-watch TV for many hours a day often have less time and energy for physical activity or face-to-face socialising. Screen time replaces time that could be spent exercising, cooking, or meeting friends. For example, studies have shown that excessive TV viewing is linked to a more sedentary lifestyle, which is a real concern in the medical community because it contributes to obesity and cardiovascular risk.

Trả lời mẫu 2

That said, I do not think watching TV automatically makes someone less social or active. It depends on the person's overall lifestyle. Someone might watch TV for an hour in the evening after a full day of work, exercise, and social interaction, and that is perfectly healthy. The problem only arises when screen time becomes excessive and starts replacing more important activities. Moderation and balance are the key factors.

Câu 15Should TV programmes include more health and medical content? Why?

AỦng hộ mạnh mẽ: nhiều nội dung y tế hơn trên TV giúp cộng đồng phòng bệnh và nâng cao nhận thức sức khỏe.

RGiải thích: nhiều người vẫn tin tưởng TV hơn Internet, nên đây là kênh truyền thông y tế hiệu quả.

EVí dụ: [[TV programmes]] về phòng chống ung thư, tiểu đường hoặc sức khỏe tâm thần có thể cứu sống nhiều người.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, I strongly believe that TV programmes should include more health and medical content, especially in Vietnam where many people still rely on TV as their main reliable source of information. Well-made educational content about disease prevention, nutrition, and mental health can reach millions of viewers who might never visit a doctor or read a medical article. For example, a simple documentary about the warning signs of stroke could save many lives if broadcast on a popular news channel during prime time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree, but the quality and accuracy of medical content on TV must be carefully controlled. If health TV programmes contain outdated advice or promote unproven treatments, they could do more harm than good. Producers should work closely with qualified doctors and hospitals like Hoc vien Quan y to create content that is both accurate and easy to understand. When done properly, health-focused educational content on television is one of the most powerful public health tools available.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 5

Part 1: Sports and exercise · Your job or studies · Part 2: Eating healthy food is important · Part 3: Healthy food

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Sports and exercise

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • do exercisetập thể dục
  • stay fitgiữ sức khỏe / duy trì vóc dáng
  • work outtập luyện (thể thao)
  • go joggingđi chạy bộ
  • play sportschơi thể thao
  • team sportmôn thể thao đồng đội
  • physical activityhoạt động thể chất
  • relieve stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • build staminatăng sức bền
  • improve concentrationcải thiện khả năng tập trung
  • warm upkhởi động trước khi tập
  • professional athletevận động viên chuyên nghiệp
  • sports facilitycơ sở / địa điểm thể thao
  • keep a healthy lifestyleduy trì lối sống lành mạnh

Câu 1Do you like playing sports?

ANói rõ bạn có thích chơi thể thao không.

RGiải thích lý do (sức khỏe, niềm vui, giảm stress...).

ENêu môn thể thao cụ thể bạn thường chơi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy playing sports in my free time. I think it is a great way to relieve stress after long study sessions. I especially love playing badminton with my classmates on weekends because it is fun and helps me stay fit.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I am not a big fan of competitive sports, but I do like physical activity in general. I prefer light exercise such as going jogging in the morning rather than team games. It helps me keep a healthy lifestyle and clears my mind before a busy day.

Câu 2What kind of exercise do you do?

ANêu loại bài tập hoặc môn thể thao bạn thực hiện.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn loại đó.

ECho biết bạn tập bao lâu hoặc tập ở đâu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually go jogging around the school campus for about thirty minutes every morning. I chose running because it is simple and does not need any special equipment. It helps me build stamina and I feel much more energetic throughout the day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I mainly do exercise at the gym near my dormitory. I focus on strength training and stretching to keep my body in good shape. I try to work out at least three times a week, even when my schedule is very busy.

Câu 3Is exercise important for young people? Why?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn: có hoặc rất quan trọng.

RGiải thích lợi ích (sức khỏe thể chất, tinh thần, học tập...).

ELiên hệ với bản thân hoặc bạn bè của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe that exercise is very important for young people. Regular physical activity helps them stay fit, reduces the risk of disease, and also improves concentration in their studies. In my case, I always feel more focused in class on the days I exercise in the morning.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, exercise is definitely important, especially for medical students like us who sit and study for many hours. Working out regularly can relieve stress and prevent back pain caused by long study sessions. I have seen many of my friends become healthier and happier after they started going to the gym.

Câu 4How often do you exercise?

ANói tần suất bạn tập thể dục mỗi tuần.

RGiải thích vì sao bạn tập nhiều hoặc ít như vậy.

ENêu ví dụ về lịch tập cụ thể của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I try to do exercise about four times a week. I think this frequency is enough to stay fit without taking too much time from my studies. On weekdays I usually go jogging, and on weekends I prefer to play badminton with friends.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I only work out once or twice a week because my medical school timetable is very tight. However, I always try to walk instead of taking the lift as a way to keep some physical activity in my daily routine. I hope to increase my exercise frequency after exams.

Câu 5Where do you usually exercise?

ANêu địa điểm bạn thường tập luyện.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích nơi đó.

EMô tả ngắn về địa điểm hoặc cảm giác khi tập ở đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually exercise in the park near my school. It is a quiet and green place, which makes going jogging very enjoyable. I find that exercising outdoors helps me feel less tired than using a sports facility indoors.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Most of the time I work out at the gym inside our military academy campus. It has good equipment and is very convenient because I do not need to travel far. The sports facility is open early in the morning, so I can exercise before my first lecture.

Câu 6Did you play any sport when you were in high school?

ANêu môn thể thao bạn đã chơi hồi cấp 3.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn môn đó hoặc cảm giác ra sao.

EKể một kỷ niệm hoặc kết quả cụ thể từ thời học phổ thông.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, when I was in high school I was on the school football team. I trained with my teammates three afternoons a week and we competed in local inter-school matches. It was a wonderful experience because I learned about teamwork and how to build stamina.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I used to play volleyball with my classmates during break times at high school. We did not have a formal team, but we played almost every day and it was a lot of fun. Those games helped me relieve stress before important exams.

Câu 7Do you prefer indoor or outdoor sports?

AChọn một trong hai: trong nhà hoặc ngoài trời.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích loại đó hơn.

ENêu ví dụ môn thể thao cụ thể bạn thích.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer outdoor sports because I love the fresh air and natural environment. Activities like going jogging or cycling outside make me feel free and energetic. I think being in nature also helps me relieve stress better than being inside a gym.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually prefer indoor sports like badminton or table tennis. The weather in Vietnam can be very hot or rainy, so playing sports inside is more comfortable and consistent. I can work out at the same time every day without worrying about the weather.

Câu 8Do you think Vietnamese people exercise enough?

AĐưa ra ý kiến: người Việt tập đủ hay chưa.

RĐưa ra lý do hoặc nguyên nhân để giải thích.

ENêu ví dụ về người bạn biết hoặc xu hướng bạn quan sát thấy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think most Vietnamese people do exercise enough. Many people have busy work schedules and spend a lot of time commuting, leaving little time for physical activity. However, I have noticed that more young people are starting to go to the gym or go jogging in recent years.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, the situation is improving but there is still room to grow. Older generations tend to walk or do traditional morning exercises in parks, which is good. But teenagers and students often prefer to stay indoors on their phones instead of playing sports, and that is a concern.

Câu 9Have you ever joined a sports club or team?

ANói bạn đã từng hoặc chưa từng tham gia câu lạc bộ/đội thể thao.

RGiải thích lý do tham gia hoặc lý do không tham gia.

EMô tả ngắn về trải nghiệm đó hoặc dự định trong tương lai.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I joined the swimming club at my university in the first year. We trained twice a week in the university pool and it really helped me build stamina. Being in a club also motivated me to work out regularly because I did not want to miss the group sessions.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have never officially joined a sports club, but I sometimes play in informal groups with my dormitory friends. We often organise team sport games like football or volleyball on Sunday mornings. I enjoy the social side of it and it is a good way to stay fit without a strict schedule.

Câu 10What sport would you like to learn in the future?

ANêu môn thể thao bạn muốn học.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn muốn học môn đó.

ENói về kế hoạch hoặc khi nào bạn có thể bắt đầu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I would love to learn how to swim properly in the future. Right now I can only do the basics, but I want to improve my technique. Swimming is a great full-body workout and it is also a very important survival skill, so I think it is worth the effort.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have always wanted to try rock climbing. I think it is an exciting sport that combines strength and problem-solving at the same time. It looks like a brilliant way to build stamina and mental toughness, and I hope to find a sports facility where I can take beginner classes after I graduate.

Câu 11Does exercise help you with your studies?

ANêu rõ bạn có thấy tập thể dục giúp ích cho việc học không.

RGiải thích cơ chế hoặc lợi ích cụ thể với việc học.

EKể một tình huống cụ thể khi thể dục giúp bạn học tốt hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I definitely think exercise helps my studies a lot. When I go jogging in the morning, I feel much more alert and able to improve concentration during lectures. Last semester, I noticed that my exam results were better in weeks when I managed to work out regularly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe exercise helps indirectly. After a hard day of studying, doing exercise for thirty minutes helps me relieve stress and sleep better at night. Good sleep then means I can absorb information more effectively the next day, so the connection between physical activity and academic performance is real for me.

Câu 12Do you watch sports on TV or online?

ANêu bạn có xem thể thao không và trên nền tảng nào.

RGiải thích môn thể thao bạn xem và lý do yêu thích.

EĐề cập đến trận đấu hoặc vận động viên bạn nhớ nhất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I enjoy watching football matches on TV, especially the international tournaments like the World Cup. Watching professional athletes compete at the highest level is very inspiring and motivates me to play sports more myself. I always stay up late to watch the big games even though I have early morning lectures.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I sometimes watch sports highlights on YouTube rather than full matches because I do not have much free time. I mainly follow Vietnamese team sport events like the SEA Games. Seeing our national athletes win medals always makes me proud and encourages me to keep a healthy lifestyle as well.

Câu 13Do you think sports should be compulsory at university?

AĐưa ra ý kiến: có nên bắt buộc hay không.

RGiải thích lợi ích hoặc lý do bạn đồng ý / không đồng ý.

EDùng ví dụ từ trường của bạn hoặc trải nghiệm cá nhân.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think sports should be compulsory at university, at least to some extent. Many students forget about physical activity when they get busy with academic work, so a required class would ensure everyone gets some do exercise time. At our military academy, we have mandatory physical training and I honestly think it improves both our health and our discipline.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not fully sure that making sports compulsory is the best approach. Some students have health conditions that make certain team sport activities difficult. I think it would be better to offer a variety of sports facilities and encourage students to choose activities they enjoy, because people who choose freely are more likely to stay fit long-term.

Câu 14Who do you usually exercise with?

ANêu bạn thường tập một mình hay cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích tập theo cách đó.

EMô tả ai là người bạn thường tập cùng và bạn thường làm gì.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually work out with my roommate because we have a similar schedule. Having a partner makes it much easier to stay motivated and we encourage each other to do exercise even when we feel tired. We often go jogging together early in the morning before breakfast.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Most of the time I do exercise alone because I find it easier to focus on my own pace and goals. I like putting on music and going jogging without having to wait for anyone. However, on weekends I sometimes join a larger group of classmates for team sport games, which is a nice change.

Câu 15How do you feel after exercising?

AMô tả cảm giác của bạn sau khi tập xong.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn có cảm giác đó.

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể về lần bạn cảm thấy rõ nhất sự khác biệt.

Trả lời mẫu 1

After doing exercise, I always feel refreshed and full of energy. The tiredness during the workout disappears quickly, and I get a sense of achievement that lasts for hours. I think this is because physical activity releases chemicals in the brain that relieve stress and improve mood naturally.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, right after I work out I feel exhausted, but within about fifteen minutes I feel amazing. My mind becomes clearer and I can improve concentration much more easily when I sit down to study. On days when I skip my morning run, I notice I am more irritable and less productive, so the difference is very clear to me.

Part 1 · Your job or studies

  • work long hourslàm việc nhiều giờ liên tục
  • on-the-job experiencekinh nghiệm thực tế trong công việc
  • manage my timequản lý thời gian
  • research paperbài nghiên cứu khoa học
  • patient carechăm sóc bệnh nhân

  • keep up withtheo kịp, cập nhật theo
  • hands-on practicethực hành trực tiếp
  • career goalmục tiêu nghề nghiệp
  • study schedulelịch học, thời khóa biểu học tập

Câu 1What do you do now: study or work?

ANói thẳng bạn đang học hay đang làm việc (hoặc cả hai).

RGiải thích tên khóa học / chuyên ngành hoặc loại công việc bạn đang làm.

EThêm một chi tiết cụ thể về nơi học / làm hoặc thời gian đã bắt đầu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I am currently studying at the Military Medical Academy. I am doing a postgraduate course in internal medicine. I started the programme about a year ago and I find it very interesting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Right now I both study and work at the same time. I am completing a postgraduate course while also working as a doctor at a military hospital. It is quite busy but I enjoy it very much.

Câu 2What do you like about your job or course?

ANêu điều bạn thích nhất về công việc hoặc khóa học.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích điều đó.

EĐưa ra một tình huống cụ thể hoặc ví dụ thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I really like the hands-on practice in my course. It helps me develop real clinical skills that I can use immediately with patients. For example, last month I practised emergency procedures and felt much more confident afterwards.

Trả lời mẫu 2

What I like most about my course is the opportunity to do medical research. I enjoy reading and writing research papers because they help me understand diseases more deeply. Last semester I wrote a paper on hypertension management and it was very rewarding.

Câu 3Is it easy or difficult for you? Why?

ANói rõ khóa học / công việc dễ hay khó với bạn.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao dễ hoặc khó.

ENêu một khía cạnh cụ thể gây khó khăn hoặc khiến bạn thấy dễ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Honestly, my course is quite challenging for me. There is a lot of material to read and I also have to work long hours at the hospital. For example, studying advanced pharmacology while working night shifts is really exhausting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think my studies are moderately difficult. Some subjects like anatomy are hard, but I find patient care quite natural because I have some experience. The most difficult part is writing research papers in a short time.

Câu 4How many hours a day do you usually study?

ANêu số giờ bạn thường học mỗi ngày.

RGiải thích lý do bạn học nhiều hay ít như vậy.

EMô tả thời điểm trong ngày bạn thường học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually study for about three or four hours every day. I need this time because my postgraduate course has a lot of new content each week. I often study in the evening after my hospital duties are finished.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On weekdays I can only study for about two hours because I work long hours at the clinic. However, on weekends I try to study five or six hours to keep up with the syllabus. I think managing my time well is very important.

Câu 5Do you prefer studying alone or with other people?

ANói bạn thích học một mình hay học nhóm.

RGiải thích lý do cho sở thích đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về khi nào bạn học một mình hoặc học nhóm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer studying alone because I can concentrate better without distractions. I can follow my own study schedule and take breaks when I need them. For difficult topics like pathophysiology, I find it easier to read quietly and make my own notes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy studying with classmates because we can discuss challenging subjects together. When one person explains a concept, everyone understands it more quickly. For example, we often review clinical cases as a group before exams and it is very helpful.

Câu 6What subject or part of your course do you find most interesting?

ANêu môn học hoặc phần nào trong khóa học bạn thích nhất.

RGiải thích điều gì khiến môn đó thú vị với bạn.

EKể một điều cụ thể bạn đã học hoặc làm trong môn đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I find clinical skills training the most interesting part of my course. I love practising real procedures because it makes learning feel meaningful. Recently I practised suturing techniques in the simulation lab and it was very exciting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The most interesting subject for me is infectious diseases. I like it because the content is very relevant to daily patient care at the hospital. During the COVID-19 period I could see how important this knowledge was in real situations.

Câu 7What is the most difficult part of your studies or job?

ANêu phần khó nhất trong việc học hoặc công việc của bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao phần đó lại khó.

EMô tả cách bạn đang cố gắng vượt qua khó khăn đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

The most difficult part of my studies is writing research papers in English. I am not very confident with academic writing, so it takes me a long time. I am trying to improve by reading published medical articles every week.

Trả lời mẫu 2

For me, the hardest part of my job is learning to manage my time between clinical duties and studying. Some days I am very tired after a long shift and it is hard to open a textbook. I try to plan my study schedule carefully each week to balance everything.

Câu 8Do you think your studies will help your future career? How?

ANói rõ bạn nghĩ việc học có giúp ích cho sự nghiệp tương lai không.

RGiải thích cách mà việc học sẽ giúp bạn.

ENêu một kỹ năng hoặc kiến thức cụ thể bạn sẽ áp dụng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe my postgraduate course will help my future career. It gives me advanced knowledge and clinical skills that are essential for a specialist doctor. For example, studying cardiology in depth will help me treat heart patients more effectively.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think my studies will definitely support my career goals. The medical training I receive now will make me a more confident and competent doctor. In particular, the research skills I am developing will help me specialize in a field I am passionate about.

Câu 9How do you feel about the workload in your course or job?

ANêu cảm nhận của bạn về khối lượng công việc hoặc học tập.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn cảm thấy như vậy.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về một tuần bận rộn hoặc nhẹ nhàng điển hình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I feel that the workload in my course is quite heavy. I have to work long hours at the hospital and then study in the evenings. Last week, for example, I had two assignments, a ward round, and a seminar all in the same week.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the workload is manageable if I manage my time well. I use a study schedule to plan each week in advance so I do not feel overwhelmed. There are busy weeks before exams, but overall I feel I can handle the pressure.

Câu 10Have you ever had to deal with a stressful situation at work or in your studies?

AXác nhận bạn đã từng gặp tình huống căng thẳng và mô tả ngắn gọn tình huống đó.

RGiải thích điều gì khiến tình huống đó căng thẳng.

EKể cách bạn đã xử lý hoặc vượt qua tình huống đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I once had to handle a medical emergency in the middle of my night shift while I was also preparing for an important exam. It was very stressful because I had to focus on patient care and study at the same time. In the end I asked a colleague to help me and I got through both tasks successfully.

Trả lời mẫu 2

A very stressful moment was when I had to present a research paper in front of professors for the first time. I was nervous because my English was not very strong then. I prepared very carefully for two weeks and the presentation went better than I expected.

Câu 11What do you want to specialize in after you finish your course?

ANêu chuyên ngành bạn muốn theo đuổi sau khóa học.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn chuyên ngành đó.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ hoặc trải nghiệm đã ảnh hưởng đến lựa chọn của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I want to specialize in cardiology after I finish my course. I am very interested in heart diseases because they are one of the leading causes of death in Vietnam. During my clinical rotations I treated several heart patients and found the work very meaningful.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My career goal is to become a specialist in emergency medicine. I enjoy working under pressure and making quick decisions. I had on-the-job experience in the emergency department last year and it confirmed that this is the right path for me.

Câu 12Do you enjoy reading or studying new things related to your field?

ANói bạn có thích tự đọc và tìm hiểu thêm về chuyên ngành không.

RGiải thích điều gì thúc đẩy bạn đọc thêm hoặc không.

ENêu một chủ đề hoặc tài liệu cụ thể bạn đã tự tìm hiểu gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I genuinely enjoy reading new things about medicine. I try to keep up with the latest medical journals and guidelines every month. Recently I read a very interesting article about new treatments for diabetes and it changed how I advise my patients.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I sometimes find it hard to read extra materials because I am already very busy. However, I try to read at least one new research paper per week. I focus on topics related to patient care so that the reading is immediately useful in my daily work.

Câu 13How do you usually prepare for exams or important tasks at work?

AMô tả cách bạn thường chuẩn bị cho kỳ thi hoặc nhiệm vụ quan trọng.

RGiải thích tại sao phương pháp đó hiệu quả với bạn.

EKể điều bạn làm trong giai đoạn chuẩn bị cuối (ví dụ tuần trước thi).

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually start preparing for exams at least two weeks in advance. I make a detailed study schedule and divide the material into small sections for each day. In the final week I review my notes and practise old exam questions to feel more confident.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Before an important exam I focus on reviewing clinical cases because they cover theory and practice together. I also do hands-on practice in the skills lab when possible. I find that active practice helps me remember information much better than just reading.

Câu 14Is there anything about your studies or job that you would like to change?

ANêu một điều bạn muốn thay đổi trong việc học hoặc công việc.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn muốn thay đổi điều đó.

EĐề xuất một cách cụ thể mà sự thay đổi đó có thể xảy ra.

Trả lời mẫu 1

If I could change one thing, I would like to have more time for hands-on practice in the hospital. Currently a lot of our time is spent in lectures, but I think practical experience teaches us more. I believe more clinical hours would help us develop stronger clinical skills.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would like to have a lighter study schedule during exam periods. The pressure of work long hours and studying at the same time is very tiring. I think if the hospital gave doctors studying for a postgraduate course a reduced shift schedule, the quality of their work and study would improve.

Câu 15Do you get on well with your colleagues or classmates?

ANói bạn có quan hệ tốt với đồng nghiệp / bạn học không.

RGiải thích điều gì giúp mối quan hệ đó tốt hoặc điều gì gây khó khăn.

EKể một ví dụ cụ thể về sự hợp tác hoặc hỗ trợ giữa các bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I get on very well with my classmates. We often help each other with difficult topics and share useful notes before exams. For example, one classmate who is strong in pharmacology always explains drug interactions to the rest of the group and it helps everyone.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have a good relationship with most of my colleagues at the hospital. We support each other during busy shifts and share on-the-job experience regularly. However, sometimes it is hard to find time to socialize because we all work long hours and have medical training commitments outside of work.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Eating healthy food is important

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • healthy foodthực phẩm lành mạnh
  • balanced dietchế độ ăn cân bằng
  • nutritious mealsbữa ăn giàu dinh dưỡng
  • boost the immune systemtăng cường hệ miễn dịch
  • prevent illnessphòng ngừa bệnh tật
  • chronic diseasebệnh mãn tính
  • energy levelsmức năng lượng trong cơ thể
  • stay focusedduy trì sự tập trung
  • mental healthsức khỏe tâm thần
  • reduce stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • fresh vegetables and fruitrau và trái cây tươi
  • junk foodđồ ăn vặt không có lợi cho sức khỏe
  • proper nutritiondinh dưỡng đúng đắn
  • long-term healthsức khỏe lâu dài

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I would like to talk about why healthy food is so important in our daily lives. In my opinion, what we eat has a huge effect on how we feel and how well we function every day. First of all, eating nutritious meals is very good for our health. When we follow a balanced diet with fresh vegetables and fruit, our bodies get the vitamins and minerals they need to work properly. For example, people who eat well tend to have stronger bodies and get sick less often. Another reason is that healthy food can help us prevent illness. A good diet can boost the immune system and lower the risk of serious chronic disease such as diabetes or heart disease. For instance, doctors always advise patients to eat less junk food and more proper nutrition to stay healthy. In addition, eating well gives us better energy levels. When I have a good breakfast with fruit and rice, I feel more active and can stay focused during long study sessions at the academy. On top of that, I believe healthy food also supports our mental health. Studies show that nutritious meals can reduce stress and improve our mood, which is very helpful for students under pressure. To sum up, eating healthy food benefits our bodies, prevents disease, gives us energy, and even helps our minds. I think everyone should pay more attention to long-term health by making better food choices every day.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Healthy food (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • healthy foodthức ăn lành mạnh, tốt cho sức khỏe
  • fast foodđồ ăn nhanh (hamburger, pizza, v.v.)
  • home-cooked mealsbữa ăn nấu tại nhà
  • balanced dietchế độ ăn cân bằng đủ chất
  • eating habitsthói quen ăn uống
  • processed foodthực phẩm chế biến sẵn
  • fresh vegetablesrau củ tươi
  • nutritional valuegiá trị dinh dưỡng
  • junk foodđồ ăn vặt ít dinh dưỡng
  • meal planninglập kế hoạch bữa ăn
  • organic producenông sản hữu cơ
  • calorie intakelượng calo nạp vào cơ thể
  • dietary habitsthói quen chế độ ăn uống
  • food awarenessnhận thức về thực phẩm

Câu 1Why do some people prefer fast food?

ANêu rõ lý do chính khiến người ta chọn đồ ăn nhanh (tiện lợi, giá rẻ).

RGiải thích vì sao yếu tố đó quan trọng với cuộc sống hiện đại.

EĐưa ví dụ cụ thể về nhóm người hay chọn đồ ăn nhanh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people prefer fast food mainly because it saves a lot of time. In modern life, people are very busy with work and study, so they do not have enough time to cook home-cooked meals. For example, office workers often grab a burger or a sandwich during their lunch break because it is quick and convenient.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, fast food is popular because it is affordable and easy to find. Not everyone has a high income, so cheap junk food options seem attractive. For instance, a meal at a fast food restaurant can cost much less than buying fresh ingredients to cook at home.

Câu 2Is healthy food expensive in your country?

AĐưa ra quan điểm rõ ràng: thức ăn lành mạnh có đắt không ở Việt Nam.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân (giá nông sản, chi phí hữu cơ, v.v.).

ESo sánh giá cụ thể hoặc nêu ví dụ thực tế ở chợ hay siêu thị.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think healthy food is not always expensive in Vietnam. Basic fresh vegetables and fruits from local markets are quite affordable for most families. However, organic produce and imported health foods can be much more costly, which makes them difficult for lower-income people to buy regularly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I believe healthy food in Vietnam can be expensive depending on the type. Traditional foods like rice, tofu, and seasonal vegetables have good nutritional value and are cheap. But modern health products such as organic produce or diet supplements cost a lot more, so not everyone can afford them.

Câu 3How can people develop healthy eating habits?

ANêu một hoặc hai cách cụ thể để xây dựng thói quen ăn uống lành mạnh.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó hiệu quả.

ECho ví dụ về hành động cụ thể trong cuộc sống hàng ngày.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the best way to develop healthy eating habits is to start with small changes. Instead of cutting out all junk food at once, people can gradually add more fresh vegetables and fruits to their meals. For example, someone could begin by replacing a sugary snack with a piece of fruit every afternoon.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, meal planning is a very effective way to build better dietary habits. When people plan their meals in advance, they are less likely to rely on fast food out of hunger or convenience. For instance, preparing lunch boxes at home the night before can help save both time and money while keeping the diet healthy.

Câu 4Do you think schools should teach children about healthy eating?

AĐồng ý hay không đồng ý với việc trường học dạy về ăn uống lành mạnh.

RGiải thích lợi ích hoặc lý do của quan điểm đó.

ENêu ví dụ về chương trình hoặc hoạt động cụ thể ở trường.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe schools should teach children about healthy food. Children form their eating habits at a young age, so learning early can have a lasting impact on their health. For example, schools could include lessons about balanced diet or even organise healthy cooking workshops for students.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that schools play an important role in promoting food awareness among children. Many parents are too busy to explain nutritional value to their kids at home. If schools include healthy eating habits in the curriculum, children can learn to make better food choices from an early age.

Câu 5How does fast food affect people's health?

ANêu tác động tiêu cực chính của đồ ăn nhanh lên sức khỏe.

RGiải thích cơ chế hoặc lý do tại sao nó gây hại.

EĐưa ví dụ về bệnh hoặc vấn đề sức khỏe liên quan.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think eating too much fast food is very harmful to health because it is high in fat, salt, and sugar. These ingredients can lead to serious problems like obesity and heart disease over time. For example, studies show that people who eat junk food regularly have a higher risk of developing type 2 diabetes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, fast food affects health mainly by increasing calorie intake without providing enough nutrients. The lack of fresh vegetables and fibre in these meals can cause digestive problems and weight gain. I have seen many young people gain weight quickly because they rely on processed food almost every day.

Câu 6Why do you think obesity is becoming more common in many countries?

ANêu nguyên nhân chính dẫn đến tình trạng béo phì gia tăng.

RGiải thích mối liên hệ giữa lối sống hiện đại và béo phì.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về thay đổi trong thói quen ăn uống hoặc sinh hoạt.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think obesity is increasing mainly because people are eating more processed food and junk food than before. Modern lifestyles are very sedentary, and people no longer follow a balanced diet like previous generations. For example, many families now eat takeaway food several times a week instead of preparing home-cooked meals.

Trả lời mẫu 2
