In my view, the rise in obesity is closely linked to changes in eating habits and a lack of food awareness. People often choose convenience over nutrition, so they consume more fast food with high calorie intake. For instance, the growth of food delivery apps has made it even easier to order unhealthy meals without leaving home.

Câu 7Should the government do more to encourage people to eat healthily?

ANêu rõ ý kiến: chính phủ có nên can thiệp vào thói quen ăn uống không.

RGiải thích tại sao vai trò của chính phủ là cần thiết hoặc không cần thiết.

EĐề xuất chính sách cụ thể hoặc nêu ví dụ từ nước khác.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think the government should take more action to help people eat healthily. Many people lack food awareness and do not understand the importance of a balanced diet. For example, the government could tax junk food and processed food heavily while reducing the price of fresh vegetables and fruits.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I partially agree that the government should help, but I also think personal responsibility is important. Campaigns that raise food awareness and provide nutrition education can be very useful. For instance, some countries have introduced food labels showing nutritional value clearly on packaging, which helps people make better choices.

Câu 8How important is it to have a balanced diet?

AKhẳng định tầm quan trọng của chế độ ăn cân bằng với sức khỏe.

RGiải thích tại sao thiếu cân bằng dinh dưỡng gây ra vấn đề.

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể về hậu quả khi không ăn đủ chất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think having a balanced diet is extremely important for both physical and mental health. When people do not get enough vitamins and minerals, their immune system becomes weak and they get sick more easily. For example, medical students who skip meals or rely on junk food during exams often feel tired and find it hard to concentrate.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, a balanced diet is the foundation of good health and should not be ignored. Without proper nutritional value from a variety of foods, the body cannot function well in the long term. For instance, a diet lacking in fresh vegetables can lead to vitamin deficiencies and problems like anaemia or poor skin health.

Câu 9Do you think people today are more health-conscious than in the past?

AĐưa ra quan điểm: người hiện đại có quan tâm đến sức khỏe hơn trước không.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân dẫn đến sự thay đổi hoặc thiếu thay đổi đó.

EDẫn chứng cụ thể về xu hướng hoặc hành vi thay đổi trong xã hội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think people today are generally more health-conscious than previous generations. There is much more information available about healthy food, balanced diet, and eating habits through the internet and social media. For example, many young people now follow fitness influencers online and are interested in tracking their calorie intake and choosing organic produce.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not sure people are truly healthier in their choices today. While food awareness has increased, the consumption of fast food and processed food has also risen significantly. For instance, despite knowing the risks, many people still choose convenience over nutrition because of their busy modern lifestyles.

Câu 10How can families encourage children to eat more vegetables and fruits?

AĐề xuất cách cụ thể để gia đình khuyến khích trẻ ăn nhiều rau và trái cây hơn.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó có tác dụng với trẻ em.

ENêu ví dụ về hoạt động hoặc thói quen cụ thể trong gia đình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the best way for families to encourage children to eat fresh vegetables and fruits is to make meals fun and colourful. Children are more likely to eat healthy food when it looks attractive and is presented in a creative way. For example, parents can cut fruits into fun shapes or let children choose which vegetables to include in a salad.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, children copy what their parents do, so adults should model good eating habits at home. If parents regularly eat home-cooked meals with plenty of fresh vegetables, children will naturally follow. For instance, involving children in cooking or grocery shopping can make them more curious about trying different healthy food options.

Câu 11Why do some people find it difficult to maintain a healthy diet?

ANêu lý do khiến nhiều người khó duy trì chế độ ăn lành mạnh lâu dài.

RGiải thích yếu tố nào trong cuộc sống hiện đại cản trở điều đó.

ECho ví dụ về tình huống thực tế khiến người ta bỏ chế độ ăn lành mạnh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people struggle to maintain a healthy diet because of their busy schedules and lack of time for meal planning. When people are tired after work, they tend to choose fast food or processed food for convenience instead of cooking home-cooked meals. For example, a doctor working a night shift may simply order takeaway rather than prepare a balanced diet at home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, the main reason people cannot keep healthy eating habits is the high cost and limited availability of fresh vegetables and organic produce in some areas. Junk food is cheap, easy to find, and heavily advertised, which makes it hard to resist. For instance, living in a neighbourhood with no fresh market nearby makes it very difficult to eat healthily every day.

Câu 12Do you think advertising has a big influence on people's food choices?

ANêu ý kiến về mức độ ảnh hưởng của quảng cáo đến lựa chọn thực phẩm.

RGiải thích cơ chế tác động của quảng cáo lên hành vi người tiêu dùng.

EĐưa ví dụ về loại quảng cáo cụ thể và đối tượng bị ảnh hưởng nhiều nhất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe advertising has a huge influence on eating habits, especially among children and teenagers. Colourful and exciting ads for junk food and fast food make these products look very appealing. For example, many children beg their parents to buy snacks they see advertised on television, even though these foods have very low nutritional value.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that advertising shapes people's food awareness and purchasing decisions significantly. However, I also think that nowadays, more brands are promoting healthy food and balanced diet options online. For instance, campaigns showing the benefits of organic produce or home-cooked meals on social media are slowly changing how younger consumers think about food.

Câu 13How do you think traditional diets compare to modern diets in terms of health?

ASo sánh chế độ ăn truyền thống với chế độ ăn hiện đại về mặt sức khỏe.

RGiải thích điểm mạnh của chế độ ăn truyền thống hoặc điểm yếu của chế độ ăn hiện đại.

EDẫn ví dụ cụ thể từ ẩm thực Việt Nam hoặc so sánh với thực phẩm phương Tây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think traditional diets are generally healthier than modern diets because they rely more on fresh vegetables, whole grains, and home-cooked meals. In Vietnam, the traditional diet is rich in vegetables, herbs, and lean protein, which provides excellent nutritional value without too much fat. In contrast, the modern diet often includes a lot of processed food and fast food that is high in calories but low in nutrients.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Comparing the two, I believe traditional diets support a more natural balanced diet because they use fresh, seasonal ingredients. Modern diets, on the other hand, tend to rely heavily on junk food and convenience meals with poor nutritional value. For example, a traditional Vietnamese meal with rice, steamed fish, and vegetables is far more nutritious than a fast food burger with fries.

Câu 14Should workplaces provide healthy food options for their employees?

ANêu ý kiến về việc nơi làm việc có nên cung cấp thức ăn lành mạnh không.

RGiải thích lợi ích cho cả nhân viên và công ty khi làm điều đó.

ECho ví dụ về chính sách thực tế hoặc loại thực phẩm có thể cung cấp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think workplaces should definitely offer healthy food options for employees. When workers have access to nutritious meals at work, their energy and productivity improve significantly. For example, a hospital canteen that serves balanced diet meals with fresh vegetables and lean protein helps doctors and nurses stay focused during long shifts.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that offering healthy food at work is a good investment for employers. Poor eating habits among staff can lead to illness and absenteeism, which costs the company money. For instance, providing subsidised meals with good nutritional value instead of vending machines full of junk food can improve both employee health and morale.

Câu 15How can people with a busy lifestyle still manage to eat healthily?

AGợi ý cách thực tế để người bận rộn vẫn duy trì được chế độ ăn lành mạnh.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó phù hợp với người có ít thời gian.

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể về thói quen hoặc công cụ hỗ trợ trong cuộc sống hàng ngày.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think meal planning at the weekend is the most practical way for busy people to keep healthy eating habits during the week. By preparing home-cooked meals in advance and storing them in the fridge, people can avoid the temptation of fast food when they are tired. For example, cooking a large batch of soup or rice with vegetables on Sunday can provide several healthy lunches for the whole week.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, busy people can still eat healthily by making smarter choices when they have to eat out. Instead of ordering junk food, they can look for restaurants that offer fresh vegetables, grilled proteins, or options with better nutritional value. For instance, choosing a rice dish with vegetables at a local restaurant is a much healthier option than a burger, and it does not take any extra time to prepare.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 6

Part 1: Transport · The weather · Part 2: Mobile phones are useful in daily life · Part 3: Mobile phones

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Transport

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • public transportphương tiện giao thông công cộng
  • motorbikexe máy
  • commuteđi lại hàng ngày giữa nhà và nơi làm/học
  • rush hourgiờ cao điểm
  • traffic jamtắc đường
  • bus routetuyến xe buýt
  • ride-hailing appứng dụng gọi xe (Grab, Bee...)
  • on footđi bộ
  • convenienttiện lợi
  • reliableđáng tin cậy, ổn định
  • cyclingđi xe đạp
  • overcrowdedđông đúc, quá tải
  • long-distance traveldi chuyển đường dài
  • fuel costchi phí xăng dầu

Câu 1How do you usually travel to work or school?

ANêu phương tiện bạn dùng hàng ngày.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn phương tiện đó.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về thời gian hoặc tình huống thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually commute to school by motorbike. It is the most common way to travel in my city because it is fast and flexible. For example, even during rush hour I can use smaller roads to avoid traffic jams and arrive on time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I normally travel to the hospital on a ride-hailing app like Grab. I prefer this because I do not have to worry about parking or fuel cost. Last week, for instance, the driver took a shortcut and I arrived ten minutes earlier than usual.

Câu 2What kind of transport do you like best?

ANêu loại phương tiện yêu thích của bạn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích nó.

EKể một tình huống hoặc chuyến đi minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite kind of transport is the motorbike. I like it because it gives me a lot of freedom and I can go anywhere without waiting. Last month I rode to a clinic outside the city and the trip was very smooth and enjoyable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I like travelling by train the best, especially for long-distance travel. Trains are reliable and I can study or rest during the journey. When I visited my family in the south last year, I took the night train and arrived feeling very refreshed.

Câu 3Is public transport convenient in your area?

ACho biết giao thông công cộng ở khu vực bạn có tiện lợi không.

RGiải thích vì sao tiện lợi hoặc không tiện lợi.

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể từ kinh nghiệm của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think public transport is becoming more convenient in my area. The city has added several new bus routes recently, so more places are connected. However, during rush hour the buses can be overcrowded, which makes the journey uncomfortable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, public transport near my school is not very convenient yet. There are only a few bus routes and they do not run very often, so I usually miss my connection. Most of my classmates prefer to use a ride-hailing app or their own motorbike for this reason.

Câu 4How long does it take you to get to school or work every day?

ANêu thời gian di chuyển của bạn mỗi ngày.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân mất nhiều hay ít thời gian.

EMô tả một ngày cụ thể để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

It usually takes me about thirty minutes to commute to the academy. The distance is not very far, but traffic jams in the morning can slow me down a lot. On days when I leave early, before rush hour, I can arrive in only twenty minutes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My daily commute takes around forty-five minutes by bus. The bus route passes through the city centre, which is always busy. One morning last week the bus was stuck in a traffic jam for twenty minutes and I was almost late for my lecture.

Câu 5Do you prefer travelling alone or with other people?

ANói rõ bạn thích đi một mình hay đi cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do cho sự lựa chọn đó.

EKể một chuyến đi để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer travelling alone, especially during my daily commute. When I am alone I can listen to medical podcasts or review my notes on the motorbike. It helps me use the travel time productively before a busy shift at the hospital.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy travelling with friends when we go on longer trips. Sharing a car or taking the train together makes long-distance travel much more fun. Last semester a group of us took the overnight train to a conference and we studied and chatted the whole way.

Câu 6Have you ever used a ride-hailing app to get around? What did you think of it?

ANói bạn đã dùng ứng dụng gọi xe chưa và cảm nhận của bạn.

RGiải thích điều bạn thích hoặc không thích.

EKể một lần cụ thể bạn sử dụng dịch vụ đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I use a ride-hailing app quite often when I am tired after a night shift. I find it very convenient because I just tap a few buttons and the driver comes to me. Last week a driver even helped me carry my heavy medical bag, which was very kind.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have tried a ride-hailing app a few times but I do not use it regularly because the fuel cost for passengers can be high during rush hour. The first time I used it, the price was nearly double the normal rate because of surge pricing. Now I only use it when I have no other option.

Câu 7Do you ever travel by bicycle? Why or why not?

ACho biết bạn có đi xe đạp không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn đi hoặc không đi.

ENêu ví dụ hoặc tình huống thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not usually go cycling to school because the roads near my area are very busy and not safe for cyclists. I tried it once on a weekend when the streets were quiet and it was actually very enjoyable. If there were more cycling lanes I would definitely do it more often.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, I enjoy cycling on weekends as a form of exercise. It is a great way to avoid traffic jams and also keep fit at the same time. Last Sunday I cycled to a park near the hospital and it took only fifteen minutes, much faster than driving.

Câu 8Is traffic a problem in the city where you live?

ANêu ý kiến về tình trạng giao thông ở thành phố bạn.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân gây ra vấn đề đó.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về một tình huống giao thông bạn đã trải qua.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, traffic jams are a serious problem in my city, especially in the morning. There are too many motorbikes and cars on the roads at the same time during rush hour. Last Monday it took me over an hour just to travel five kilometres to the hospital.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Traffic is not too bad in the district where I live, but the city centre can be very overcrowded at peak times. The main problem is that public transport is still limited, so everyone drives their own motorbike. I think better bus services would help reduce the congestion significantly.

Câu 9Have you ever had a bad experience with transport? What happened?

AKể lại một trải nghiệm tồi tệ liên quan đến phương tiện đi lại.

RGiải thích điều gì đã gây ra vấn đề đó.

EMô tả kết quả hoặc hậu quả của sự việc đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I once had a very stressful commute when my motorbike broke down on the way to an important exam. I was stuck on a busy road during rush hour and could not find a mechanic nearby. I had to use a ride-hailing app at the last minute and barely made it to the exam room on time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I remember a bad experience on public transport last year when the bus I was on got stuck in a major traffic jam for almost two hours. The bus was overcrowded and the air conditioning was broken, so it was very hot and uncomfortable. After that day I decided to always leave home much earlier when I take the bus.

Câu 10Would you like to own a car in the future? Why or why not?

ANêu ý kiến về việc bạn có muốn sở hữu xe hơi không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn muốn hoặc không muốn.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về khi nào xe hơi sẽ hữu ích hoặc không cần thiết.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would love to own a car in the future. As a doctor, I sometimes need to travel to different hospitals or clinics at short notice, and a car would make that much easier. It would also be very useful for long-distance travel when I visit my family in another province.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I am not sure I need a car because fuel cost and parking fees in the city are very high. A motorbike or ride-hailing app already meets most of my daily transport needs. I think I would rather save that money for other things at this stage of my life.

Câu 11How do you think transport in Vietnam will change in the next ten years?

ADự đoán những thay đổi về giao thông ở Việt Nam trong tương lai.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn nghĩ những thay đổi đó sẽ xảy ra.

ENêu một ví dụ cụ thể về loại phương tiện hoặc cơ sở hạ tầng mới.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think public transport will improve a lot in Vietnam over the next ten years. Many cities are already building metro lines and adding new bus routes to reduce traffic jams. Once people have a reliable and affordable alternative, fewer of them will use personal motorbikes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe electric vehicles will become very popular in Vietnam within the next decade. As fuel cost rises and people become more aware of pollution, many riders will switch to electric motorbikes or cars. I can already see more electric scooters on the road compared to just two or three years ago.

Câu 12Do you walk anywhere regularly? Where do you usually walk to?

ANói bạn có thường xuyên đi bộ không và đi đến đâu.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn đi bộ đến những nơi đó.

EMô tả một lần đi bộ gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I walk to the canteen on foot every day during my lunch break. It is only about five minutes from the ward and I enjoy the short walk to clear my head between patients. Walking a little every day also helps me stay active despite my busy schedule.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not walk very much because most places I need to go are too far to travel on foot. However, I sometimes walk to a small coffee shop near the dormitory in the evening to relax after studying. It takes about ten minutes and I find the fresh air very refreshing after a long day.

Câu 13Do you think it is important to use environmentally friendly transport? Why?

ANêu quan điểm về việc sử dụng phương tiện thân thiện với môi trường.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó quan trọng hoặc không quan trọng với bạn.

ECho ví dụ về loại phương tiện xanh mà bạn biết hoặc đã thử.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think using environmentally friendly transport is very important, especially in big cities. Air pollution from motorbikes and cars causes many respiratory health problems, which I see every day as a medical student. Simple choices like cycling or using public transport instead of private vehicles can make a real difference.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree it is important, but I also think it is not always practical in Vietnam right now. Public transport is not always reliable or available everywhere, so many people have no choice but to use a motorbike. I hope that as infrastructure improves, more people will be able to make greener transport choices.

Câu 14How do you feel about travelling during rush hour?

AMô tả cảm xúc của bạn khi di chuyển vào giờ cao điểm.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn cảm thấy như vậy.

EKể một lần cụ thể bạn phải đi vào giờ cao điểm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I find travelling during rush hour very stressful and tiring. The roads are extremely overcrowded and traffic jams can last for a very long time, which wastes a lot of energy before I even start work. Whenever possible, I try to leave home very early to avoid the worst of the congestion.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have got used to rush hour after commuting for several years, but I still do not enjoy it. The noise and heat from so many motorbikes around me make the commute quite unpleasant. I usually listen to an audiobook on my earphone to stay calm and make the time feel more useful.

Câu 15If you could choose any form of transport for a holiday trip, what would you choose?

ANêu phương tiện bạn sẽ chọn cho một chuyến đi nghỉ.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn phương tiện đó.

EMô tả chuyến đi lý tưởng bạn muốn thực hiện.

Trả lời mẫu 1

For a holiday trip I would choose to travel by train. It is a reliable and comfortable option for long-distance travel, and I can enjoy the scenery outside the window. I have always wanted to take the Reunification Express from Hanoi to Ho Chi Minh City and stop at different cities along the way.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would choose to travel by motorbike for a holiday because it gives me total freedom to explore at my own pace. I could stop whenever I see something interesting without worrying about a fixed schedule or fuel cost becoming too high. My dream is to ride on foot through the mountains of Ha Giang with some close friends.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · The weather

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • tropical climatekhí hậu nhiệt đới
  • rainy seasonmùa mưa
  • dry seasonmùa khô
  • humidẩm ướt
  • mild weatherthời tiết ôn hòa
  • thunderstormcơn giông bão
  • sunny dayngày nắng đẹp
  • cool breezelàn gió mát
  • heat waveđợt nắng nóng
  • heavy rainmưa lớn
  • foggy morningbuổi sáng sương mù
  • weather forecastdự báo thời tiết
  • stay indoorsở trong nhà
  • outdoor activitieshoạt động ngoài trời

Câu 1What is the weather like in your country?

ANêu loại khí hậu chính của Việt Nam (nhiệt đới, nóng ẩm).

RGiải thích đặc điểm thời tiết theo mùa hoặc theo vùng miền.

EĐưa ra ví dụ cụ thể về thời tiết ở nơi bạn sống.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Vietnam has a tropical climate with high temperatures and humidity for most of the year. The country is divided into a rainy season and a dry season, which makes the weather quite different throughout the year. In the south where I live, it is usually hot and humid from March to November. During the rainy season, we often have heavy rain in the afternoons. I think the weather here is quite predictable once you know the seasons.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My country has a tropical climate that varies a lot between the north and the south. In Hanoi, there are four seasons, but in Ho Chi Minh City, there are only two: a rainy season and a dry season. The summers can be very hot, sometimes reaching over 38 degrees Celsius. I grew up in the north, so I experienced cold winters and hot summers. The variety of weather across Vietnam is actually one of the things I find interesting about living here.

Câu 2What kind of weather do you like most?

ANêu loại thời tiết bạn yêu thích nhất (nắng, mát, mưa nhẹ...).

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích loại thời tiết đó (cảm giác, sức khỏe, hoạt động...).

EKể điều bạn thường làm hoặc cảm thấy khi có loại thời tiết đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I like mild weather the most, when it is cool but not too cold. This kind of weather makes me feel energetic and ready to work. On days like that, I enjoy going for a walk outside the hospital during my break. A gentle cool breeze makes everything feel more comfortable. I think mild weather is perfect for both working and relaxing.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My favourite weather is a sunny day with a light cool breeze. I find that sunshine improves my mood and helps me focus better at work. When the weather is nice, I feel more motivated to do outdoor activities like jogging or cycling in the morning. A good sunny day after a long period of heavy rain feels especially refreshing. I always look forward to those kinds of mornings before my hospital shift.

Câu 3Does the weather affect your mood?

ATrả lời thẳng: có hoặc không, thời tiết ảnh hưởng đến tâm trạng bạn.

RGiải thích loại thời tiết nào ảnh hưởng và ảnh hưởng thế nào.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về tình huống thực tế trong cuộc sống/công việc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, the weather definitely affects my mood. When there is a sunny day, I feel cheerful and productive, which helps me a lot during long hospital shifts. On the other hand, a thunderstorm or several grey days in a row make me feel tired and less motivated. I noticed this pattern especially during my internship when I had to work night shifts. I try to compensate by keeping my workspace bright on rainy days.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, the weather does not affect my mood very much. I have trained myself to stay focused whether it is a sunny day or a day with heavy rain. As a medical student, I need to maintain a stable mindset regardless of outside conditions. However, I do find that very hot weather during a heat wave makes it harder to concentrate when studying. In those cases, I prefer to stay indoors with the air conditioning on.

Câu 4Do you usually check the weather forecast before going out?

ANêu thói quen của bạn: có hay không kiểm tra dự báo thời tiết.

RGiải thích lý do vì sao bạn có hoặc không có thói quen đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về lần bạn kiểm tra hoặc không kiểm tra và kết quả ra sao.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always check the weather forecast before leaving the house, especially on my days off. Living in a tropical climate means the weather can change very quickly, and I do not want to be caught in heavy rain without an umbrella. I usually use a weather app on my phone every morning. Once I ignored the forecast and got completely soaked on my way to the library. Since then, I have never skipped checking it.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not check the weather forecast very often. I usually just look out the window to decide what to wear or whether to bring an umbrella. During the rainy season, I simply carry an umbrella every day as a habit, so I do not need to check. My colleagues at the hospital often laugh at me because I always have an umbrella ready. I think it is a practical solution for living in a tropical climate.

Câu 5Has the weather ever stopped you from doing something you planned?

ANêu trực tiếp: có, thời tiết đã từng làm bạn hủy hoặc thay đổi kế hoạch.

RGiải thích loại thời tiết nào gây ra điều đó và hoàn cảnh như thế nào.

EKể một tình huống cụ thể khi điều đó xảy ra.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, the weather has definitely stopped me from doing things I planned. Last year, I planned a weekend trip to the beach with my classmates, but a thunderstorm hit our area and we had to cancel. It was very disappointing because we had been looking forward to the trip for weeks. We ended up staying indoors and watching films together instead. I learned to always check the extended weather forecast when planning trips.

Trả lời mẫu 2

There was one time when a sudden heat wave forced me to change my plan. I had arranged to join an outdoor sports event at our university, but the temperature was so extreme that the organisers cancelled it for safety reasons. I was quite disappointed because I had been preparing for it. However, I understand that health always comes first, especially in extreme weather. Now I always have a backup plan for activities that depend on good weather.

Câu 6Do you prefer hot or cold weather? Why?

AChọn rõ ràng: nóng hay lạnh, và nêu sở thích của bạn.

RGiải thích lý do dựa trên cảm giác cơ thể, sức khỏe hoặc hoạt động yêu thích.

EMô tả điều bạn thích làm trong loại thời tiết đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer cold weather over hot weather. When it is cool, I feel more alert and comfortable, which is important for studying medicine. Hot and humid days make me sweat a lot and feel exhausted after just a short walk. I enjoy wearing warm clothes and drinking hot tea on cool mornings. I think cold weather is better for concentration and long hours of study.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually prefer hot weather, even though it can be uncomfortable sometimes. Warm weather energizes me and I feel more active on sunny days. I grew up in a tropical climate, so my body is used to the heat. I love going to the beach or doing outdoor activities when it is warm and bright. Cold weather, on the other hand, makes me want to stay in bed and do nothing.

Câu 7What do you usually do when the weather is very hot?

ANêu thói quen của bạn khi trời nóng (uống nước, ở trong nhà, tắm...).

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn làm như vậy (sức khỏe, thoải mái, thói quen...).

ECho ví dụ cụ thể từ thực tế cuộc sống hàng ngày của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

When the weather is very hot, especially during a heat wave, I try to stay indoors as much as possible. I drink a lot of water and avoid going outside between noon and three in the afternoon. As a medical student, I know how dangerous dehydration can be, so I always carry a water bottle. I also prefer to study in the library where there is air conditioning. Staying cool helps me focus better on my coursework.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On very hot days, I usually wake up early to do my exercise before the temperature rises. I go jogging around six in the morning when there is still a cool breeze. After that, I stay indoors for the rest of the morning and study or read. I also eat lighter meals and avoid heavy food during a heat wave. I find that adjusting my schedule to the weather helps me stay healthy and productive.

Câu 8Is there a type of weather that you dislike? Why?

ANêu loại thời tiết bạn không thích và khẳng định rõ ràng.

RGiải thích lý do cụ thể tại sao bạn không thích loại thời tiết đó.

EMô tả cảm giác hoặc vấn đề bạn gặp phải khi có loại thời tiết đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I really dislike foggy mornings, especially when I have to commute to the hospital early. The fog makes visibility poor and travelling by motorbike feels quite dangerous. I also find that foggy weather makes me feel a bit gloomy and less motivated. Once I was nearly late to morning rounds because I had to drive very slowly in thick fog. I always hope for clear mornings on days when I have important duties.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The type of weather I dislike most is a thunderstorm in the middle of the day. Sudden heavy rain and strong winds make it almost impossible to move around the city safely. I once had to take shelter under a small roof for nearly an hour waiting for a storm to pass. It disrupted my whole schedule that day. I know storms are natural, but they are very inconvenient for someone with a busy hospital timetable.

Câu 9Do you think the weather in your city has changed in recent years?

ANêu nhận xét của bạn: thời tiết có thay đổi hay không trong những năm gần đây.

RGiải thích bạn nhận thấy sự thay đổi đó như thế nào (nóng hơn, mưa thất thường...).

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về sự thay đổi bạn đã quan sát được.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe the weather in my city has changed in recent years. The heat waves seem to come earlier and last longer than when I was a child. My parents also say that the rainy season is now more unpredictable than before. I studied some environmental health topics in medical school that confirmed these changes are linked to climate change. It is concerning because extreme weather affects public health significantly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my observation, the weather has become less predictable over the past few years. We now have unexpected heavy rain even during the dry season, which was quite rare before. The tropical climate here seems to be shifting, with temperatures rising noticeably each summer. I remember as a student in secondary school, the summers were hot but manageable. Now the heat feels much more intense, and heat waves have become more common.

Câu 10Do you prefer to stay indoors or go outside when it rains?

ANêu rõ bạn thích ở trong nhà hay ra ngoài khi trời mưa.

RGiải thích lý do: thích sự ấm cúng trong nhà hay không ngại mưa.

EMô tả cụ thể bạn làm gì khi ở trong nhà hoặc khi ra ngoài dưới mưa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer to stay indoors when it rains, especially during heavy rain. I find rainy days perfect for reading medical textbooks and reviewing notes. I make myself a warm drink and sit by the window listening to the rain, which I actually find quite relaxing. Going outside in heavy rain without proper gear can make you ill, so it is also a health-conscious choice. Rainy days have become some of my most productive study days.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not mind going outside when it rains lightly. I always carry an umbrella during the rainy season, so a gentle shower does not bother me. I actually enjoy the smell of rain and the cooler temperature it brings. However, if there is a thunderstorm with strong wind, I definitely prefer to stay indoors and wait for it to pass. Light rain feels refreshing, but a serious storm is a different matter entirely.

Câu 11What outdoor activities do you enjoy doing in good weather?

ANêu những hoạt động ngoài trời bạn yêu thích khi thời tiết đẹp.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích những hoạt động đó (sức khỏe, giải trí, thư giãn).

EMô tả khi nào và ở đâu bạn thường làm những hoạt động đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

On a nice sunny day, I love going for a morning run in the park near my dormitory. Outdoor activities like jogging help me manage the stress of medical school very well. I usually run for about thirty minutes and then do some stretching exercises. The fresh air and cool breeze in the early morning make me feel refreshed for the whole day. I try to do this at least three times a week when the weather allows.

Trả lời mẫu 2

When the weather is good, I enjoy cycling around the city with a few friends from my class. Outdoor activities are a great way to disconnect from studying for a while. We usually go on weekend mornings when there is a gentle cool breeze and it is not too hot yet. Sometimes we stop at a small cafe and have breakfast together. These outings are really important for my mental health during the demanding years of medical training.

Câu 12Do you think weather affects people's health? Can you give an example?

AKhẳng định: thời tiết có ảnh hưởng đến sức khỏe của con người.

RGiải thích cơ chế hoặc lý do tại sao thời tiết ảnh hưởng đến sức khỏe.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể từ kiến thức y khoa hoặc kinh nghiệm thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, as a medical student I have learned that weather has a significant impact on health. During heat waves, the number of patients with heatstroke and dehydration increases noticeably at hospitals. The elderly and young children are especially vulnerable to extreme temperatures. I observed this pattern during my clinical rotations last summer. It reminded me how important it is for doctors to give patients advice on staying safe in extreme weather.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Weather definitely affects health, and I see evidence of this in my studies. When the rainy season begins, we notice more patients coming in with respiratory infections and dengue fever. The humid and warm conditions during the rainy season are ideal for mosquitoes and bacteria to spread. I also know from personal experience that sleeping in very cold air conditioning can cause a sore throat the next morning. Balancing comfort with health is something I think about every day.

Câu 13Do you watch or listen to weather forecasts regularly?

ANêu thói quen của bạn: có xem/nghe dự báo thời tiết thường xuyên không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn theo dõi hoặc không theo dõi dự báo thời tiết.

ENêu cụ thể cách bạn xem dự báo (ứng dụng, tivi, hay cách khác).

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I check the weather forecast almost every day, usually right after I wake up. I use a weather app on my phone which gives me an hourly prediction for the day. This helps me plan whether to bring a raincoat or an umbrella, especially during the rainy season. Being organised about the weather saves me a lot of trouble. I find it is one of the simplest habits that makes my daily routine smoother.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not watch the weather forecast very often on television, but I do check a weather app on my phone a few times a week. In a tropical climate like Vietnam, the forecast is not always accurate anyway, so I prefer to prepare for all possibilities. During the rainy season, I carry a compact umbrella with me every single day without checking the forecast. I only pay close attention to the forecast when I am planning outdoor activities on my days off.

Câu 14How do you feel when the weather is cold and grey for many days?

AMô tả cảm giác của bạn khi thời tiết lạnh và âm u kéo dài nhiều ngày.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn có cảm giác đó (thiếu ánh sáng, lạnh, không ra ngoài được).

ENêu bạn thường làm gì để cải thiện tâm trạng trong những ngày như vậy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

When the weather is cold and grey for many days, I start to feel a bit low and unmotivated. Without a sunny day to look forward to, my energy levels drop and I find it harder to get out of bed in the morning. I read that lack of sunlight can genuinely affect people's mood, and I believe that is true in my case. To cope, I try to keep a regular schedule and exercise indoors. Staying busy is the best way I have found to get through long grey periods.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I actually find cold and grey days quite comfortable for studying. When I cannot do outdoor activities, I feel less tempted to go out and more focused on my books. I make a pot of tea, put on some background music, and study for hours without distraction. However, if it goes on for more than a week without any sunny day, I do start to miss the sunlight. I think everything is fine in moderation, even grey weather.

Câu 15Is there a season you enjoy most in Vietnam? Why?

AChọn mùa yêu thích của bạn ở Việt Nam và nêu rõ tên mùa đó.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích mùa đó hơn các mùa khác.

EMô tả hoạt động hoặc kỷ niệm đặc biệt gắn liền với mùa đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite season in Vietnam is the dry season, especially from December to February in the south. The weather is warm but not excessively hot, and there is usually a pleasant cool breeze in the evenings. I enjoy this time of year because I can do outdoor activities without worrying about sudden heavy rain. This period also includes the Tet holiday, which is always a wonderful time for family gatherings. The combination of good weather and festive atmosphere makes it my favourite time of year.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy the early part of the rainy season the most, around May and June. After the extremely dry and hot months, the first rains bring a wonderful sense of relief and the air smells so fresh. The temperatures drop slightly and the cool breeze after an afternoon shower feels amazing. I find the sound of rain very calming when I am studying late at night. That short period when the rains first arrive always feels like a fresh start to me.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Mobile phones are useful in daily life

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • mobile phoneđiện thoại di động
  • access informationtruy cập thông tin
  • search the internettìm kiếm trên mạng
  • stay in touchgiữ liên lạc
  • instant messagingnhắn tin tức thì
  • video callgọi video
  • streaming musicnghe nhạc trực tuyến
  • entertainment appứng dụng giải trí
  • online paymentthanh toán trực tuyến
  • daily taskscác công việc hàng ngày
  • convenienttiện lợi
  • at any timebất cứ lúc nào
  • keep up to datecập nhật thông tin mới nhất
  • save timetiết kiệm thời gian

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I would like to talk about why mobile phones are so useful in our daily tasks and everyday life. Personally, I think they have become one of the most important tools for modern people. First of all, mobile phones help us access information very quickly. Whenever we need to know something, we can just search the internet in a few seconds. For example, as a medical student, I often look up medical terms or drug information right on my phone during class. Another reason is that mobile phones make communication much easier. We can stay in touch with family and friends through instant messaging or video calls, even when we are far apart. For example, I video call my parents every week because I study far from home. In addition, mobile phones offer a lot of entertainment apps. We can enjoy streaming music, watch films, or play games to relax after a long study day. On top of that, one idea of my own is that mobile phones also support online payment, so we can pay for food or shopping without carrying cash, which really helps us save time and stay organised. To sum up, mobile phones are truly convenient devices that help us keep up to date, communicate, relax, and manage our lives more easily.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Mobile phones (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • mobile phoneđiện thoại di động
  • stay connectedgiữ liên lạc
  • social mediamạng xã hội
  • distractionsự phân tâm, mất tập trung
  • screen timethời gian sử dụng màn hình
  • online learninghọc trực tuyến
  • digital addictionnghiện thiết bị kỹ thuật số
  • emergency contactliên lạc khẩn cấp
  • health appứng dụng sức khoẻ
  • cyberbullyingbắt nạt trên mạng
  • hands-freerảnh tay, không cầm điện thoại
  • limit phone usehạn chế sử dụng điện thoại
  • productivitynăng suất làm việc
  • face-to-face communicationgiao tiếp trực tiếp

Câu 1Why do people use mobile phones so often?

ANêu lý do chính: điện thoại rất tiện lợi và đa năng.

RGiải thích: giúp liên lạc, tìm thông tin, giải trí mọi lúc mọi nơi.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ dùng điện thoại tra cứu tài liệu y khoa ngay tại phòng bệnh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think people use mobile phones so often because they are extremely convenient. You can stay connected with family, friends, and colleagues at any time and in any place. They also allow us to search for information instantly. For example, as a medical student, I often use my phone to look up drug information between classes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people rely on mobile phones because they combine so many functions in one device. You can use them for communication, navigation, shopping, and entertainment. Social media also keeps people checking their phones constantly. For instance, many of my classmates check their phones first thing in the morning for news and messages.

Câu 2Do mobile phones cause any problems?

AKhẳng định: có, điện thoại gây ra một số vấn đề.

RGiải thích: gây mất tập trung, ảnh hưởng sức khoẻ và các mối quan hệ.

EVí dụ: sinh viên mất ngủ vì dùng điện thoại quá khuya.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I believe mobile phones can cause several problems. The biggest issue is distraction, especially for students who struggle to focus on studying. Spending too much screen time on phones can also harm our eyesight and sleep quality. I have noticed that many classmates stay up late scrolling through social media and feel tired the next day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think mobile phones do cause problems, particularly for young people. Digital addiction is a growing concern, with some people unable to put their phones down even during meals or conversations. This reduces face-to-face communication and can weaken real relationships. For example, I have seen families sitting together but everyone is looking at their own phone instead of talking.

Câu 3Should students use mobile phones at school?

AĐưa ra quan điểm rõ ràng: có thể dùng nhưng cần có giới hạn.

RGiải thích: điện thoại hỗ trợ học tập nhưng cũng dễ gây mất tập trung.

EVí dụ: dùng điện thoại tra từ điển hoặc tìm kiếm tài liệu học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think students should be allowed to use mobile phones at school, but only for educational purposes. Phones can support online learning by giving students quick access to dictionaries and study resources. However, there should be clear rules to prevent distraction during lessons. For example, teachers could allow phone use only during research activities, not during lectures.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, students should limit phone use at school during class time. Phones are a major distraction that reduces concentration and learning quality. However, they can be useful during breaks or for emergency contact with parents. At our medical academy, I think allowing phones only in designated areas would be a good balance.

Câu 4How have mobile phones changed the way people communicate?

ANêu sự thay đổi rõ rệt: giao tiếp trở nên nhanh hơn và dễ hơn nhiều.

RGiải thích: người ta ít gặp mặt trực tiếp hơn, thay vào đó nhắn tin hoặc gọi video.

EVí dụ: gia đình ở xa vẫn giữ liên lạc hàng ngày qua video call.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Mobile phones have completely changed the way we communicate. In the past, people had to meet in person or write letters, but now we can stay connected instantly through calls and messages. Video calls have made long-distance communication feel much more personal. For example, I can talk to my family in another province every evening without any difficulty.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think mobile phones have made communication faster but less personal in some ways. Face-to-face communication has decreased because people prefer to send a quick text rather than meet in person. While this saves time, it can reduce the depth of relationships. For instance, some people find it easier to express emotions through messages than through direct conversation.

Câu 5Do you think mobile phones are more helpful or harmful for health?

AĐưa ra lập trường: có thể hữu ích nếu dùng đúng cách.

RGiải thích: ứng dụng sức khoẻ rất có ích nhưng dùng quá nhiều gây hại.

EVí dụ: dùng ứng dụng theo dõi nhịp tim hoặc nhắc uống thuốc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think mobile phones can be very helpful for health if used wisely. There are many health apps that help people track their exercise, monitor sleep, and remind them to take medication. As a medical student, I find apps for anatomy and drug references very useful. However, we must control screen time to avoid eye strain and poor sleep.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, mobile phones are harmful to health when people cannot limit phone use. Excessive screen time causes headaches, poor posture, and sleep problems. Digital addiction can also increase stress and anxiety. Although health apps exist, many people use their phones mainly for entertainment, which does more harm than good.

Câu 6Should the government limit how much time people spend on mobile phones?

ANêu quan điểm: không nên, vì đây là quyền tự do cá nhân.

RGiải thích: trách nhiệm tự quản lý bản thân quan trọng hơn luật cấm.

EVí dụ: cha mẹ có thể dùng tính năng kiểm soát thời gian màn hình cho trẻ em.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think the government should directly limit phone use for adults, because personal freedom is important. People should be responsible for managing their own screen time. However, the government could run public education campaigns about the dangers of digital addiction. Parents, for example, should use parental control features to manage their children's phone usage.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe the government should introduce some guidelines, especially for children. Excessive screen time among young people is linked to poor mental health and academic problems. Schools and families need support from official policies to limit phone use during study hours. For instance, several countries have already banned mobile phones in classrooms, and the results have been positive.

Câu 7How do mobile phones affect the relationship between parents and children?

ANêu tác động hai chiều: vừa giúp kết nối, vừa tạo khoảng cách.

RGiải thích: cha mẹ dễ theo dõi con hơn nhưng cả hai đều dễ bị phân tâm.

EVí dụ: cha mẹ bận nhìn điện thoại, ít nói chuyện với con.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Mobile phones can both help and harm the relationship between parents and children. On the positive side, parents can always reach their children through emergency contact and track their safety. However, when both parents and children are absorbed in their own phones, face-to-face communication suffers. I have seen families where no one talks at the dinner table because everyone is on their phone.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think mobile phones sometimes create a distance between parents and children rather than bringing them closer. Children may prefer social media and online friends over talking with their parents. At the same time, parents who are always on their phones set a poor example. To improve this, families should agree on phone-free times, such as during meals or before bedtime.

Câu 8Do you think young people are more dependent on mobile phones than older people? Why?

AĐồng ý: người trẻ phụ thuộc nhiều hơn vì lớn lên cùng công nghệ.

RGiải thích: người trẻ dùng điện thoại cho mọi việc từ học tập đến giải trí.

EVí dụ: sinh viên dùng điện thoại để học, đặt đồ ăn, đặt xe, và giải trí.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think young people are generally more dependent on mobile phones than older generations. We grew up with technology and use our phones for almost everything, from online learning to ordering food and transport. Social media is also a big part of young people's daily lives. For many students I know, going a day without a phone feels almost impossible.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that young people tend to rely more on mobile phones, but this is not entirely negative. We use phones to boost productivity through planning apps, note-taking, and research. Older people may use phones mainly for calls, while younger users take advantage of a much wider range of features. However, this heavy reliance does increase the risk of digital addiction.

Câu 9Should people use mobile phones while driving? Why or why not?

APhản đối rõ ràng: tuyệt đối không nên vì rất nguy hiểm.

RGiải thích: dùng điện thoại khi lái xe gây mất tập trung và tai nạn.

EVí dụ: nhiều vụ tai nạn giao thông xảy ra do tài xế nhìn điện thoại.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe people should never use mobile phones while driving. It is one of the most dangerous forms of distraction on the road and causes thousands of accidents every year. Even using a hands-free device can reduce concentration. Drivers should pull over safely if they need to make or receive a call.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Using a mobile phone while driving is extremely dangerous and should be completely banned. The distraction caused by looking at a screen, even for a second, can lead to fatal accidents. While some people argue that hands-free calls are safer, research shows they still reduce driver attention significantly. The law in Vietnam already prohibits this, and I fully support strict enforcement.

Câu 10How can mobile phones be used to improve medical care?

ANêu lợi ích cụ thể trong y tế: điện thoại hỗ trợ chẩn đoán và theo dõi bệnh nhân.

RGiải thích: bác sĩ và bệnh nhân đều có thể tra cứu thông tin và kết nối nhanh hơn.

EVí dụ: ứng dụng telemedicine giúp bệnh nhân vùng xa gặp bác sĩ qua video call.

Trả lời mẫu 1

As a medical student, I think mobile phones have great potential to improve healthcare. Doctors can use health apps to monitor patients remotely and receive real-time data about blood pressure or heart rate. Online learning platforms on phones also help medical professionals update their knowledge quickly. For example, telemedicine allows patients in rural areas to consult a doctor without travelling far.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Mobile phones can significantly improve medical care, especially in emergency situations. Patients can use apps to record symptoms and share them with doctors before an appointment, making consultations more efficient. Health apps that remind patients to take medication on time also improve treatment outcomes. At our hospital, doctors use messaging apps to quickly consult colleagues about difficult cases.

Câu 11Compare the advantages and disadvantages of using mobile phones for studying.

ANêu cả hai mặt một cách cân bằng: có lợi cho việc học nhưng cũng dễ gây sao nhãng.

RGiải thích: dễ tra cứu tài liệu nhưng [[social media]] và trò chơi làm mất thời gian.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa dùng app Anki để học thuộc lòng từ vựng y tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Mobile phones offer clear advantages for studying, such as easy access to online learning platforms, dictionaries, and research materials at any time. Students can also use flashcard apps to memorise medical terminology effectively. However, the disadvantages are significant too. Distraction from social media and games can seriously reduce study time and productivity.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my experience, mobile phones are a double-edged tool for students. On one hand, they enable flexible online learning and quick communication with teachers. On the other hand, screen time spent on entertainment often creeps into study time. I think the key is self-discipline: setting specific times to limit phone use and focus on studying without interruptions.

Câu 12Do you think mobile phones have made people less able to communicate in person? Why?

AĐồng ý một phần: điện thoại làm giảm kỹ năng giao tiếp trực tiếp của nhiều người.

RGiải thích: người ta quen nhắn tin hơn nói chuyện, nên ngại và thiếu kỹ năng giao tiếp mặt đối mặt.

EVí dụ: nhiều bạn trẻ cảm thấy lo lắng khi phải nói chuyện trực tiếp với người lạ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I partly agree that mobile phones have weakened some people's ability to communicate in person. When people rely on texting and social media, they get less practice with face-to-face communication skills like eye contact and active listening. Some young people feel anxious when speaking directly with strangers. However, for others, online communication actually helps them gain confidence before speaking in person.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think mobile phones have changed rather than simply reduced our communication skills. Many people now prefer written messages because they feel less pressured than direct conversation. This can lead to misunderstandings since text lacks tone and emotion. As future doctors, we must maintain strong face-to-face communication skills because building trust with patients is essential and cannot be replaced by digital tools.

Câu 13Should mobile phones be banned in hospitals? Why or why not?

AĐồng ý có giới hạn: nên hạn chế ở một số khu vực, không cấm hoàn toàn.

RGiải thích: điện thoại có thể ảnh hưởng thiết bị y tế và làm mất tập trung nhân viên y tế.

EVí dụ: cấm dùng điện thoại trong phòng mổ và phòng hồi sức nhưng cho phép ở khu hành chính.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think mobile phones should be restricted in certain areas of hospitals rather than completely banned. In operating theatres and intensive care units, phones can interfere with medical equipment and create distraction for healthcare workers. However, in waiting areas and offices, phones are useful for communication and accessing patient records. A balanced policy would protect patient safety while allowing productivity.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, a total ban on mobile phones in hospitals is not practical in modern healthcare. Doctors and nurses rely on phones to access health apps, look up drug information, and coordinate care quickly. However, strict rules should apply in sensitive areas. Patients should also be reminded to use phones quietly and respectfully so as not to disturb others who are resting or receiving treatment.

Câu 14How might mobile phone technology change in the future?

ADự đoán xu hướng: điện thoại sẽ thông minh hơn và tích hợp nhiều công nghệ tiên tiến.

RGiải thích: trí tuệ nhân tạo và cảm biến sinh học sẽ biến điện thoại thành thiết bị y tế cá nhân.

EVí dụ: điện thoại tương lai có thể phát hiện bệnh sớm qua phân tích giọng nói hoặc hình ảnh da.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe mobile phones will become far more intelligent in the future, with built-in artificial intelligence that can assist with complex tasks. Health apps will likely be able to monitor vital signs continuously and detect early signs of illness. For medical professionals, this could be a powerful tool for early diagnosis. The phone may eventually act as a personal health app device that connects directly to hospital systems.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, future mobile phones will blur the line between communication devices and medical tools. Sensors may be able to measure blood sugar, oxygen levels, and even detect stress through voice analysis. This will be especially valuable in rural areas where access to doctors is limited. However, we must ensure that this technology remains affordable so that everyone, not just the wealthy, can benefit from these advances.

Câu 15Do you think children should be given mobile phones at a young age? Why or why not?

AKhông nên cho trẻ nhỏ dùng điện thoại quá sớm: cần chờ đến độ tuổi nhất định.

RGiải thích: dùng quá sớm ảnh hưởng phát triển não bộ, thị lực và kỹ năng xã hội.

EVí dụ: trẻ em nên được khuyến khích vui chơi ngoài trời thay vì nhìn màn hình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think young children should be given their own mobile phones. Excessive screen time at an early age can harm brain development, damage eyesight, and reduce physical activity. Children need to develop social skills through play and face-to-face communication, not through screens. If parents want children to use a phone for safety, a basic phone for calls only is a better option than a smartphone.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think children should only be introduced to mobile phones when they are mature enough to use them responsibly, perhaps around twelve or thirteen years old. Before that, digital addiction and exposure to harmful content on social media pose serious risks. Parents should supervise usage carefully and set clear rules about screen time. Teaching children healthy phone habits early is more effective than banning phones entirely later in life.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 7

Part 1: Your house or room · Reading · Part 2: Friends play an important role in our life · Part 3: Friends

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Your house or room

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • live in a flatsống trong căn hộ chung cư
  • share a roomở chung phòng với người khác
  • cosyấm cúng, thoải mái
  • spaciousrộng rãi
  • tidy updọn dẹp, sắp xếp gọn gàng
  • home comfortstiện nghi ở nhà
  • feel at homecảm thấy thoải mái như ở nhà
  • natural lightánh sáng tự nhiên
  • personal belongingsđồ dùng cá nhân
  • quiet neighbourhoodkhu dân cư yên tĩnh
  • decoratetrang trí
  • landlordchủ nhà trọ
  • rent a roomthuê phòng trọ
  • feel crampedcảm thấy chật hẹp, bí bách

Câu 1Where do you live?

ANêu nơi bạn đang sống (thành phố, khu vực, loại chỗ ở).

RGiải thích lý do bạn sống ở đó (công việc, học tập, gia đình).

EMô tả một chi tiết cụ thể về nơi đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I currently live in a flat near the military academy in Hanoi. I moved here because it is close to my workplace, so I do not have to travel far every day. The area is quite a quiet neighbourhood, which I really appreciate after a busy shift at the hospital.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I rent a room in a shared house in the suburbs of Ho Chi Minh City. I chose this place because the rent is affordable and the landlord is very helpful and friendly. There is a small market nearby, so it is easy for me to buy things I need every day.

Câu 2What is your favourite room in your house?

ANêu tên phòng yêu thích của bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích phòng đó.

ECho ví dụ về việc bạn thường làm trong phòng đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite room is definitely my bedroom. It is small but very cosy, and I have decorated it with some plants and photos that make me feel at home. After a long day at the hospital, I love relaxing there with a good book.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I love the living room the most because it is the most spacious room in my house. It gets a lot of natural light during the day, which makes it feel bright and welcoming. My family and I often watch films together there on weekends, so it holds many happy memories.

Câu 3Do you like living there? Why?

ANói rõ bạn có thích hay không thích sống ở đó.

RĐưa ra lý do chính (tiện lợi, môi trường, con người).

ENêu một ví dụ cụ thể minh họa cho lý do đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy living here because it is very convenient. The hospital where I work is only ten minutes away, so I save a lot of time every day. There are also many good restaurants and coffee shops nearby, which makes life much more enjoyable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I have mixed feelings about living there. The quiet neighbourhood is great for studying, but sometimes I feel cramped because my room is quite small. I manage by keeping it tidy and only keeping my most important personal belongings there.

Câu 4Do you live alone or with other people?

ANói bạn sống một mình hay với ai.

RGiải thích lý do hoặc hoàn cảnh dẫn đến tình trạng đó.

EChia sẻ cảm nhận hoặc một tình huống cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I share a room with a colleague from the same department. We decided to rent a room together to save money on costs. It works out well because we have similar schedules and we both try to keep the room clean and organised.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I live alone in a small studio flat near the city centre. I prefer this arrangement because I value my privacy and I can keep my personal belongings in order without worrying about disturbing anyone. It can be a little lonely sometimes, but I enjoy the peace and quiet after work.

Câu 5How long have you lived in your current home?

ANêu khoảng thời gian bạn đã sống ở chỗ ở hiện tại.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chuyển đến hoặc ở lại đó lâu như vậy.

ENói về điều gì đó thay đổi hoặc không thay đổi trong thời gian đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I have lived in my current flat for about two years now. I moved here right after I started my postgraduate programme, so it has been my home throughout my studies. Over time I have made it more cosy by adding some furniture and a few personal touches.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have only been living here for six months, so I am still getting used to the place. I moved here when I was transferred to this hospital branch, so the decision was quite sudden. Slowly I am starting to feel at home, especially now that I know my neighbours better.

Câu 6Is your home near your workplace or school?

ANói rõ nhà bạn có gần nơi làm việc hay không.

RGiải thích điều đó ảnh hưởng đến cuộc sống hàng ngày của bạn như thế nào.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về thuận lợi hoặc bất lợi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my flat is very close to the hospital where I work, which is one of the main reasons I chose it. It only takes me about five minutes to walk there, so I can sleep a little longer in the morning. This is especially helpful when I have early morning rounds or emergency duties.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Unfortunately, my room is quite far from the academy, so I have to take a bus every day. The journey takes about forty minutes each way, which can be tiring after a long shift. I am planning to find a place to rent a room closer to the hospital when my current contract ends.

Câu 7What do you usually do in your room in the evenings?

ANêu hoạt động bạn thường làm vào buổi tối trong phòng.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích làm điều đó.

ECho một ví dụ cụ thể hoặc thói quen thường xuyên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In the evenings, I usually review my medical notes and prepare for the next day at the hospital. I find it easier to concentrate in my own room because it is quiet and I have all my personal belongings organised around me. Sometimes I also watch a short video to relax before going to sleep.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I often spend my evenings tidying up and doing some light exercise in my room. After being on my feet all day at the hospital, I enjoy the simple home comforts like making a cup of tea and reading. Occasionally I video-call my family back in my hometown, which always makes me feel better.

Câu 8Do you prefer a tidy or a relaxed, messy room? Why?

ANói rõ sở thích của bạn: phòng gọn gàng hay thoải mái hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn có sở thích đó.

EMinh họa bằng một thói quen hoặc tình huống cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly prefer a tidy room because I find it easier to focus when everything is in order. As a medical student, I deal with a lot of information every day, and a messy environment makes me feel stressed. I always tidy up before studying so that my mind feels clear and organised.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I tend to have a relaxed approach to tidiness during busy weeks. When I have lots of assignments and hospital duties, I sometimes let things pile up. However, I always make sure to tidy up on my days off because I know a clean room helps me feel at home and recharge properly.

Câu 9Is there anything you would like to change about your room or home?

ANêu điều bạn muốn thay đổi trong phòng hoặc nhà.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó quan trọng với bạn.

EMô tả cụ thể bạn sẽ thay đổi như thế nào nếu có thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

If I could change one thing, I would make my room more spacious. Right now it feels cramped when I spread out my books and study materials. I would love to have a bigger desk and more shelf space to organise my medical references properly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would love to have more natural light in my flat. The windows are quite small, so the rooms feel a little dark during the day. I have added a few lamps to help, but I think natural light is much better for the eyes, especially when I am reading for long periods.

Câu 10Do you decorate your room? What have you added to make it feel personal?

ANói bạn có trang trí phòng không và bạn đã thêm gì.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn những vật trang trí đó.

EMô tả một đồ vật cụ thể và ý nghĩa của nó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have decorated my room with a few small plants and some family photos on the wall. I chose these because they make the space feel warm and personal, even though I am far from home. The photos especially help me feel at home when I am tired or homesick.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have not done much to decorate my room because I am renting and the landlord does not allow me to paint the walls. However, I have added a colourful rug and some posters of places I want to travel to one day. These small touches make the room feel much more like mine.

Câu 11Do you have a quiet place at home to study or read?

ANói bạn có nơi yên tĩnh để học hay đọc sách ở nhà không.

RGiải thích điều đó ảnh hưởng đến việc học của bạn như thế nào.

EMô tả nơi đó hoặc nêu một tình huống cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I am lucky to have a small desk in my room where I can study without being disturbed. The quiet neighbourhood where I live also helps, because there is not much noise from outside. I find that having a dedicated study corner really helps me focus on my medical coursework.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really, because I share a room with a colleague and we sometimes have different schedules. When he is awake and watching something, it is hard for me to concentrate. I usually go to the library or a nearby coffee shop when I need a truly quiet place to review my notes.

Câu 12How important is it to you to feel comfortable at home?

ANêu mức độ quan trọng của việc cảm thấy thoải mái ở nhà đối với bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao sự thoải mái lại có ý nghĩa với bạn.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ về cách bạn tạo ra sự thoải mái.

Trả lời mẫu 1

It is very important to me because my home is the place where I recover after long and demanding hospital shifts. I need home comforts like a clean bed, good lighting, and a quiet space to truly rest. Without these things, I find it difficult to stay focused and motivated the next day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

It matters a lot to me, but I have learned to adjust my expectations since starting this programme. During residency, you do not always get the perfect living situation, so I focus on small things like keeping my room cosy and having a comfortable chair to sit in. These little things make a big difference to my overall wellbeing.

Câu 13Do you spend a lot of time at home, or are you usually out?

ANói bạn thường ở nhà nhiều hay hay ra ngoài.

RGiải thích lý do dẫn đến thói quen đó.

ENêu một ví dụ về một ngày điển hình của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Because of my hospital duties, I am often out for most of the day and sometimes the night as well. I usually only come home to sleep and prepare for the next shift, so my flat is mainly a place to rest. However, on my days off I really enjoy staying in and enjoying the home comforts I have been missing.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually spend quite a lot of time at home, especially in the evenings and on weekends. After busy days at the hospital, I prefer to stay in and recharge rather than go out. I enjoy cooking simple meals and reviewing my study materials in my cosy room, which helps me unwind.

Câu 14What is the best thing about the area where you live?

ANêu điều tốt nhất về khu vực bạn đang sống.

RGiải thích tại sao điều đó quan trọng với bạn trong cuộc sống hàng ngày.

EKể một câu chuyện ngắn hoặc ví dụ cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

The best thing about my area is that it is a very quiet neighbourhood, which is perfect for someone who needs to study a lot. There is very little traffic noise at night, so I can sleep well and wake up feeling refreshed. I think good rest is essential for a medical professional, so I really value this.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I love that there is a large park just a short walk from where I live in a flat. I often go there in the mornings to exercise before my shift begins. Being close to green spaces makes the area feel much more relaxed and healthy, even though we are in the middle of the city.

Câu 15Would you prefer to live in a house or a flat in the future?

ANói rõ bạn muốn sống trong nhà riêng hay căn hộ trong tương lai.

RGiải thích lý do bạn có sở thích đó.

EMô tả ngôi nhà hoặc căn hộ lý tưởng của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In the future, I would love to live in a small house with a garden. I currently live in a flat and I miss having outdoor space where I can grow plants and relax in fresh air. I think a garden would be wonderful for my mental health, especially after stressful days at work.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think I would prefer a modern flat in a convenient location. A spacious flat with plenty of natural light and good facilities nearby would suit my lifestyle as a busy doctor. I do not need a large place, just somewhere cosy and well-organised where I can truly feel at home after work.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Reading

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • reading habitthói quen đọc sách
  • concentrate ontập trung vào
  • expand vocabularymở rộng vốn từ vựng
  • a wide range ofmột loạt các / nhiều loại
  • keep up withtheo kịp / cập nhật

  • paperbacksách bìa mềm
