Câu 1Do you like reading books?

ANói thẳng bạn có thích đọc sách hay không.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn thích hoặc không thích.

EĐưa ra ví dụ cụ thể về loại sách hoặc thời gian bạn đọc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy reading books, especially in the evening. It helps me relax after a long day at the hospital. I find that books give me a chance to learn new things outside of my medical studies. For example, I usually read for about an hour before I go to sleep.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I do not read regularly because I am very busy with my studies. However, I try to pick up a book on weekends when I have more free time. I think reading books is still important even for busy people. Last weekend I managed to read a few chapters of a story I like.

Câu 2What kind of books do you like?

ANêu rõ thể loại sách bạn yêu thích.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích thể loại đó.

EKể tên một cuốn sách cụ thể hoặc tác giả bạn đã đọc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I mainly like non-fiction books, especially books about health and science. I enjoy them because they help me improve my knowledge and connect well with my medical training. For example, I recently read a book about the human brain that I found very interesting. It gave me a deeper understanding of neurology.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer fiction, especially detective stories and novels. I like them because they let me escape from stress and use my imagination. Reading fiction feels like a reward after a hard week of studying. My favourite author is Agatha Christie and I have read several of her books.

Câu 3Do you prefer reading books or using the Internet? Why?

AChọn rõ ràng một trong hai: sách hoặc Internet.

RGiải thích lý do bạn ưa thích lựa chọn đó hơn.

EĐưa ra tình huống hoặc ví dụ cụ thể từ cuộc sống của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer reading books because I can concentrate on the content much better. When I use the Internet, there are too many distractions and I often lose focus. A paperback book feels more comfortable for long study sessions. For example, when I need to study a complex medical topic, I always look for a textbook first.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer using the Internet because it helps me keep up with the latest medical research quickly. Books can become outdated, but online sources are always updated. I also like that I can find a wide range of articles and videos in one place. For instance, I use medical websites every day to check new treatment guidelines.

Câu 4How often do you read books?

ANói tần suất cụ thể bạn đọc sách trong tuần hoặc tháng.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn đọc nhiều hoặc ít như vậy.

EMô tả thói quen đọc cụ thể trong ngày hoặc tuần của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I try to read regularly, about three or four times a week. I do this because I believe a strong reading habit is important for a doctor. I usually read for about thirty minutes during my lunch break. It helps me stay informed and also gives my mind a short rest from clinical work.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I only read books once or twice a month because my schedule is very busy. Most of my reading time is spent on medical journals and study notes rather than general books. I wish I could read more often, but right now my studies take priority. I hope to build a better reading habit after I finish my exams.

Câu 5Where do you usually read books?

ANêu địa điểm cụ thể bạn thường đọc sách.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích đọc ở nơi đó.

EMô tả khung cảnh hoặc đưa ra ví dụ về một lần đọc gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually read books in my room at night because it is quiet and I can concentrate on the text. I sit at my desk with a lamp on so I do not strain my eyes. It has become part of my evening routine before sleeping. Sometimes I also read in the common room of my dormitory on weekends.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to read in the library because there are few distractions there. The library also has a wide range of books that I can borrow books from easily. I find it much easier to concentrate on reading when I am surrounded by other students who are also studying. Last week I spent two hours there reading a book about medical history.

Câu 6Do you read more physical books or e-books?

ANói rõ bạn đọc sách giấy hay sách điện tử nhiều hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn ưa thích hình thức đó.

ECho ví dụ về tình huống bạn dùng mỗi loại.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I read more e-books than physical books because they are very convenient. I can carry many e-books on my phone and read anywhere during spare moments. This is very useful for a medical student who is always on the move. For example, I often read a medical e-book while waiting between lectures.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I still prefer paperback books over e-books because I find them easier on my eyes. After staring at a computer screen all day, reading a paperback feels more relaxing. I also like being able to make notes in the margins of a physical book. My favourite paperback is a pharmacology textbook that I use almost every day.

Câu 7Did you read a lot when you were a child?

ANói bạn có đọc nhiều hồi nhỏ hay không.

RGiải thích hoàn cảnh hoặc lý do bạn đọc nhiều hay ít.

EKể lại một kỷ niệm hoặc cuốn sách bạn nhớ từ thời thơ ấu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I read a lot when I was young because my parents always encouraged me. My mother used to take me to the library every Saturday to borrow books. I especially loved adventure stories and picture books about science. Those reading habits from childhood definitely helped me become a better student today.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I did not read very much as a child because I preferred playing outside with friends. I only read books when my teachers required it for school assignments. However, I remember one story about a young doctor that really inspired me. That book is actually one of the reasons I decided to study medicine.

Câu 8Have you ever read an English book?

AXác nhận bạn đã hoặc chưa đọc sách tiếng Anh.

RGiải thích mục đích hoặc cảm nhận của bạn khi đọc.

ENêu tên sách hoặc mô tả nội dung bạn đã đọc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have read a few English books, mainly medical textbooks and medical journals. I read them because most of the latest research is published in English. At first it was quite difficult, but my reading skills in English have improved a lot. Last semester I read an English textbook about internal medicine from cover to cover.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have only read short English non-fiction articles, not full books. I find it hard to concentrate on long English texts because my vocabulary is still limited. However, I am trying to expand my vocabulary by reading one English article every day. I hope to read a full English book before the end of this year.

Câu 9Do you think reading is important for doctors? Why?

ANêu quan điểm rõ ràng về tầm quan trọng của việc đọc với bác sĩ.

RGiải thích lý do cụ thể liên quan đến nghề y.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về tình huống bác sĩ cần đọc nhiều.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe reading books and medical journals is essential for doctors. Medicine changes very fast and doctors need to keep up with new treatments and discoveries. If a doctor does not read regularly, they may miss important updates that could affect their patients. For example, new guidelines for treating infectious diseases are published every year.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think reading is very important, but not only medical journals. Doctors should also read books on communication and psychology to understand patients better. A doctor who reads a wide range of materials becomes a more well-rounded professional. I try to read one article about doctor-patient communication every week for this reason.

Câu 10What was the last book you read?

ANêu tên hoặc chủ đề của cuốn sách gần nhất bạn đọc.

RNói tại sao bạn chọn cuốn sách đó.

EChia sẻ cảm nhận hoặc điều bạn học được từ cuốn sách.

Trả lời mẫu 1

The last book I read was a non-fiction book about the history of medicine. I chose it because I wanted to understand how treatments have changed over the centuries. It was very interesting and helped me appreciate modern medicine much more. I would recommend that book to any medical student.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The last book I read was a short novel I borrowed from a friend. I chose it because I needed something light to read after a stressful week of exams. It was a fiction story about friendship and I enjoyed it very much. Reading that novel reminded me how important it is to take a break from study sometimes.

Câu 11Do you prefer reading alone or with other people?

ANói rõ bạn thích đọc một mình hay cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do bạn ưa thích kiểu đó.

EMô tả một tình huống cụ thể phản ánh sở thích của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I definitely prefer reading alone because I need silence to concentrate on the text. When other people are around, I find it hard to absorb what I am reading. I usually go to a quiet corner of the library where I will not be disturbed. This way I can read for a long time without losing focus.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually enjoy reading with my study group sometimes. We each read a different section of our textbook and then share what we have learned. This makes our study sessions more efficient and helps us all improve our knowledge faster. I think this kind of shared reading works especially well for complex medical topics.

Câu 12Do you usually finish every book you start reading?

ANói thật bạn có thường đọc hết sách hay không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn đọc hết hoặc bỏ dở.

EKể về một cuốn sách bạn đã đọc hết hoặc bỏ dở và lý do.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I almost always finish every book I start. Once I begin reading a book, I feel a strong desire to know how it ends. I think it is respectful to the author to finish reading what they wrote. The last book I completed was a novel about a surgeon, and I finished it in just one week.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not always finish reading every book I start. Sometimes a book turns out to be too difficult or not as interesting as I expected. When that happens, I prefer to put the book down and find something more useful. I believe it is better to read a book you enjoy than to force yourself through one you dislike.

Câu 13Has a book ever changed the way you think?

AXác nhận có hay không, và nêu cuốn sách đó.

RGiải thích cuốn sách đó đã tác động đến suy nghĩ của bạn như thế nào.

EMô tả cụ thể sự thay đổi trong quan điểm hoặc hành động của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, one book really changed my thinking. I read a non-fiction book about how lifestyle choices affect long-term health. After reading that book, I started exercising more and paying more attention to my diet. It made me realise that doctors need to practice what they teach patients about healthy habits.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I read a book about a famous Vietnamese doctor who worked in very difficult conditions during the war. That book changed the way I see my own challenges as a medical student. It made me feel grateful and more committed to my medical career. I often recommend that book to my classmates when they feel stressed about their studies.

Câu 14Do you prefer reading on paper or on a screen?

AChọn rõ: giấy hay màn hình.

RGiải thích lý do về sức khỏe, sự tiện lợi hoặc sở thích cá nhân.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về khi nào bạn dùng mỗi hình thức.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer reading on paper because it is much easier on my eyes after a long day of looking at screens. Holding a paperback also helps me concentrate on the content without the temptation to check my phone. I find that I remember information better when I read on paper. For study notes, I always print them out rather than reading them on a screen.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer reading on a screen because it is more practical for my lifestyle. I can access e-books and medical journals instantly without going to the library. Reading on my tablet also lets me highlight text and search for key words very quickly. When I need to keep up with the latest research, online reading is simply much faster.

Câu 15Would you like to read more in the future? Why?

ANói rõ bạn có muốn đọc nhiều hơn trong tương lai không.

RGiải thích lý do hoặc mục tiêu đọc của bạn.

ENêu loại sách hoặc kế hoạch cụ thể bạn muốn thực hiện.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would love to read more books in the future, especially after I graduate. Right now my time is very limited, but I plan to build a stronger reading habit once my workload reduces. I want to read more non-fiction books about global health and public medicine. I believe reading widely will make me a more effective and empathetic doctor.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Yes, I definitely want to read more in the future. I would like to expand my vocabulary in English by reading more English medical journals and books. I also want to explore fiction more because I think stories help develop empathy, which is very important in medicine. My goal is to read at least one book every month starting next year.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Friends play an important role in our life

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • play an important roleđóng vai trò quan trọng
  • share happinesschia sẻ niềm vui
  • share problemschia sẻ khó khăn / tâm sự
  • emotional supportsự hỗ trợ tinh thần
  • go through difficult timesvượt qua giai đoạn khó khăn
  • lend a helping handgiúp đỡ ai đó
  • have fun togethervui chơi cùng nhau
  • relieve stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • motivate each otherđộng viên lẫn nhau
  • personal growthsự phát triển bản thân
  • expand your horizonsmở rộng tầm nhìn / hiểu biết
  • loyal friendngười bạn trung thành
  • strengthen friendshipcủng cố tình bạn
  • feel less lonelycảm thấy bớt cô đơn

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I would like to talk about why friends play an important role in our life. In my opinion, having good friends makes life much happier and more meaningful. First of all, friends are the people we share happiness and share problems with. When something good happens, we want to tell our friends first. And when we feel sad or stressed, talking to a friend gives us emotional support. For example, when I failed an exam last year, my best friend listened to me and helped me feel better. Another reason is that friends lend a helping hand when we go through difficult times. They can help us with practical things like studying, moving house, or finding a job. I remember my friend helped me prepare for an important test by studying with me every evening. In addition, friends help us have fun together and relieve stress. Doing activities like playing sports, watching films, or just chatting can make us forget our worries. This is very important for students who have a lot of pressure. On top of that, good friends can motivate each other and support our personal growth. They give honest advice and help us expand your horizons by introducing new ideas or hobbies. To sum up, friends help us emotionally, practically, and personally. A loyal friend is truly one of the greatest gifts in life, and we should always work to strengthen friendship with the people we care about.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Friends (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • close friendbạn thân thiết
  • long-term friendshiptình bạn lâu dài
  • keep in touchgiữ liên lạc
  • trust and respecttin tưởng và tôn trọng
  • emotional supporthỗ trợ tinh thần
  • social circlevòng kết bạn xã hội
  • common interestssở thích chung
  • childhood friendbạn thời thơ ấu
  • peer pressureáp lực từ bạn bè
  • online friendshiptình bạn trực tuyến
  • quality over quantitychất lượng hơn số lượng
  • make new friendskết bạn mới
  • support networkmạng lưới hỗ trợ
  • bond with someonegắn kết với ai đó

Câu 1Why are friends important to young people?

ANêu rõ bạn bè rất quan trọng với người trẻ.

RGiải thích vì sao: hỗ trợ tinh thần, học hỏi, chia sẻ.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể từ cuộc sống học sinh/sinh viên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think close friends are very important for young people. During this stage of life, people face many challenges such as school pressure and family problems, so having someone to talk to provides valuable emotional support. For example, when a student feels stressed about exams, a good friend can listen and help them feel better. Without friends, young people can feel lonely and lose confidence.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Friends play a big role in the development of young people. Through friendships, young people learn important social skills like communication and trust and respect. For instance, when teenagers join clubs or school teams, they make new friends who share common interests and help each other grow. I believe these relationships shape who they become as adults.

Câu 2Is it better to have many friends or a few close friends?

AChọn rõ ràng: một số ít bạn thân hoặc nhiều bạn.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao lựa chọn đó tốt hơn.

EĐưa ra ví dụ so sánh hoặc tình huống thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my opinion, it is better to have a few close friends rather than many acquaintances. The reason is that quality over quantity matters more in friendship, because true friends are there for you when you really need help. For example, when I was going through a difficult time, it was my two or three closest friends who supported me, not the many people I just say hello to. A large social circle can feel empty if there is no real connection.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think having many friends has its own advantages and should not be underestimated. A wide social circle gives you more opportunities to meet different people, learn new ideas, and get help in various situations. For example, in the medical field, knowing many colleagues can help you share knowledge and find resources more easily. However, I agree that a balance is ideal, with a few people you can truly bond with someone at a deeper level.

Câu 3How can people keep long-term friendships?

ANêu cách duy trì tình bạn lâu dài.

RGiải thích tại sao những cách đó có hiệu quả.

ECho ví dụ về hành động cụ thể hoặc thói quen.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe the most important way to maintain a long-term friendship is to keep in touch regularly, even when life gets busy. People drift apart when they stop communicating, so making the effort to call or meet shows that you care. For example, my best friend and I have a habit of having coffee together once a month, and this simple routine has kept our friendship strong for many years. Consistency and effort are key.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To keep a long-term friendship, people need to show trust and respect and be honest with each other. Friendships can survive disagreements if both people are willing to listen and forgive. For instance, a childhood friend and I once had a serious argument, but because we talked it through and were honest, our friendship actually became stronger. I also think offering emotional support during hard times is what truly bonds people together.

Câu 4Do you think friendships change as people get older? Why?

AĐồng ý hoặc không đồng ý rằng tình bạn thay đổi theo tuổi tác.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân: bận rộn hơn, ưu tiên khác đi.

ESo sánh tình bạn hồi nhỏ và lúc trưởng thành.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think friendships definitely change as people get older. When we are young, we have more free time and see our friends every day at school, so it is easy to bond with someone naturally. As adults, responsibilities like work and family take up most of our time, making it harder to keep in touch. For example, after graduating from university, many of my classmates moved to different cities and we communicate much less than before.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that friendships evolve over time, but I do not think this is always a negative change. As people mature, they tend to value quality over quantity and focus on a smaller group of truly meaningful relationships. For instance, older adults often have a reliable support network of just a few trusted people rather than a large but shallow social circle. I believe these fewer but deeper friendships can be more satisfying.

Câu 5How do you think technology affects modern friendships?

ANêu quan điểm về tác động của công nghệ lên tình bạn.

RGiải thích tác động tích cực hoặc tiêu cực.

ELấy ví dụ về mạng xã hội hoặc nhắn tin.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think technology has made it much easier to keep in touch with friends, especially those who live far away. Thanks to social media and video calls, online friendships can now feel almost as real as face-to-face ones. For example, during the pandemic, many people maintained their support network through group chats and video calls, which helped reduce loneliness. Without technology, many long-term friendships would have faded away.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While technology helps people stay connected, I believe it can also weaken the quality of friendships. When people rely only on messages and social media, they may lose the habit of spending real time together and bond with someone on a deeper level. For instance, two people might chat online every day but never truly understand each other the way they would through a face-to-face conversation. I feel that online friendships often lack the depth of in-person relationships.

Câu 6Should schools teach children how to make and keep friends? Why?

AĐồng ý hay không đồng ý việc nhà trường dạy kỹ năng kết bạn.

RGiải thích vì sao điều đó có ích hoặc không cần thiết.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về hoạt động nhóm hoặc kỹ năng xã hội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe that schools should help children learn how to make new friends and develop social skills. Many children struggle with peer pressure or feel left out, and without guidance they may not know how to handle these situations. For example, schools could organise group activities where students practise trust and respect and learn to cooperate. These early lessons can help children build healthier relationships throughout their lives.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the most important social skills come from family and daily experience rather than formal lessons at school. Parents play a bigger role in teaching children how to bond with someone and show trust and respect. However, schools can still help by creating environments where children naturally make new friends, for example through team sports or art clubs. In this way, school supports friendship without needing a specific subject for it.

Câu 7Do you think it is easy to make friends in a new place, such as a new city or new workplace? Why?

ANêu ý kiến về mức độ dễ/khó khi kết bạn ở môi trường mới.

RGiải thích những yếu tố ảnh hưởng: tính cách, môi trường.

EVí dụ từ trường học mới, bệnh viện, hay thành phố mới.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Personally, I find it quite challenging to make new friends in a new environment, especially for adults. Unlike children who share classrooms and naturally develop common interests, adults often have limited time and opportunities to meet people. For example, when doctors start working at a new hospital, they are usually very busy and may only have short breaks to interact with colleagues. It takes more effort and time to turn a colleague into a close friend.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think making friends in a new place is easier than people expect, as long as you have a positive attitude. Shared experiences, like starting a new job at the same time as others, naturally help people bond with someone quickly. For instance, when I joined a new study group, we all faced the same challenges, which gave us common interests and made conversation easy. Being open and friendly is the most important thing when trying to expand your social circle.

Câu 8How important is it to have friends who share the same interests as you?

ANêu mức độ quan trọng của việc có sở thích chung với bạn bè.

RGiải thích tại sao sở thích chung giúp ích hoặc không bắt buộc.

EVí dụ về hoạt động chung hoặc bạn bè có sở thích khác.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think having common interests with friends is very helpful because it gives you natural things to talk about and do together. When friends enjoy the same hobbies, it is easier to spend time together and bond with someone more deeply. For example, my closest friends and I all love hiking, and our trips together have created some of my best memories. Shared activities make the friendship feel more meaningful and enjoyable.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While common interests are useful, I do not think they are essential for a strong friendship. Sometimes, friends with different hobbies can actually learn a lot from each other and make the relationship more interesting. For instance, one of my best friends is very interested in music while I prefer sports, but we still bond with someone over our shared values of trust and respect. I believe personality and character matter more than having the same hobbies.

Câu 9Do you think men and women approach friendship differently? How?

ANêu ý kiến về sự khác biệt trong cách nam và nữ kết bạn.

RGiải thích lý do dựa trên xã hội hoặc tâm lý.

EVí dụ về cách giao tiếp hoặc hoạt động của bạn bè nam/nữ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my observation, men and women do tend to approach friendship differently. Women are often more likely to share feelings and provide emotional support to each other through conversation, while men tend to bond with someone through shared activities like sports or games. For example, a group of male friends might watch football together without talking deeply, but still feel a strong connection. These differences are not rules, but they are common patterns I have noticed.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think the differences between male and female friendships are becoming less significant in modern society. Both men and women now value emotional support, honesty, and trust and respect in their friendships. For instance, in my medical school class, both male and female students formed strong support networks based on mutual encouragement rather than gender. I believe the quality of a friendship depends more on the individuals than on whether they are male or female.

Câu 10How can a busy person, such as a doctor or student, find time to maintain friendships?

AGợi ý cách người bận rộn duy trì tình bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao việc dành thời gian là cần thiết dù bận.

EVí dụ cụ thể về thói quen hoặc cách sắp xếp thời gian.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Even for very busy people like doctors or medical students, maintaining a support network of friends is important for mental health. The key is to keep in touch regularly through small but consistent efforts, such as sending a short message or making a brief phone call during a break. For example, I try to send a voice message to a close friend every week, even when I am on a busy hospital rotation. Small gestures like this show that you care and help long-term friendships survive.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe busy professionals should plan social time just like they plan work tasks, treating it as a priority rather than an optional extra. Scheduling a regular meet-up, even once a month, can be enough to maintain a long-term friendship and provide valuable emotional support. For instance, a group of doctor friends I know has a standing dinner every first Sunday of the month, and this simple habit has kept them close friends for over ten years. Routine and commitment are the solutions.

Câu 11Do you think childhood friends are more important than friends made in adulthood? Why?

ASo sánh tình bạn thời thơ ấu và tình bạn khi trưởng thành.

RGiải thích điểm mạnh của mỗi loại tình bạn.

EVí dụ từ trải nghiệm cá nhân hoặc quan sát xã hội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think childhood friends hold a special place because they know you before you had any social pressure or responsibilities. The shared memories and experiences from a young age create a unique bond with someone that is very hard to replace. For example, a childhood friend often knows your family, your old habits, and your true personality, which makes the relationship feel very authentic. This depth of history is something adult friendships often take much longer to build.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While childhood friends are precious, I believe friends made in adulthood can be equally important. Adult friendships are often based on common interests and shared values that you have consciously chosen, rather than just circumstances like living in the same neighbourhood. For instance, colleagues who work together through difficult challenges, like a demanding medical training programme, can develop an extremely strong support network built on real respect and trust and respect. I value both types for different reasons.

Câu 12Should people try to be friends with their colleagues at work? Why or why not?

ANêu ý kiến về việc có nên kết bạn với đồng nghiệp hay không.

RGiải thích lợi ích hoặc rủi ro của tình bạn nơi công sở.

EVí dụ từ môi trường bệnh viện hoặc nơi làm việc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think becoming friends with colleagues is very beneficial, especially in a high-pressure environment like a hospital. When coworkers are also friends, they provide better emotional support to each other, communicate more openly, and work together more effectively as a team. For example, a nursing team that has a genuine bond with someone outside of work hours is likely to be more cooperative and caring during difficult shifts. Friendly workplaces tend to have higher job satisfaction.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While workplace friendships can be positive, I think people should be careful about mixing professional and personal relationships. Sometimes, when close friends work together, it can be hard to give honest feedback or make impartial decisions, which can affect work quality. For instance, a supervisor who is also a close personal friend might find it difficult to address a colleague's mistakes fairly. I believe it is fine to have a friendly atmosphere, but maintaining clear professional boundaries is equally important.

Câu 13How does having good friends affect a person's health and well-being?

ANêu ảnh hưởng tích cực của tình bạn đến sức khỏe.

RGiải thích cơ chế: giảm stress, tăng hạnh phúc.

EDẫn ví dụ thực tế hoặc kiến thức y học cơ bản.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Research consistently shows that having strong friendships has a positive effect on both mental and physical health. Friends provide emotional support during stressful times, which helps reduce anxiety and prevents feelings of isolation. For example, patients who have a strong support network of friends tend to recover more quickly from illness because they feel more motivated and less alone. As a medical student, I find it very relevant that long-term friendships are linked to lower rates of depression and even longer life expectancy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe good friends are one of the most important factors in a person's overall well-being. When you have close friends who you trust and respect, you are more likely to share your problems rather than keeping them inside, which reduces stress significantly. For instance, doctors who have a reliable support network among their peers tend to handle the emotional burden of their work much better than those who face it alone. A sense of belonging that comes from friendship is truly essential for mental health.

Câu 14Do you think it is possible to have a genuine friendship with someone from a very different background or culture?

ANêu quan điểm về tình bạn giữa người có nền tảng văn hóa khác nhau.

RGiải thích những yếu tố giúp vượt qua sự khác biệt.

EVí dụ về bạn bè quốc tế hoặc giao lưu văn hóa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, I believe genuine friendship can develop between people from very different backgrounds. What matters most in any friendship is trust and respect, honesty, and a willingness to understand the other person's perspective, not a shared cultural background. For example, during an international medical conference, many doctors make new friends with colleagues from other countries and bond with someone deeply over their shared passion for medicine. Differences in culture can actually make a friendship richer and more educational.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think cross-cultural friendships are possible but require more patience and effort than friendships between people with common interests and similar backgrounds. Misunderstandings can sometimes arise from different communication styles or cultural values. For instance, a Vietnamese doctor making friends with a Western colleague may need time to adjust to different levels of directness in conversation. However, once both people commit to trust and respect and open communication, these friendships can be extremely meaningful and rewarding.

Câu 15How should people handle conflicts or disagreements with their friends?

ANêu cách xử lý xung đột trong tình bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao cách đó giúp tình bạn bền vững hơn.

EVí dụ về tình huống cụ thể và cách giải quyết.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the best way to handle a conflict with a friend is to talk about the problem honestly and calmly as soon as possible. Avoiding the issue or keeping feelings inside usually makes things worse and can seriously damage a long-term friendship. For example, if a friend cancels plans repeatedly, it is better to explain how that makes you feel rather than suddenly stopping contact. Dealing with problems openly shows trust and respect and usually makes the bond with someone even stronger in the end.

Trả lời mẫu 2

When a conflict arises with a friend, I believe it is important to first take some time to calm down before having a conversation. Reacting immediately when emotions are high can lead to saying things you regret, which can harm a close friendship. For instance, I once had a disagreement with a colleague and friend; after waiting a day and thinking about both sides, our conversation was much more productive and we were able to maintain trust and respect. Patience and empathy are the most important tools for resolving friendship conflicts.

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Part 1 · The Internet

Câu 1How often do you use the Internet?

ANêu tần suất bạn dùng Internet (mỗi ngày, mỗi tuần...).

RGiải thích tại sao bạn dùng nhiều hoặc ít.

ECho ví dụ về thời điểm cụ thể bạn thường dùng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I use the Internet every day, usually for several hours. As a medical student, I need it to study and keep up with new information. For example, I browse the web every morning before class to read about medical topics.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I use the Internet almost all the time because my studies require it. I cannot imagine a day without online resources for my coursework. In the evening, I usually stream videos or chat with friends after studying.

Câu 2What do you usually use it for?

ANêu mục đích chính bạn dùng Internet.

RGiải thích tại sao mục đích đó quan trọng với bạn.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về việc bạn làm trên mạng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I mainly use the Internet to search for information for my medical studies. It helps me find articles and textbooks very quickly. For example, I often download research papers from medical websites to prepare for my assignments.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I use it for many things, but mostly for online learning and social media. I watch lectures online and then relax by checking my social media accounts. Sometimes I also do online shopping when I am too busy to go to the store.

Câu 3Do you think the Internet is useful for students? Why?

ANêu ý kiến của bạn (có ích hay không).

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn nghĩ vậy.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về cách Internet giúp ích cho sinh viên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think the Internet is very useful for students. It gives us access to online resources and reliable sources of information at any time. For example, I can search for information about diseases and treatments without going to the library.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe the Internet is useful, but students must use it wisely. It offers a lot of online learning opportunities that help us study more effectively. However, it is easy to waste time online on entertainment instead of studying.

Câu 4How long do you spend on the Internet each day?

ANêu số giờ bạn dùng Internet mỗi ngày.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn dùng nhiều hay ít như vậy.

ECho ví dụ về những gì bạn làm trong thời gian đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I spend about five or six hours on the Internet every day. This is mainly because my medical studies require a lot of online learning and research. For example, I spend at least two hours searching for information for my assignments each evening.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to limit my time to around three hours a day because I do not want to develop internet addiction. I use the first hour for studying and online resources, and the rest for relaxing. Sometimes I stream videos for about an hour before bed.

Câu 5What websites or apps do you use most often?

ANêu tên các trang web hoặc ứng dụng bạn dùng nhiều nhất.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích dùng chúng.

ECho ví dụ về cách bạn sử dụng một trong số chúng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I use YouTube and Google most often because they are very helpful for studying. I can find online resources and medical lectures on YouTube very easily. For example, I watched a video about surgery techniques last week to prepare for my exam.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I mostly use Facebook and medical databases like PubMed. Facebook helps me stay connected with my classmates and share study materials. I use PubMed to find reliable sources for my research papers.

Câu 6Do you prefer to shop online or in a physical store? Why?

ANêu sở thích của bạn (mua online hay tại cửa hàng).

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích cách đó hơn.

ECho ví dụ về lần gần đây bạn mua sắm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer online shopping because it saves me a lot of time. As a busy student, I do not have time to visit many stores. For example, last month I bought all my textbooks online and they were delivered to my door.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer shopping in a physical store because I like to see the product before buying it. I only do online shopping for things like books or electronics that I am already sure about. For example, I always buy clothes in the store but download digital textbooks online.

Câu 7Do you use the Internet to communicate with friends and family? How?

ANêu bạn có dùng Internet để liên lạc không và dùng cách nào.

RGiải thích tại sao phương tiện đó tiện lợi với bạn.

ECho ví dụ về người bạn thường liên lạc và cách liên lạc.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I use the Internet to stay connected with my family every day. I usually make a video call with my parents in the evening because they live far from the city. It feels much better than just sending a text message.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I use social media and messaging apps to communicate with my friends. We have a group chat where we share notes and study tips. Sometimes we have a group video call to discuss difficult topics together before an exam.

Câu 8Have you ever taken an online course? What was it about?

ANêu bạn đã từng học online chưa và học môn gì.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn khóa học đó.

ECho ví dụ về điều bạn học được từ khóa học đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have taken an online learning course about English for medical purposes. I chose it because I needed to improve my vocabulary for my studies. I learned many useful terms and now I feel more confident when I read medical journals.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have not taken a formal online learning course, but I often watch free lectures on YouTube. I use these online resources to understand difficult topics in anatomy and physiology. For example, I watched a whole series about the human heart last semester.

Câu 9Do you think spending too much time on the Internet is harmful? Why?

ANêu ý kiến của bạn về tác hại của việc dùng Internet quá nhiều.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao nó có hại hoặc không.

ECho ví dụ về tác hại cụ thể mà bạn biết hoặc đã thấy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think spending too much time online can be very harmful. It can lead to internet addiction and cause problems with sleep and concentration. For example, I know some classmates who waste time online until midnight and then feel tired during class.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think it depends on how you use it. Using online resources for studying is always beneficial. However, if you waste time online watching unrelated videos for hours, it can affect your health and academic performance.

Câu 10Do you use the Internet for entertainment? What do you enjoy watching or reading?

ANêu bạn có dùng Internet để giải trí không và bạn thích xem hay đọc gì.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn thích nội dung đó.

ECho ví dụ về nội dung cụ thể bạn đã xem hoặc đọc gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I enjoy using the Internet for entertainment after a long study day. I like to stream videos on Netflix, especially medical dramas because they are both relaxing and educational. Last weekend I watched a series about emergency room doctors and found it very interesting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I use the Internet to read news and watch short videos on social media. I enjoy reading health articles from reliable sources like the WHO website. It helps me relax while still learning something useful.

Câu 11Do you think the Internet has changed the way people learn? How?

ANêu ý kiến về việc Internet đã thay đổi cách học của mọi người.

RGiải thích cụ thể sự thay đổi đó như thế nào.

ECho ví dụ về cách bạn hoặc người khác học khác đi nhờ Internet.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe the Internet has changed learning completely. Online learning allows students to study at any time and from any place, which is very flexible. For example, I can search for information on any topic within seconds, whereas before I would need to go to the library.

Trả lời mẫu 2

The Internet has made online resources available to everyone, not just people near big universities. I think this is a very positive change because it gives equal opportunities to learn. For example, I can download lectures from top universities abroad and study the same content as their students.

Câu 12Have you ever had a problem because of the Internet, for example fake news or losing personal data?

ANêu bạn đã từng gặp vấn đề liên quan đến Internet chưa.

RGiải thích vấn đề đó xảy ra như thế nào.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về tình huống bạn đã gặp.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I once read some medical information online that turned out to be from an unreliable website. I realized the importance of always checking reliable sources after that experience. Now I only use the Internet for medical facts if the website is from a trusted organisation.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I have not had a serious problem, but I sometimes see fake news on social media about health topics. It worries me because some people believe these stories without checking the facts. I always try to search for information from official and reliable sources before sharing anything.

Câu 13Do you think life would be difficult without the Internet? Why?

ANêu ý kiến về việc cuộc sống có khó khăn hơn không nếu không có Internet.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao Internet quan trọng hoặc không quan trọng đến vậy.

ECho ví dụ về một điều cụ thể sẽ rất khó nếu không có Internet.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think life would be much more difficult without the Internet, especially for students. I rely on online resources and the ability to search for information every single day. Without it, I could not access my university's online library or submit assignments on time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Life would definitely be harder without the Internet, but I think people would manage. Before the Internet existed, students still studied successfully using books and libraries. However, I would personally miss the ability to stay connected with friends through social media and to video call my family.

Câu 14Do you check your phone or the Internet first thing in the morning?

ANêu thói quen buổi sáng của bạn có liên quan đến điện thoại hay Internet không.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn làm vậy hoặc tại sao bạn không làm vậy.

ECho ví dụ về điều đầu tiên bạn thường xem khi thức dậy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I usually check my phone first thing in the morning to read messages from my classmates. We often share study schedules and important notes through our group chat on social media. I think it is a useful habit as long as I do not waste time online too long before getting up.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try not to check my phone immediately when I wake up because I want to start the day calmly. I have read that checking social media first thing in the morning can increase stress. Instead, I prefer to eat breakfast first and then browse the web for news or study materials.

Câu 15Would you like to reduce the amount of time you spend on the Internet? Why or why not?

ANêu bạn có muốn giảm thời gian dùng Internet không.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn muốn hoặc không muốn giảm.

ECho ví dụ về điều bạn sẽ làm thay thế hoặc điều bạn lo ngại nếu giảm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would like to reduce the time I spend on entertainment online. I sometimes feel that I waste time online watching videos when I should be studying or resting properly. If I could cut down my social media use by one hour a day, I think I would feel less tired.

Trả lời mẫu 2

No, I do not want to reduce my Internet use because most of it is for studying. I rely heavily on online learning and online resources to keep up with my medical programme. However, I do try to balance my time so that I do not develop internet addiction.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Shopping habits

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • go shoppingđi mua sắm
  • shopping malltrung tâm thương mại
  • online shoppingmua sắm trực tuyến
  • window shoppingđi xem hàng mà không mua
  • bargainmón hàng giá rẻ / mặc cả
  • brand namehàng có thương hiệu nổi tiếng
  • compare pricesso sánh giá cả
  • daily necessitiesđồ dùng hàng ngày thiết yếu
  • budgetngân sách / số tiền dự kiến chi tiêu
  • impulse buyingmua hàng bốc đồng, không có kế hoạch
  • local marketchợ địa phương
  • delivery servicedịch vụ giao hàng
  • sale / discountgiảm giá
  • shopping listdanh sách mua sắm

Câu 1How often do you go shopping?

ANói tần suất bạn đi mua sắm (mỗi tuần, mỗi tháng...).

RGiải thích lý do tần suất đó (bận học, gần chợ...).

ECho ví dụ lần gần nhất bạn đi mua sắm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually go shopping about once a week, mainly on weekends. Because I am very busy with my medical studies during the week, I do not have much free time. Last Saturday, I went to the local market near my dormitory to buy food and daily necessities. I think once a week is enough for me.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I only go shopping two or three times a month because I prefer to buy things online shopping instead. It saves me a lot of time and I can compare prices easily on my phone. For example, last month I ordered most of my personal care products from an app. The delivery service brought everything to my door within two days.

Câu 2What do you usually buy?

ANói những loại hàng bạn thường mua nhất.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn hay mua những thứ đó.

EĐưa ra một ví dụ cụ thể về món đồ bạn mua gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually buy daily necessities such as food, toiletries, and stationery. As a student, I need to keep my costs low, so I mostly buy basic things rather than luxury items. For example, last week I bought rice, vegetables, and some medicine from the local market. I rarely spend money on clothes or entertainment.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I often buy medical textbooks and study materials because I am a post-graduate student. I also buy daily necessities like food and cleaning products. Recently, I found a great bargain on a second-hand anatomy textbook online. I always check if there is a sale / discount before I buy anything that is not essential.

Câu 3Do you like shopping alone or with others? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích đi một mình hay đi cùng người khác.

RGiải thích lý do bạn có sở thích đó.

EKể một trải nghiệm cụ thể khi đi mua sắm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer go shopping alone because I can make decisions faster and focus on what I really need. When I shop with friends, we often spend too much time talking and I end up impulse buying things I do not need. For example, last time I went with a friend to a shopping mall, I spent twice my usual budget. So now I mostly go by myself.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy shopping with my close friends or family more than shopping alone. Shopping together is more fun and my friends often help me compare prices or choose better products. For instance, my roommate helped me find a good bargain on a winter jacket last month. Having another opinion makes me feel more confident about my choices.

Câu 4Do you prefer shopping online or in a physical store?

ANói rõ bạn thích mua hàng online hay tại cửa hàng.

RGiải thích ưu điểm của lựa chọn bạn thích.

ECho ví dụ về lần mua sắm theo cách đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer online shopping because it is much more convenient for a busy student like me. I can order things at any time, even late at night after studying. Last week, I bought a new stethoscope from an online store and used a discount code to save some money. The delivery service arrived the next morning, which was very fast.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer shopping in a physical store because I like to see and touch the products before I buy them. For clothes and shoes especially, I need to try them on to check the size and quality. I also enjoy the experience of walking around a shopping mall with a cup of coffee. However, I always check my budget before I enter any shop.

Câu 5Do you make a shopping list before you go to the store?

ANói bạn có lập danh sách mua sắm trước hay không.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn làm hoặc không làm vậy.

EKể điều gì xảy ra khi bạn có hoặc không có danh sách.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always make a shopping list on my phone before I go to the store. I do this because I have a tight budget and I want to avoid impulse buying unnecessary things. One time I forgot my list and I bought too many snacks instead of the vegetables I actually needed. Now I never leave without checking my list first.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I rarely make a shopping list and I usually just remember what I need in my head. I do not buy many different things, so it is not too difficult to remember. However, I sometimes forget important items like soap or toothpaste. Perhaps I should start writing a list to help me compare prices and plan my spending better.

Câu 6How much money do you usually spend when you go shopping?

ACho biết bạn thường chi bao nhiêu tiền mỗi lần mua sắm.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chi nhiều hoặc ít như vậy.

ENêu ví dụ về lần bạn chi nhiều hoặc ít hơn thông thường.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually spend around 300,000 to 500,000 dong each time I go shopping for daily necessities. I try to keep within my budget because my income as a student is limited. Sometimes I find a bargain at the local market and spend less than expected. But if I need to buy books or equipment for my studies, the amount can be much higher.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to limit my spending to about 200,000 dong per week on food and basic supplies. I always look for a sale / discount to make sure I get the best value. However, last month I went over my budget because I needed to buy several new medical reference books. I learned that I need to plan more carefully and separate my study costs from my living costs.

Câu 7Have you ever bought something and then regretted it?

ANói có hay không và mô tả món đồ đó.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn hối hận về việc mua đó.

EKể điều bạn học được từ trải nghiệm đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I once bought an expensive brand name sports bag that I saw on sale at a shopping mall. At the time I thought it was a great bargain, but later I realized I already had a perfectly good bag at home. I had made an impulse buying decision without thinking carefully. Since then, I always wait one day before buying anything expensive.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really, because I am quite careful with my budget and I usually only buy things I truly need. I always compare prices on several websites before I spend money on anything. However, there was one time I ordered a cheap phone case through online shopping and the quality was very poor. I learned that very low prices do not always mean a good deal.

Câu 8Do you enjoy window shopping, or do you only shop when you need something?

ANói bạn có thích đi xem hàng không mục đích không, hay chỉ mua khi cần.

RGiải thích lý do cho thói quen của bạn.

EKể một lần bạn đi xem hàng gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I sometimes enjoy window shopping at the shopping mall on weekends just to relax after a stressful week of studying. I do not always buy anything, but I like to see what new products are available. Last Sunday I spent an hour looking at electronic gadgets without spending a single dong. It is a nice way to unwind without affecting my budget.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am a very practical person, so I only go shopping when I actually need something specific. I do not really enjoy window shopping because it can lead to impulse buying and wasted time. If I see something I like, I write it down and think about it for a few days before deciding. This habit helps me stay within my budget every month.

Câu 9Do you pay with cash or use a card or mobile payment when you shop?

ANói bạn thường thanh toán bằng hình thức nào.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn cách đó.

ECho ví dụ về lần thanh toán gần đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually pay with cash when I shop at the local market because most small vendors there do not accept cards. I find cash easier to control my budget because I can see exactly how much I am spending. Last weekend I brought exactly 200,000 dong to the market and bought all my vegetables and fruit without going over. It is a simple but effective way to manage money.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to use mobile payment apps when I do online shopping because it is faster and safer. Many apps also offer extra sale / discount coupons if you pay through them. For example, last week I got 15% off my order just by using a specific payment app. I still use cash at the local market, but for most other purchases I go cashless.

Câu 10Do you think shopping is a hobby or just a necessity for you?

ANói shopping với bạn là sở thích hay chỉ là nhu cầu thiết yếu.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn cảm thấy như vậy.

ECho ví dụ về cách bạn tiếp cận việc mua sắm trong cuộc sống hàng ngày.

Trả lời mẫu 1

For me, shopping is mostly a necessity rather than a hobby. As a medical student, I have very little free time, so I go shopping quickly and only buy daily necessities. I do not find browsing shops very exciting because I am always thinking about my studies. I think when I finish my degree and have a stable income, I might start to enjoy shopping more.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually consider online shopping to be a small hobby of mine. I enjoy browsing different websites, reading reviews, and looking for the best bargain or sale / discount. It is relaxing and I sometimes spend an hour just exploring new products even if I do not buy anything. Of course, I have to be careful not to overspend beyond my budget.

Câu 11Do you prefer to buy brand-name products or cheaper alternatives?

ANói bạn thích hàng thương hiệu hay hàng rẻ hơn.

RGiải thích lý do cho sự lựa chọn của bạn.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về loại sản phẩm cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I generally prefer cheaper alternatives over brand name products because I am on a limited student budget. For most daily necessities like shampoo or notebooks, the cheaper version works just as well. However, for medical equipment like a stethoscope, I do invest in a reliable brand name because quality really matters. I always try to compare prices before making my final decision.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I like to buy brand name products when I can find them at a bargain price during a sale / discount event. I believe some brand name items last longer and are better value in the long run. For example, I bought a brand name backpack two years ago and it is still in perfect condition. I think quality is worth paying a little more for sometimes.

Câu 12Where do you usually go shopping - a supermarket, market, or small shop?

ANêu địa điểm bạn thường mua sắm nhất.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn nơi đó.

EMô tả một lần mua sắm cụ thể ở đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually shop at the local market near my school because the prices are lower than supermarkets and the vegetables are very fresh. I can also compare prices between different stalls easily. Last Thursday morning, I bought enough food for the whole week for just 150,000 dong. I think local market shopping is the most economical option for students.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to shop at a supermarket because everything is organized and clearly priced, which makes it easier to manage my budget. I can find all my daily necessities in one place without wasting time. On weekends, I sometimes visit the shopping mall to buy things that are not available at a regular supermarket. I also like the air-conditioning, especially in summer.

Câu 13Do you look for sales or discounts when you shop?

ANói bạn có tìm kiếm khuyến mãi hay giảm giá không.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn quan tâm hoặc không quan tâm đến điều này.

EKể một lần bạn tiết kiệm được tiền nhờ khuyến mãi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always look for a sale / discount before I buy anything, especially for online shopping. I follow several apps that send me notifications when my favourite products are on sale. Last month I waited two weeks for a discount on a book I wanted and saved 30% of the original price. Finding a good bargain feels very satisfying.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not spend much time looking for sale / discount offers because it can take too long and distract me from studying. I would rather pay the normal price and save time. However, if I notice a bargain while I am already in a store, I will take advantage of it. I think time is more valuable than a small saving for me right now.

Câu 14Has your shopping habit changed since you became a student?

ANói thói quen mua sắm của bạn đã thay đổi như thế nào kể từ khi đi học.

RGiải thích nguyên nhân dẫn đến sự thay đổi đó.

ESo sánh cách bạn mua sắm bây giờ với trước đây.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my shopping habits have changed a lot since I became a post-graduate student. Before, I used to go to the shopping mall with my family and buy brand name clothes without worrying about the price. Now I have a strict budget and I only buy daily necessities. I do much more online shopping to save time and I always compare prices carefully.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My habits have changed mainly because I now live away from home and must manage my own money. In the past, my parents bought everything, so I never needed to make a shopping list or think about my budget. Now I plan my spending each month and look for the best bargain I can find. I think this experience has made me much more responsible with money.

Câu 15Do you think people shop too much these days? Why?

AĐưa ra ý kiến của bạn về việc mua sắm quá mức trong xã hội hiện nay.

RGiải thích lý do bạn nghĩ như vậy.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể từ cuộc sống hoặc quan sát của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people do shop too much these days, especially because online shopping makes it so easy to buy things at any time. Social media shows us many advertisements that encourage impulse buying and make us want things we do not really need. For example, some of my friends buy new clothes every week just because they are on sale / discount. I believe we should think more carefully before spending money.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I do not think everyone shops too much, but I agree that impulse buying is a growing problem for young people. Easy access to online shopping apps and constant sale / discount notifications can make it hard to control spending. However, I personally try to stick to my budget and only buy daily necessities. I think education about money management is very important for students like us.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Free time is important for everyone

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • free timethời gian rảnh rỗi
  • relax and rechargethư giãn và nạp lại năng lượng
  • reduce stressgiảm căng thẳng
  • mental healthsức khỏe tâm thần
  • pursue a hobbytheo đuổi sở thích
  • personal interestsở thích cá nhân
  • family bondinggắn kết gia đình
  • quality timethời gian chất lượng bên nhau
  • strengthen relationshipscủng cố các mối quan hệ
  • physical healthsức khỏe thể chất
  • stay activeduy trì sự vận động
  • overall well-beingsức khỏe toàn diện
  • feel refreshedcảm thấy sảng khoái
  • daily routinethói quen hàng ngày
  • take a breaknghỉ ngơi một chút
  • productivenăng suất, hiệu quả

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I strongly agree that free time is important for everyone. Without enough rest and personal time, people cannot function well in their daily routine. Let me explain by giving some reasons and examples. First of all, free time helps us relax and recharge. When we take a break from work or study, our minds become calmer and we feel refreshed. For example, after a long week of studying, I usually listen to music for an hour, and it really helps me reduce stress and protect my mental health. Another reason is that free time allows people to pursue a hobby and develop their personal interest. For instance, many of my classmates enjoy drawing or playing guitar in their spare time, which makes them happier and more productive the next day. In addition, spending free time with family is very valuable. Quality time together, like having meals or watching films, helps strengthen relationships and creates warm memories. I think this kind of family bonding is something we should never ignore. On top of that, free time is also a chance to exercise and stay active, which improves our physical health and overall well-being. To sum up, free time is not a luxury - it is a real necessity for a balanced and healthy life.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Free time (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • free timethời gian rảnh rỗi
  • leisure activitiescác hoạt động giải trí
  • work-life balancecân bằng giữa công việc và cuộc sống
  • spend timedành thời gian
  • outdoor activitieshoạt động ngoài trời
  • social mediamạng xã hội
  • stress reliefgiải tỏa căng thẳng
  • personal developmentphát triển bản thân
  • physical exercisetập thể dục thể thao
  • quality timethời gian chất lượng bên gia đình/bạn bè
  • hobbiessở thích cá nhân
  • entertainmentgiải trí
  • rechargenạp lại năng lượng
  • well-beingsức khỏe tinh thần và thể chất

Câu 1Do people today have enough free time?

ANêu ý kiến của bạn: có hay không có đủ thời gian rảnh.

RGiải thích lý do: công việc bận rộn, áp lực cuộc sống hiện đại.

EĐưa ví dụ cụ thể: bác sĩ, nhân viên văn phòng làm thêm giờ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think most people today have enough free time. Modern life is very busy, and many people spend long hours at work or school, leaving little time for themselves. For example, doctors and nurses often work extra shifts and have very little time to recharge after a long day. I believe this lack of work-life balance is a serious problem for well-being.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people today actually have more free time than before because of modern technology and machines that save time on daily tasks. However, many people choose to fill that time with social media or extra work instead of real leisure activities. For instance, many young people spend hours scrolling on their phones rather than doing something meaningful. So while the time exists, it is not always used well.

Câu 2How do young people spend their free time?

ANêu các hoạt động phổ biến mà giới trẻ thường làm khi rảnh.

RGiải thích tại sao họ chọn những hoạt động đó: tiện lợi, thú vị, xã hội.

EĐưa ví dụ cụ thể: xem phim, chơi game, gặp bạn bè.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think young people today mostly spend time on digital activities during their free time. Many of them enjoy watching videos, playing games online, or browsing social media for hours. For example, most of my classmates spend their evenings on their phones after a busy day of studying. While this can be relaxing, I think it is important to also include some physical exercise or time with family.

Trả lời mẫu 2

From what I observe, young people spend their free time in quite varied ways. Some prefer outdoor activities like cycling or playing sports, while others enjoy creative hobbies such as drawing or cooking. In my university, many students join clubs and use their spare time for personal development by learning new skills. I think this is a very healthy and productive way to spend time.

Câu 3Is free time more important now than in the past?

ANêu ý kiến: quan trọng hơn hay không, so sánh xưa và nay.

RGiải thích lý do: áp lực công việc cao hơn, nhu cầu sức khỏe tinh thần nhiều hơn.

ESo sánh cuộc sống ngày nay và trước đây: công nghệ, nhịp sống nhanh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe free time is more important now than in the past because modern life is much more stressful. People face greater pressure at work and in daily life, so they need time to recharge and take care of their well-being. In the past, life moved more slowly and work was often physical, so people could rest naturally. Today, mental stress is a bigger concern, and leisure activities help people manage that stress.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not sure free time is more important now than before. In the past, people also needed rest and time with family, and quality time with loved ones has always been valuable. However, I think the way we use free time has changed a lot because of technology and entertainment options. Today people have more choices but sometimes feel more tired because they cannot truly switch off from work, so stress relief activities have become more necessary.

Câu 4Why do some people find it difficult to enjoy their free time?

ANêu lý do tại sao một số người không biết cách tận hưởng thời gian rảnh.

RGiải thích: quen làm việc, cảm giác tội lỗi khi nghỉ ngơi, thiếu sở thích.

EVí dụ: người làm việc nhiều không biết phải làm gì khi rảnh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think some people find it hard to enjoy their free time because they are so used to being busy. When they finally have time to relax, they feel guilty for not working or studying, so they cannot truly rest. For example, some medical students I know feel anxious when they are not studying, even during weekends. I believe it is important to understand that stress relief and rest are also productive for long-term well-being.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, some people struggle to enjoy leisure activities because they have never developed any real hobbies. Without an interest to turn to, free time can feel empty or boring. For instance, a person who has only focused on work may not know how to spend time doing something fun or creative. I think it helps to try different outdoor activities or social events to find what you enjoy.

Câu 5Should employers give workers more free time? Why or why not?

ANêu ý kiến rõ ràng: nên hay không nên cho nhân viên nhiều thời gian rảnh hơn.

RGiải thích lý do: năng suất tăng, sức khỏe tốt hơn, ít nghỉ ốm hơn.

EVí dụ: một số công ty cho nhân viên nghỉ ngắn trong ngày, kết quả tốt hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly think employers should give workers more free time because it directly improves work-life balance and productivity. When employees have time to rest and do leisure activities, they return to work with more energy and focus. For example, some companies in Japan tried a four-day work week and found that workers produced better results. I believe that stress relief leads to better performance, so more free time benefits both the worker and the company.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think giving more free time to workers is a good idea, but it depends on the type of job. In some fields like medicine, it is not always possible to reduce hours without affecting patient care. However, employers can still support well-being by encouraging short breaks, physical exercise during the day, or flexible schedules. I think the key is finding a good work-life balance that works for both sides without reducing the quality of work.

Câu 6How has technology changed the way people spend their free time?

ANêu những thay đổi do công nghệ mang lại trong hoạt động giải trí.

RGiải thích: dễ tiếp cận hơn, nhiều lựa chọn hơn nhưng cũng ít vận động hơn.

ESo sánh trước và sau khi có smartphone, streaming, mạng xã hội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Technology has completely changed how people spend their free time. Before smartphones, people were more likely to do outdoor activities, read books, or socialize in person. Now, most leisure activities happen online, from watching streaming shows to scrolling social media. For example, young people today rarely go outside for entertainment when they can find everything on their phone. While this is convenient, I think it reduces physical exercise and real human connection.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think technology has made free time more flexible and interesting because people can now learn new skills, enjoy entertainment, or connect with friends from home. For instance, online platforms allow people to pursue hobbies like cooking, music, or art by watching tutorials anytime. This means personal development can happen even during short breaks. However, I also think people need to be careful not to spend all their free time passively and should still make time for physical exercise.

Câu 7Do you think spending free time alone or with others is more beneficial? Why?

AChọn một trong hai hoặc cả hai và nêu ý kiến rõ ràng.

RGiải thích lợi ích: một mình giúp thư giãn sâu, cùng người khác tăng kết nối.

EVí dụ từ kinh nghiệm bản thân hoặc quan sát.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I personally think spending free time with others is more beneficial because humans need social connection for good well-being. Sharing leisure activities with friends or family creates positive memories and reduces stress more effectively than being alone. For example, having a meal together or playing sports with friends gives me much more energy than watching TV by myself. I believe quality time with people we care about is one of the best forms of stress relief.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, spending some free time alone is actually very important, especially for people with stressful jobs like doctors or students. Being alone allows you to recharge properly without the pressure of social interaction. For instance, I find that reading or taking a quiet walk by myself helps me clear my mind after a long day. Of course, quality time with friends is also important, but I think everyone needs a healthy balance of both.

Câu 8Should children be given more free time instead of extra classes or homework? Why?

ANêu ý kiến: nên hay không nên giảm bài tập và lớp học thêm cho trẻ.

RGiải thích: trẻ cần chơi để phát triển, quá nhiều áp lực ảnh hưởng sức khỏe.

ESo sánh hệ thống giáo dục ở Việt Nam và các nước khác.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe children should have more free time instead of too many extra classes. Children need time to play and explore, as this supports their creativity and emotional well-being. For example, research shows that children who have time for outdoor activities and unstructured play develop better social and problem-solving skills. I think hobbies and play are just as important as academic study for a child's personal development.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I understand why parents want children to attend extra classes, but I think children also need free time to rest and enjoy leisure activities. Too much pressure at a young age can cause stress and reduce their love of learning. For instance, in Finland, children have more free time and shorter school days but still perform very well academically. I believe a good work-life balance should start from childhood so children learn to manage their time well.

Câu 9Why do some people prefer active leisure activities while others prefer passive ones?

AGiải thích sự khác biệt về tính cách và nhu cầu dẫn đến lựa chọn khác nhau.

RLý do: tính cách hướng nội/hướng ngoại, mức độ mệt mỏi sau công việc.

EVí dụ: người thích thể thao so với người thích đọc sách hay xem phim.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think people choose different types of leisure activities based on their personality and energy levels. Extroverted people often enjoy active hobbies like team sports, dancing, or socializing because these activities make them feel energized. For example, my colleague always goes running or plays volleyball after work to recharge. On the other hand, more introverted people may prefer quieter activities like reading or cooking as a form of stress relief.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, the type of work someone does strongly influences how they prefer to spend their free time. People who have physically demanding jobs often prefer passive entertainment like watching films to rest their bodies. However, people who sit at a desk all day may feel the need for outdoor activities and physical exercise to compensate. I think both types of leisure activities are valuable as long as they help a person maintain good well-being.

Câu 10How important is it for families to spend free time together?

ANêu tầm quan trọng của việc dành thời gian rảnh cùng gia đình.

RGiải thích: gắn kết gia đình, trẻ phát triển tốt hơn, giảm stress.

EVí dụ: bữa ăn gia đình, đi chơi cuối tuần cùng nhau.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think quality time with family is extremely important for everyone's well-being. When families spend time together, they build stronger relationships and create a support system that helps each member deal with life's challenges. For example, having meals together as a family or going on weekend trips creates positive memories and keeps communication open. I believe that leisure activities shared with family are some of the most meaningful ways to use free time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that family free time is important, but I also think the quality of the time matters more than the quantity. Even a short shared activity like cooking a meal together or watching a film can strengthen family bonds. For instance, in my family, we try to have one quality time activity every weekend, which helps us stay close despite our busy schedules. I think as long as families make some effort to spend time together regularly, the relationship will remain strong.

Câu 11Do you think people in cities and people in the countryside spend their free time differently? Why?

ASo sánh cách người thành thị và nông thôn dành thời gian rảnh.

RGiải thích sự khác biệt: cơ sở vật chất, lối sống, tốc độ cuộc sống.

EVí dụ cụ thể về hoạt động ở thành phố và nông thôn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think people in cities and the countryside spend their free time quite differently due to their environment and available facilities. City dwellers have access to cinemas, gyms, and restaurants, so they tend to enjoy indoor entertainment and social outings. In contrast, people in the countryside are more likely to engage in outdoor activities like farming, fishing, or visiting nature. For example, my relatives in the countryside spend their evenings gardening and socializing with neighbours, which I find very peaceful.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe that while the specific leisure activities may differ, the desire to rest and enjoy free time is the same for both groups. City people may spend their free time in coffee shops or shopping centres because those options are easily available. Rural residents might spend theirs doing physical exercise outdoors or attending community events. I personally think countryside free time is often more natural and relaxing, while city free time offers more variety and entertainment.

Câu 12Should governments invest more in public spaces for leisure, such as parks and sports centres? Why?

ANêu ý kiến: chính phủ có nên đầu tư nhiều hơn vào không gian công cộng không.

RGiải thích lợi ích: sức khỏe cộng đồng, giảm tệ nạn xã hội, tăng chất lượng sống.

EVí dụ: công viên, trung tâm thể thao, không gian văn hóa công cộng.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, I think governments should invest much more in public spaces for leisure activities like parks, sports centres, and walking paths. When people have access to free or affordable spaces for physical exercise and relaxation, their overall well-being improves significantly. For example, cities with well-maintained parks and cycling lanes tend to have healthier and happier residents. I believe providing good infrastructure for free time is one of the best investments a government can make for public health.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that investing in public leisure spaces is beneficial, but I think governments need to consider what types of facilities people actually need and use. For instance, building expensive sports centres in areas where people prefer outdoor activities might not be the best use of resources. I think simple improvements like clean parks, safe walking paths, and community gardens can make a big difference to work-life balance without costing too much. The key is to consult local communities about what kinds of leisure activities they want to enjoy.

Câu 13How does having free time affect a person's mental health?

ANêu tác động của thời gian rảnh đến sức khỏe tinh thần.

RGiải thích cơ chế: giảm căng thẳng, cải thiện tâm trạng, phục hồi năng lượng.

EVí dụ: người không có thời gian rảnh dễ bị kiệt sức, trầm cảm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe that having enough free time has a very positive effect on mental health. When people can step away from work and do leisure activities they enjoy, their stress levels decrease and their mood improves significantly. For example, taking time for hobbies like music or drawing can act as powerful stress relief and help people process difficult emotions. I think well-being is impossible to maintain without regular periods of genuine rest and enjoyment.

Trả lời mẫu 2

From my experience as a medical student, I can see that a lack of free time is directly linked to burnout and poor mental health. When people have no time to recharge, they become exhausted and lose motivation for everything, including work. However, I also think that not all free time has the same effect on mental health. Passively watching TV all day is different from engaging in active leisure activities or quality time with friends, which genuinely improves mood and reduces anxiety.

Câu 14Do you think older people and younger people have different ideas about how to use free time well?

ASo sánh quan điểm của người lớn tuổi và người trẻ về việc sử dụng thời gian rảnh.

RGiải thích sự khác biệt: giá trị, ưu tiên, thói quen theo thế hệ.

EVí dụ: người già thích đọc sách, làm vườn; người trẻ thích mạng xã hội, du lịch.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think there is quite a big difference in how older and younger people view free time. Older generations often prefer calm leisure activities such as reading, gardening, or spending quality time with grandchildren, as they value peace and connection. Younger people, on the other hand, tend to seek excitement and new experiences through travel, social media, or trying new hobbies. For example, my parents enjoy quiet evenings at home while I prefer to go out and explore new places on weekends.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think older and younger people can have surprisingly similar ideas about how to use free time well, even if their specific activities differ. Both groups generally agree that free time should include some physical exercise, social connection, and activities that bring joy. For instance, both my grandmother and I enjoy cooking as a hobby, though she makes traditional dishes and I like to experiment with new recipes.
