I believe the core idea of using free time for personal development and happiness is shared across generations.

Câu 15Should people be more careful about how they spend their free time? Why?

ANêu ý kiến: có nên lên kế hoạch và chú ý hơn đến cách dùng thời gian rảnh không.

RGiải thích: thời gian hữu hạn, lựa chọn hoạt động tốt giúp phát triển bản thân.

ESo sánh người dùng thời gian rảnh có ý thức và người lãng phí thời gian.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do think people should be more thoughtful about how they use their free time, but not in a stressful way. If most of your leisure activities are passive, like scrolling social media for hours, you may feel tired rather than refreshed. For example, replacing some screen time with physical exercise or a creative hobby can greatly improve both mood and personal development. I believe a little planning can help people get much more value from the free time they have.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think it is important to be intentional with free time, but people should also allow themselves to relax without guilt. Sometimes stress relief simply means doing nothing, and that is perfectly healthy too. However, over time, people who consistently use their free time for personal development, outdoor activities, or quality time with others tend to feel more satisfied with life. I think the key is to find a balance between structured hobbies and genuine unplanned rest, so that free time truly helps you recharge.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 9

Part 1: Family · Weekends · Part 2: Learning English brings many advantages · Part 3: Learning English

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Family

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • nuclear familygia đình hạt nhân (bố mẹ và con cái)
  • extended familygia đình mở rộng (bao gồm ông bà, cô chú...)
  • close-knit familygia đình gắn bó, đoàn kết
  • family bondsợi dây gắn kết gia đình
  • spend quality timedành thời gian có ý nghĩa bên nhau
  • get togethertụ họp, sum vầy
  • household choresviệc nhà
  • family gatheringbuổi tụ họp gia đình
  • look up tongưỡng mộ, kính trọng
  • raise a familynuôi dạy/xây dựng gia đình
  • family traditiontruyền thống gia đình
  • only childcon một
  • share responsibilitieschia sẻ trách nhiệm
  • supportive familygia đình luôn ủng hộ, hỗ trợ

Câu 1How many people are there in your family?

ANêu số người trong gia đình và đó là những ai.

RGiải thích kiểu gia đình (hạt nhân hay mở rộng) hoặc hoàn cảnh sống.

EVí dụ cụ thể về một thành viên hoặc đặc điểm của gia đình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

There are four people in my nuclear family: my parents, my younger sister, and me. We all live together in one house, so we are quite a close-knit family. My sister and I share a room, which means we are very close. I really enjoy living with all of them.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My extended family is quite large, but there are five people in my immediate household. We have my grandparents, my parents, and me. I am an only child, so I get a lot of attention from everyone. It can be noisy sometimes, but I love the warm atmosphere at home.

Câu 2Who are you closest to in your family?

ANêu tên hoặc vai vế của người thân thiết nhất.

RGiải thích lý do tại sao gần gũi với người đó.

EKể một việc cụ thể hai người thường làm hoặc từng chia sẻ cùng nhau.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I am closest to my mother in my family. She is always willing to listen to my problems and gives me very practical advice. Whenever I feel stressed about work or study, she is the first person I talk to. I think our family bond is very strong because we talk every day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think I am closest to my older brother. We grew up together and have always supported each other through difficult times. He was the one who encouraged me to pursue medicine, and I really look up to him. We still call each other every week even though we live in different cities now.

Câu 3What do you usually do together?

ANêu hoạt động phổ biến nhất mà cả gia đình thường làm cùng nhau.

RGiải thích tại sao hoạt động đó quan trọng hoặc được ưa thích.

EMô tả một dịp cụ thể hoặc thói quen thường xuyên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

We usually have dinner together every evening as a family. It is the best time for everyone to catch up and talk about the day. My mother cooks home-cooked meals which everyone loves. I think sharing meals is one of the most important family traditions we have.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My family loves to get together on weekends to watch movies or go for walks in the park. We also enjoy family gatherings during holidays like Tet, when all the relatives come to visit. These moments help us spend quality time with each other despite our busy schedules. It is something I really look forward to every week.

Câu 4Do you come from a large or small family?

ANêu gia đình lớn hay nhỏ và có bao nhiêu người.

RGiải thích cảm nhận về việc lớn lên trong gia đình đó.

EĐưa ra ví dụ về một lợi ích hoặc thách thức cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I come from a relatively small nuclear family with just three people: my parents and me. Growing up as an only child meant I had a lot of attention and support from my parents. However, I sometimes wished I had a sibling to play with. Overall, I think my close-knit family gave me a very stable childhood.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I come from quite a large extended family with many aunts, uncles, and cousins. There are always people around during holidays, which makes celebrations very lively. Sometimes it is a little noisy, but I love the energy of a big family gathering. I believe growing up in a large family taught me to share responsibilities and get along with many different people.

Câu 5What does your family usually do during holidays or Tet?

ANêu hoạt động chính gia đình thường làm trong dịp lễ hoặc Tết.

RGiải thích ý nghĩa hoặc lý do tại sao hoạt động đó đặc biệt.

EKể một kỷ niệm hoặc chi tiết cụ thể trong dịp đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

During Tet, my family always gets together at my grandparents' house for a big meal. It is our most important family tradition and everyone looks forward to it. My grandmother prepares special dishes and we exchange good wishes for the new year. These moments really strengthen our family bond.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We usually spend national holidays by going on short trips to the countryside. My parents believe that spending quality time in nature is good for the whole family. Last year, we visited Ha Long Bay together and it was a wonderful experience. I think these trips help us create lasting memories as a family.

Câu 6Are you similar to or different from your parents?

ANêu bạn giống hay khác bố mẹ ở điểm nào.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn nghĩ mình giống hoặc khác họ.

EĐưa ra một đặc điểm tính cách hoặc sở thích cụ thể để minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think I am quite similar to my father in terms of personality. Like him, I am very determined and I always try to finish what I start. He taught me to be disciplined, which has helped me a lot in my medical studies. I really look up to him as my role model and I hope to raise a family as well as he did.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe I am quite different from my parents in some ways. My parents are both very traditional, but I tend to have more modern views about work and lifestyle. For example, they prefer staying at home, while I enjoy going out and meeting new people. However, we share the same core values about the importance of family bond and education.

Câu 7Who makes most of the decisions in your family?

ANêu ai thường đưa ra quyết định trong gia đình.

RGiải thích tại sao người đó có vai trò đó.

EKể một ví dụ về quyết định quan trọng người đó đã đưa ra.

Trả lời mẫu 1

In my family, my father usually makes the major decisions, especially about finances and important plans. He is the main breadwinner and has a lot of experience in life. However, my mother always gives her opinion and my father respects it. I think they share responsibilities well and make a great team together.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think decisions in my family are made together rather than by one person. We usually get together and discuss any big issue before making a final choice. This approach helps everyone feel involved and respected. I learned from this that a supportive family is one where every voice is heard.

Câu 8Do you help with household chores at home?

ANêu bạn có giúp việc nhà không và giúp việc gì.

RGiải thích lý do bạn làm hoặc không làm việc nhà thường xuyên.

EKể tên một việc nhà cụ thể bạn thường làm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I try to help with household chores whenever I am at home. I usually cook on weekends and wash the dishes after meals. I believe that everyone in the family should share responsibilities so that no one person carries too much burden. It also makes me feel more connected to my family.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not help as much as I should because of my busy study schedule. However, when I have free time, I help my mother with cleaning and doing laundry. I think contributing to household chores is a way to show appreciation for the family. I plan to help more once my exams are finished.

Câu 9Has your family influenced your choice of career?

ANêu gia đình có ảnh hưởng đến lựa chọn nghề nghiệp của bạn không.

RGiải thích ảnh hưởng đó như thế nào hoặc tại sao không.

EKể về một người thân hoặc sự kiện cụ thể đã tác động đến quyết định của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, my family had a big influence on my decision to study medicine. My father is a doctor and I always looked up to him when I was young. Watching him help patients made me want to do the same. My whole close-knit family was very supportive when I chose this career path.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My family did not directly push me into medicine, but they always encouraged me to pursue a career that helps others. My grandmother was often sick when I was a child, and caring for her made me interested in health and medicine. My parents fully support my choice and often say they are proud of me. Their encouragement has been the strongest motivation for me.

Câu 10How often do you see your extended family, such as grandparents or cousins?

ANêu tần suất gặp gỡ họ hàng hoặc ông bà.

RGiải thích lý do gặp thường xuyên hoặc không thường xuyên.

EMô tả một dịp gặp mặt gần đây hoặc đáng nhớ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I see my extended family about two or three times a year, mainly during Tet and summer holidays. We are not able to meet more often because everyone lives in different provinces. However, whenever we do get together, it is always a joyful occasion with lots of food and laughter. I always feel a strong family bond during these reunions.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I see my grandparents almost every month because they live in the same city as me. I try to visit them on weekends whenever my schedule allows. These visits are very meaningful to me because my grandparents share many stories and life lessons. I think spending quality time with older family members is very important.

Câu 11What is something important your family has taught you?

ANêu một bài học quan trọng mà gia đình đã dạy bạn.

RGiải thích tại sao bài học đó có giá trị với bạn.

EKể cách bài học đó đã giúp ích cho bạn trong cuộc sống hoặc học tập.

Trả lời mẫu 1

The most important thing my family has taught me is to be responsible and hardworking. My parents always said that nothing comes without effort, and they showed me this through their own example. This lesson has helped me stay focused during my medical training even when it is very challenging. I am grateful for this value that comes from our family tradition.

Trả lời mẫu 2

My family taught me the importance of family bond and staying connected to the people you love. My parents always made time for us despite their busy jobs, which showed me that relationships matter more than material things. This teaching has shaped how I treat my own friends and colleagues. I hope to pass the same values on when I raise a family of my own.

Câu 12Do you think family is more important than friends? Why?

ANêu rõ quan điểm của bạn: gia đình quan trọng hơn hay bạn bè quan trọng hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn cho rằng như vậy.

EĐưa ra một tình huống cụ thể để minh họa quan điểm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I personally believe that family is slightly more important than friends, though both are valuable. My close-knit family has been with me since birth and knows me better than anyone else. When I had a serious problem during my first year of medical school, it was my family who helped me get through it. I think the family bond is simply deeper and longer-lasting.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think family and friends are equally important in different ways. My family provides me with unconditional love and a sense of belonging, while my friends give me energy and fresh perspectives. Sometimes I find it easier to share responsibilities and worries with close friends. I believe a happy life needs both a supportive family and good friendships.

Câu 13Would you like to live with your parents after you get married?

ANêu bạn có muốn sống cùng bố mẹ sau khi kết hôn không.

RGiải thích lý do muốn hoặc không muốn.

ENêu điều kiện hoặc tình huống cụ thể ảnh hưởng đến quyết định đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think I would prefer to live separately from my parents after I get married, at least for a while. Having our own space would help my future partner and me raise a family in our own way. Of course, I would always stay close to my parents and visit them often. I believe it is possible to maintain a strong family bond even without living under the same roof.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would not mind living with my parents after getting married, especially in Vietnamese culture where this is quite common. Living together would allow us to share responsibilities and take care of the elderly at home. It would also be easier for my parents to help with childcare if we get together every day. However, I think mutual respect and clear communication would be very important in that situation.

Câu 14How do members of your family support each other?

ANêu các thành viên gia đình hỗ trợ nhau như thế nào.

RGiải thích tại sao sự hỗ trợ đó quan trọng với bạn.

EKể một tình huống cụ thể mà bạn nhận hoặc cho đi sự hỗ trợ trong gia đình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My family is very supportive family and we always help each other in practical ways. When I was preparing for my university entrance exam, my parents reduced my household chores so I could focus on studying. My sister often helped me revise and test my knowledge. This kind of support made me feel that I was never alone in my challenges.

Trả lời mẫu 2

We support each other emotionally more than anything else. Whenever someone in the family is going through a hard time, the others make sure to listen and offer encouragement. For example, when my mother was ill last year, my father and I took turns caring for her and managing the home. I think this ability to share responsibilities during tough times is what makes us a truly close-knit family.

Câu 15What is your happiest memory with your family?

ANêu kỷ niệm hạnh phúc nhất mà bạn có với gia đình.

RGiải thích tại sao kỷ niệm đó đặc biệt hoặc ý nghĩa với bạn.

EMô tả chi tiết cụ thể về thời gian, địa điểm hoặc điều đã xảy ra.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My happiest memory is a family trip we took to Da Nang when I was about fifteen years old. It was the first time we had ever spent quality time away from home all together. We spent a whole week on the beach, eating seafood and watching sunsets. That trip made me realise how much I love my family and how important these family gatherings are.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think my happiest memory is the day I received my acceptance letter to medical school. My whole close-knit family gathered in the living room and everyone cried happy tears together. My grandmother prepared a big meal to celebrate and we stayed up late talking and laughing. It was a moment that showed me how much my supportive family cared about my future.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Weekends

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • stay at homeở nhà, không ra ngoài
  • go outra ngoài, đi chơi
  • recharge my energynạp lại năng lượng
  • catch up with friendsgặp gỡ, hội ngộ bạn bè
  • household choresviệc nhà
  • weekend routinethói quen cuối tuần
  • free timethời gian rảnh
  • outdoor activitieshoạt động ngoài trời
  • take a breaknghỉ ngơi, tạm dừng
  • quality timethời gian có chất lượng (bên gia đình/bạn bè)
  • unwindthư giãn, giải tỏa căng thẳng
  • sleep inngủ nướng, dậy muộn
  • explorekhám phá (nơi mới, hoạt động mới)
  • refreshedcảm thấy sảng khoái, tươi tỉnh trở lại

Câu 1What do you usually do at the weekend?

ANêu thẳng hoạt động chính bạn thường làm vào cuối tuần.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn chọn hoạt động đó (vì bận rộn cả tuần, cần nghỉ ngơi...).

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về một buổi cuối tuần điển hình của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually stay at home and rest at the weekend. During the week I have a very heavy schedule at the hospital, so I really need to take a break. For example, on Saturday morning I often sleep in until eight or nine o'clock and then read medical journals in the afternoon.

Trả lời mẫu 2

At the weekend I normally catch up with friends or go to the gym. I feel it is important to stay active even when I am not working. Last weekend, for instance, I went jogging in the park with two colleagues and we had coffee together afterwards.

Câu 2Do you prefer staying at home or going out? Why?

ANói rõ bạn thích ở nhà hay ra ngoài.

RĐưa ra lý do cụ thể cho sở thích đó.

EMô tả một ví dụ hoặc tình huống minh họa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer staying at home on weekends because my job as a medical student is very tiring. Being at home helps me recharge my energy for the new week. For example, I usually cook a simple meal, watch a documentary, and unwind on the sofa in the evening.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I definitely prefer going out whenever I have the chance. Staying indoors all week makes me feel restless, so I need fresh air and new surroundings. Last Sunday I took a short trip to a nearby park and I felt completely refreshed when I came back.

Câu 3Is the weekend important to you?

AKhẳng định rõ weekend có quan trọng với bạn không.

RNêu lý do tại sao nó quan trọng hoặc không quan trọng.

ECho ví dụ về điều bạn sẽ mất nếu không có cuối tuần.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, the weekend is very important to me. It is the only time I have to take a break from studying and clinical practice. Without the weekend, I would not have any quality time with my family, and I think that would affect my mental health.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, the weekend is extremely important to me because it gives me time to recharge my energy. I spend Monday to Friday in lectures and at the hospital, so two days of free time help me stay motivated. If I did not have weekends, I think I would burn out very quickly.

Câu 4Do you spend your weekends differently in summer and winter?

ANói bạn có thay đổi hoạt động theo mùa không.

RGiải thích thời tiết hoặc lịch học ảnh hưởng như thế nào.

ESo sánh một hoạt động mùa hè và một hoạt động mùa đông cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I spend my weekends quite differently depending on the season. In summer I enjoy outdoor activities like cycling or swimming because the weather is nice. In winter, however, I prefer to stay at home, drink hot tea, and review my study notes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really - my weekend routine is fairly similar all year. I usually do household chores on Saturday morning no matter the season. The only difference is that in summer I sometimes go to the park in the evening to enjoy the cool breeze.

Câu 5Do you think people need two days off at the weekend?

AĐưa ra ý kiến rõ ràng: có hay không cần hai ngày nghỉ.

RGiải thích lý do dựa trên sức khỏe, năng suất, hoặc cuộc sống cá nhân.

ELiên hệ đến kinh nghiệm cá nhân hoặc thực tế bạn từng thấy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe people need at least two days off at the weekend. One day is simply not enough to recharge my energy and complete all household chores. In my own experience, when I only had one day off during a busy rotation, I felt exhausted the whole following week.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think two days off is ideal, but one full day of real rest can also be enough. The most important thing is the quality time you spend, not just the number of hours. For example, one deeply relaxing day at home can sometimes feel more restorative than two busy days running errands.

Câu 6What time do you usually wake up on weekends?

ANói giờ bạn thường dậy vào cuối tuần.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn dậy vào giờ đó.

EMô tả bạn thường làm gì ngay sau khi thức dậy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

On weekends I usually sleep in until about eight o'clock, which is much later than on weekdays. I do this because my body needs extra rest after a long week of early hospital shifts. After waking up, I like to make a slow breakfast and enjoy my free time without any rush.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually wake up at the same time on weekends - around six in the morning - because my body clock is already set. I use the quiet morning hours to unwind, do some light stretching, and plan my day. I find that starting early helps me feel more refreshed and productive.

Câu 7Do you spend time with your family at the weekend?

ANói bạn có dành thời gian cho gia đình không.

RGiải thích lý do hoặc hoàn cảnh khiến bạn làm vậy.

ECho ví dụ về một hoạt động cụ thể bạn làm cùng gia đình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, spending quality time with my family is one of the most important parts of my weekend. I live quite far from home, so when I visit on weekends I really treasure every moment. We usually have a big family lunch together and catch up on each other's news.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to spend time with my family, but it depends on my duty schedule. When I am not on call, I call my parents on Saturday evening for a video chat. Occasionally I go out with my younger sibling to a cafe or a park, which I really enjoy.

Câu 8Do you ever do any exercise or sport at the weekend?

ANói rõ bạn có tập thể dục cuối tuần không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn tập hoặc không tập.

ENêu môn thể thao hoặc hình thức tập cụ thể bạn thích.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I try to do some exercise every weekend because I sit down most of the week. I usually go for a morning run or join a group fitness class at the gym near my dormitory. Last Saturday I ran five kilometres and felt much more refreshed for the rest of the day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not exercise as much as I should at the weekend. After a tiring week, I find it hard to motivate myself to do outdoor activities. However, I sometimes do simple stretching at home for about twenty minutes, which still helps me recharge my energy a little.

Câu 9Do you prefer a quiet weekend or a busy weekend?

AChọn rõ bạn thích cuối tuần yên tĩnh hay bận rộn.

RGiải thích lý do phù hợp với lối sống hoặc tính cách của bạn.

EMô tả một cuối tuần lý tưởng theo sở thích đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I definitely prefer a quiet weekend over a busy one. As a medical student I deal with a lot of pressure during the week, so I need time to simply unwind and do nothing special. My ideal weekend is staying in, reading a good book, and having a home-cooked meal.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer a busy weekend because I easily get bored when I stay at home all day. I like to plan different activities - catch up with friends, visit a new place, or join a community event. A full and varied weekend makes me feel that I have used my free time well.

Câu 10Have you ever had a weekend trip? Did you enjoy it?

ANói bạn đã từng đi chơi cuối tuần chưa và có thích không.

RGiải thích điều gì làm cho chuyến đi đó đáng nhớ hoặc không.

EKể ngắn về một địa điểm hoặc khoảnh khắc cụ thể trong chuyến đi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I went on a weekend trip to Ha Long Bay with some classmates last year and I absolutely loved it. The scenery was beautiful and it was a great chance to explore somewhere new outside of Hanoi. That trip helped me recharge my energy more than any normal weekend at home.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I had a short weekend trip to my hometown a few months ago to see my family. It was enjoyable but also a little tiring because the bus journey took four hours each way. I think a trip is most rewarding when you have enough time to unwind properly, not just rush back the next day.

Câu 11Do you help with household chores at the weekend?

ANói bạn có làm việc nhà cuối tuần không.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn làm hoặc không làm việc nhà.

ECho ví dụ về một việc nhà cụ thể bạn thường làm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always do some household chores on Saturday morning. I share a dormitory room, so I need to clean and tidy up regularly. Usually I do the laundry and mop the floor, and then I feel comfortable spending the rest of the weekend in a clean space.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I help with chores, but I try to finish them quickly so I have more free time for myself. I think it is important to keep my living space organised, but I do not want household chores to take up the whole weekend. So I usually set a two-hour limit and then stop and do something enjoyable.

Câu 12Do you use your phone or computer a lot at the weekend?

ANói bạn có dùng điện thoại hay máy tính nhiều cuối tuần không.

RGiải thích mục đích sử dụng và lý do bạn dùng nhiều hay ít.

ECho ví dụ về nội dung bạn xem hoặc việc bạn làm trên thiết bị.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do use my phone quite a lot at the weekend, mainly to catch up with friends and family through video calls. I also sometimes watch medical lectures or short educational videos online to stay up to date. However, I try to take a break from screens for at least a few hours each day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I try to reduce my screen time at the weekend to unwind properly. During the week I stare at a computer all day writing reports and studying, so weekends are my chance to rest my eyes. I prefer doing outdoor activities or reading a paper book instead of scrolling through my phone.

Câu 13Is there anything you do every weekend without fail?

ANói về một thói quen hoặc hoạt động bạn luôn làm mỗi cuối tuần.

RGiải thích tại sao hoạt động đó trở thành thói quen cố định.

EMô tả cụ thể khi nào và như thế nào bạn thực hiện thói quen đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I call my parents every Sunday evening without fail - it is a firm part of my weekend routine. We are quite close but I see them rarely because of my studies, so this call is very important to me. It only lasts about twenty minutes but it always gives me a sense of quality time with them.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Every weekend I make sure to prepare my clothes and notes for the coming week on Sunday afternoon. I know it sounds more like work than leisure, but it is part of my weekend routine and it helps me feel organised and calm. After that small task, I allow myself to fully relax for the rest of the evening.

Câu 14What do you enjoy doing on a rainy weekend?

ANói bạn thích làm gì khi cuối tuần trời mưa.

RGiải thích tại sao hoạt động đó phù hợp với thời tiết mưa.

EMô tả một buổi mưa cụ thể mà bạn cảm thấy thích.

Trả lời mẫu 1

On a rainy weekend I love to stay at home, make a hot drink, and watch a film or a documentary. The sound of rain makes it very easy to unwind and I feel no pressure to go out or be productive. Last month I had a whole rainy Saturday like this and I found it incredibly peaceful.

Trả lời mẫu 2

When it rains on the weekend I use the time to study topics I am curious about but rarely have time for. I see a rainy day as an opportunity to explore a new subject, such as reading about recent medical research. It does not feel like a waste of free time because I genuinely find it interesting.

Câu 15Would you like to change how you spend your weekends? Why or why not?

ANói bạn có muốn thay đổi cách dùng cuối tuần không.

RGiải thích điều bạn muốn giữ nguyên hoặc muốn thay đổi và tại sao.

ECho ví dụ về điều bạn sẽ làm khác nếu có thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would like to change my weekends a little. Right now I spend too much time on household chores and not enough time on outdoor activities or hobbies. If I could reorganise my schedule, I would finish all chores on Saturday morning and keep Sunday completely free to explore new places.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Not really - I am quite happy with my current weekend routine. I have found a good balance between resting, doing some exercise, and spending quality time with people I care about. The only small change I might make is to sleep in a little longer on Sunday, since that is the one day I truly have no commitments.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Learning English brings many advantages

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • communicate with foreignersgiao tiếp với người nước ngoài
  • language barrierrào cản ngôn ngữ
  • job opportunitiescơ hội việc làm
  • competitive job marketthị trường việc làm cạnh tranh
  • higher salarymức lương cao hơn
  • access informationtiếp cận thông tin
  • medical researchnghiên cứu y khoa
  • international journalstạp chí quốc tế
  • broaden your horizonsmở rộng tầm nhìn
  • cultural understandinghiểu biết văn hóa
  • global citizencông dân toàn cầu
  • professional developmentphát triển chuyên môn
  • self-confidencesự tự tin
  • fluent in Englishthông thạo tiếng Anh

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I strongly believe that learning English is one of the most valuable things a person can do today. There are several important reasons why I think this. First of all, English helps us communicate with foreigners much more easily. When we travel or meet people from other countries, English removes the language barrier. For example, as a medical student, I can talk to foreign doctors at international conferences and exchange ideas freely. Another reason is that English gives us better job opportunities. In today's competitive job market, employers prefer candidates who are fluent in English because they can work with international partners. For example, doctors who speak English can apply for jobs at foreign hospitals or receive a higher salary at private clinics. In addition, English allows us to access information that is not yet available in Vietnamese. Most important medical research and international journals are written in English, so we can update our knowledge and improve our professional development. On top of that, I think learning English also helps us broaden your horizons and develop cultural understanding. When we understand other cultures, we become more open-minded and can truly be a global citizen. It also builds our self-confidence when we speak successfully. To sum up, learning English brings many great advantages for both our personal and professional lives.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · Learning English (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • global languagengôn ngữ toàn cầu
  • compulsory subjectmôn học bắt buộc
  • communicate effectivelygiao tiếp hiệu quả
  • language barrierrào cản ngôn ngữ
  • medical terminologythuật ngữ y khoa
  • self-studytự học
  • language skillskỹ năng ngôn ngữ
  • online resourcestài nguyên trực tuyến
  • professional developmentphát triển nghề nghiệp
  • academic researchnghiên cứu học thuật
  • native speakerngười nói tiếng mẹ đẻ
  • improve fluencycải thiện sự lưu loát
  • pronunciationphát âm
  • international conferencehội nghị quốc tế

Câu 1Why is English important today?

ANêu thẳng: tiếng Anh là ngôn ngữ toàn cầu quan trọng nhất.

RGiải thích: dùng trong khoa học, y tế, kinh doanh và giao tiếp quốc tế.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ cần đọc tài liệu y khoa quốc tế hoặc tham dự hội nghị.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think English is extremely important today because it is the world's global language. Most scientific and medical knowledge is published in English, so professionals need it to stay updated. For example, as a doctor, I need to read academic research and medical terminology in English to provide the best care for my patients.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, English is vital in the modern world because it allows people to communicate effectively across different countries. Without English, there would be a serious language barrier in international business and science. For instance, doctors who attend international conferences need strong English skills to share and receive new medical knowledge.

Câu 2Is English difficult for students in your country?

AĐưa ra quan điểm: có, tiếng Anh khó với nhiều học sinh Việt Nam.

RGiải thích: hệ thống ngữ pháp và phát âm rất khác tiếng Việt.

EVí dụ: học sinh gặp khó khăn với phát âm và cách dùng mạo từ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I believe English is quite difficult for students in Vietnam. The main reason is that English pronunciation and grammar are very different from Vietnamese. For example, many students struggle with language skills like speaking and listening because they do not have many chances to practice with native speakers.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think English is challenging for Vietnamese students, but it depends on the person. The language barrier is real because Vietnamese has tones while English does not. However, with access to online resources and good teachers, many students can improve fluency over time if they study hard.

Câu 3Should English be compulsory at school?

ANêu quan điểm: có, tiếng Anh nên là môn bắt buộc.

RGiải thích: vì nó mở ra cơ hội học tập và nghề nghiệp trong tương lai.

EVí dụ: học sinh học tiếng Anh sớm sẽ dễ tiếp cận tài liệu quốc tế hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I strongly believe that English should be a compulsory subject at school. The reason is that English opens many doors for future education and careers. For example, students who learn English early can access better online resources, understand academic research, and have more opportunities for professional development later in life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, making English compulsory at school is a good idea because it gives every child an equal chance to learn the global language. Without this requirement, many students from less wealthy families might never learn English at all. Schools should make sure that English teaching focuses on real language skills like speaking and listening, not just grammar.

Câu 4How can students improve their English speaking skills effectively?

AGợi ý cách: luyện nói thường xuyên là cách tốt nhất.

RGiải thích: thực hành đều đặn giúp xây dựng sự tự tin và sự lưu loát.

EVí dụ: tham gia câu lạc bộ tiếng Anh hoặc xem phim tiếng Anh hàng ngày.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the best way to improve fluency in speaking is to practice every day without fear of making mistakes. Students should try to speak with others as much as possible, even in informal settings. For example, joining an English-speaking club or finding a language partner can help students communicate effectively and build confidence quickly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To improve speaking skills, I believe students should use online resources like podcasts and videos to listen to native speakers regularly. Listening carefully helps with pronunciation and natural expressions. For instance, watching medical lectures in English can help medical students learn both the global language and medical terminology at the same time.

Câu 5Do you think learning English is more important for medical students than for other students? Why?

ANêu quan điểm: có, tiếng Anh đặc biệt quan trọng hơn với sinh viên y khoa.

RGiải thích: hầu hết tài liệu y khoa và nghiên cứu đều bằng tiếng Anh.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ cần đọc tạp chí y khoa quốc tế và tham dự hội thảo.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think English is even more important for medical students than for others. Most academic research and medical terminology are written in English, so without this skill, doctors cannot access the latest knowledge. For example, to attend an international conference or read a medical journal, strong language skills are absolutely necessary.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that English is especially important for medical students. The reason is that medicine changes quickly and new findings are published in English first. If doctors cannot understand these updates, there could be a language barrier between them and modern treatments. Therefore, investing in professional development through English study is a duty, not just a choice.

Câu 6Should schools spend more time teaching English or focus on other subjects?

ANêu quan điểm: nên cân bằng, nhưng tăng thời gian tiếng Anh là cần thiết.

RGiải thích: tiếng Anh hỗ trợ tất cả các môn học khác trong thế giới hiện đại.

EVí dụ: học sinh dùng tiếng Anh để tìm tài liệu khoa học và toán học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think schools should spend more time on English without dropping other subjects. English is the global language that supports all other areas of learning. For example, a student who can use online resources in English can find better information for science, medicine, and math than a student who cannot.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, balance is important, but English definitely deserves more class time. The reason is that without strong language skills, students are limited in what they can learn and achieve. Students who study English well are better prepared for professional development and can communicate effectively with people all over the world.

Câu 7How has technology changed the way people learn English?

ANêu quan điểm: công nghệ đã làm cho việc học tiếng Anh dễ dàng và linh hoạt hơn.

RGiải thích: học viên có thể truy cập tài liệu và luyện tập mọi lúc mọi nơi.

EVí dụ: dùng app học tiếng Anh hoặc xem video YouTube của người nói tiếng Anh bản địa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Technology has completely changed how people learn English in a very positive way. With online resources like apps, websites, and video platforms, students can self-study at any time that suits them. For example, medical students can watch lectures by native speakers to improve both their pronunciation and their knowledge of medical terminology.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe technology has made it much easier to improve fluency in English. In the past, learners had few chances to hear real English, but now they can access millions of hours of content online. For instance, students can join virtual language groups, watch movies, and even take online courses to develop all their language skills from home.

Câu 8Do you think children learn English more easily than adults? Why?

ANêu quan điểm: trẻ em học tiếng Anh dễ hơn người lớn.

RGiải thích: não trẻ em linh hoạt hơn và tiếp thu ngôn ngữ tự nhiên hơn.

EVí dụ: trẻ học phát âm chuẩn hơn vì chúng chưa bị ảnh hưởng bởi tiếng mẹ đẻ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe children generally learn English more easily than adults. Young learners absorb language skills naturally without worrying too much about making mistakes. For example, children tend to develop better pronunciation because they can imitate native speakers very closely, while adults often keep their own accent from their first language.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, children do learn faster, but adults have some advantages too. Children pick up the global language through play and songs naturally, which makes it feel effortless. However, adults who use self-study methods and online resources can also improve fluency quickly if they are motivated, especially when they need English for professional development.

Câu 9Why do some people find it hard to speak English even after many years of study?

ANêu quan điểm: vì họ học nhiều ngữ pháp nhưng thiếu thực hành giao tiếp.

RGiải thích: hệ thống giáo dục truyền thống chú trọng thi cử hơn là nói chuyện.

EVí dụ: học sinh học 10 năm tiếng Anh nhưng không thể nói chuyện với người nước ngoài.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people struggle to speak English because they focus too much on grammar and not enough on real language skills like speaking and listening. The traditional education system often prepares students for tests, not for real conversation. As a result, even after years of study, students cannot communicate effectively when they meet a native speaker in real life.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, the main reason is a lack of practice and confidence. Many students are afraid to make mistakes, so they avoid speaking English at all. This creates a language barrier inside their own mind. If students use online resources more and try to speak every day, they can gradually improve fluency and overcome this problem.

Câu 10Should English be the official language of international medicine? Why or why not?

ANêu quan điểm: có, tiếng Anh nên là ngôn ngữ chính thức của y học quốc tế.

RGiải thích: vì nó giúp các bác sĩ trên toàn thế giới chia sẻ kiến thức dễ dàng hơn.

EVí dụ: hầu hết tạp chí y khoa hàng đầu thế giới đã được viết bằng tiếng Anh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think English should be recognised as the global language of international medicine because it already is in practice. Most top medical journals and academic research are published in English, so doctors worldwide depend on it. For example, understanding medical terminology in English is essential for any doctor who wants to attend international conferences or read the latest studies.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that English plays a key role in international medicine, but I think this also creates inequality. Doctors who struggle with language skills may miss important research because of the language barrier. Ideally, we should keep English as the main language for professional development in medicine, but also provide better translation tools and support for non-English speakers.

Câu 11How important is it to have good English pronunciation when communicating in a medical context?

ANêu quan điểm: phát âm rõ ràng rất quan trọng trong y tế vì có thể ảnh hưởng đến sự an toàn bệnh nhân.

RGiải thích: phát âm sai có thể gây hiểu lầm thuật ngữ y tế nghiêm trọng.

EVí dụ: nhầm lẫn tên thuốc hoặc liều lượng do phát âm không rõ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think pronunciation is very important in medical communication because misunderstandings can be dangerous. If a doctor cannot communicate effectively with a foreign colleague due to unclear speech, it could affect patient safety. For example, mixing up medical terminology because of poor pronunciation during an international conference or teleconsultation can lead to serious errors.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Good pronunciation is helpful, but I believe clarity and confidence matter more than sounding exactly like a native speaker. In medicine, the goal is to communicate effectively so that information is understood correctly. As long as doctors can explain medical terminology and discuss academic research clearly, small accent differences are usually not a problem in international settings.

Câu 12Do you think English teaching methods in Vietnam need to change? How?

ANêu quan điểm: có, phương pháp dạy tiếng Anh cần thay đổi để thực tế hơn.

RGiải thích: phương pháp hiện tại tập trung vào ngữ pháp và thi cử thay vì giao tiếp.

EVí dụ: nên tăng cường các hoạt động nói và nghe trong lớp học.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I definitely think English teaching in Vietnam needs to change. Currently, many classes focus too much on grammar for exams, and students do not develop real language skills. Schools should move toward more communicative activities, giving students chances to self-study and practice speaking so they can improve fluency and not just pass tests.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that teaching methods need to be updated. Teachers should use more online resources and bring real-life situations into the classroom, especially for medical students who need medical terminology and professional English. Instead of only testing grammar, schools should assess whether students can communicate effectively in situations they will actually face in their careers.

Câu 13Compare learning English in a classroom with learning English by yourself at home. Which is more effective?

ANêu quan điểm: kết hợp cả hai là tốt nhất, nhưng tự học có thể linh hoạt hơn cho người bận.

RGiải thích: lớp học cung cấp cấu trúc và phản hồi, tự học cho phép cá nhân hóa.

EVí dụ: bác sĩ bận có thể dùng app và video để tự học vào buổi tối.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think both have advantages, but classroom learning is more effective for beginners because a teacher can correct pronunciation and give structured lessons. In a classroom, students get immediate feedback and chances to practice language skills with others. However, for busy medical students, self-study using online resources can be a great way to continue learning outside class hours.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, self-study at home can be very powerful if the learner is motivated and disciplined. With so many online resources available, students can listen to native speakers, practice medical terminology, and improve fluency at their own pace. However, a classroom provides social interaction and a teacher who can identify weaknesses, so combining both methods is probably the most effective approach.

Câu 14Why might some Vietnamese doctors feel nervous about using English at international events?

ANêu quan điểm: vì họ lo lắng về phát âm và thiếu tự tin khi nói trước người nước ngoài.

RGiải thích: ít có cơ hội thực hành tiếng Anh trong môi trường thực tế.

EVí dụ: lo sợ người khác không hiểu hoặc cảm thấy xấu hổ khi mắc lỗi.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many Vietnamese doctors feel nervous at international conferences because they worry about their pronunciation and grammar. They have strong knowledge of medical terminology, but may lack confidence in spoken English. For example, they might fear that their accent will cause a language barrier and that colleagues will not understand them clearly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, the nervousness comes from limited practice with real English conversation. Most doctors in Vietnam study English for written exams, so they do not often communicate effectively in spoken situations. However, if they focus on professional development through English workshops and self-study with online resources, their confidence can grow significantly over time.

Câu 15Should hospitals in Vietnam require doctors to have a certain level of English proficiency? Why?

ANêu quan điểm: có, bệnh viện nên yêu cầu trình độ tiếng Anh nhất định.

RGiải thích: vì tiếng Anh cần thiết để đọc tài liệu y tế, điều trị bệnh nhân nước ngoài và hội nhập quốc tế.

EVí dụ: bệnh viện lớn tiếp nhận bệnh nhân quốc tế cần bác sĩ có thể giao tiếp bằng tiếng Anh.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe that hospitals should require a minimum level of English proficiency for their doctors. The reason is that English is the global language of medicine, and doctors must be able to read academic research and understand medical terminology from international sources. For example, a hospital that receives foreign patients needs doctors who can communicate effectively without a language barrier.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that some level of English ability should be required, especially for professional development and research. Doctors who cannot use English are cut off from the latest academic research and cannot participate in international conferences. However, hospitals should also provide support and training to help doctors improve fluency, rather than just setting a requirement without offering the resources to meet it.

CK1 · Speaking Practice Bank

Speaking Test 10

Part 1: Food and meals · Travel · Part 2: Living in a city has many benefits · Part 3: City life

A Answer – trả lời thẳng câu hỏiR Reason – nêu lý do, giải thíchE Example – ví dụ, chi tiết cụ thểĐậm đỏ = từ vựng chủ đề

Part 1

SOCIAL INTERACTION (Interview)

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Food and meals

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • favourite mealbữa ăn yêu thích
  • home-cooked mealsbữa ăn nấu tại nhà
  • eat outăn ngoài hàng
  • healthy foodthức ăn lành mạnh
  • street foodđồ ăn đường phố
  • balanced dietchế độ ăn cân bằng
  • skip a mealbỏ bữa
  • cook from scratchtự nấu từ nguyên liệu tươi
  • food habitsthói quen ăn uống
  • try new dishesthử các món mới
  • comfort foodmón ăn giúp cảm thấy dễ chịu/quen thuộc
  • snackđồ ăn vặt / bữa phụ
  • meal prepchuẩn bị bữa ăn trước
  • foreign cuisineẩm thực nước ngoài

Câu 1What is your favourite meal of the day?

ANêu tên bữa ăn yêu thích nhất trong ngày.

RGiải thích vì sao bữa đó đặc biệt với bạn.

ECho ví dụ về món ăn hoặc cảm giác khi có bữa đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite meal of the day is breakfast. I love it because it gives me energy to start a busy day at the medical academy. For example, I usually have a bowl of home-cooked meals|pho or some bread with eggs, and I always feel much better after eating a good breakfast.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think dinner is my favourite meal of the day. After a long day of studying and working, I can finally sit down and eat without rushing. My family usually prepares home-cooked meals together, which makes dinner feel special and relaxing.

Câu 2What do you usually eat for that meal?

ANêu cụ thể những món bạn thường ăn cho bữa đó.

RGiải thích lý do chọn những món đó (ngon, nhanh, tốt cho sức khoẻ...).

EMô tả một ví dụ cụ thể về bữa ăn đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

For breakfast, I usually eat a bowl of rice with some vegetables and a fried egg. I choose these foods because they are simple but give me enough energy. Sometimes I also have a cup of green tea to help me feel more alert before my morning classes.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I normally eat street food near the academy for lunch, like banh mi or rice with grilled meat. It is quick and affordable, which suits my busy schedule. Occasionally I try different stalls to try new dishes and discover new flavours.

Câu 3Do you like cooking? Why or why not?

ANói rõ bạn có thích nấu ăn hay không.

RĐưa ra lý do chính cho câu trả lời đó.

EKể một ví dụ về lần nấu ăn hoặc lý do không có thời gian nấu.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy cooking in my free time. I find it relaxing after a stressful day, and I like knowing exactly what goes into my food. Last weekend, I tried to cook from scratch a simple vegetable soup, and it turned out quite well.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, I do not like cooking very much because I am always very busy with my medical studies. I rarely have enough time to cook from scratch, so I usually buy street food or rely on the canteen. However, I do appreciate people who can prepare home-cooked meals because they are usually healthier.

Câu 4How many meals do you eat per day?

ANêu số bữa ăn bạn thường có mỗi ngày.

RGiải thích lý do ăn bấy nhiêu bữa (thói quen, lịch học...).

EĐưa ví dụ về một bữa cụ thể trong ngày của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually eat three meals a day: breakfast, lunch, and dinner. I try not to skip a meal because as a medical student I know how important it is for concentration and health. In the morning I always make time for at least a small breakfast before class.

Trả lời mẫu 2

On busy days, I sometimes only manage two meals because I skip a meal|skip lunch when I have long shifts at the hospital. I also eat a light snack in the afternoon to keep my energy up. I know this is not ideal for a balanced diet, but my schedule makes it difficult.

Câu 5Do you prefer eating at home or eating out? Why?

AChọn một trong hai: ăn ở nhà hoặc ăn ngoài.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích lựa chọn đó hơn.

ECho ví dụ cụ thể về tình huống ăn ở nhà hoặc ăn ngoài.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer home-cooked meals because they are healthier and more affordable. My mother always uses fresh ingredients and cooks without too much oil or salt. For example, on weekends when I go home, I always feel much better after eating her food compared to restaurant meals.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to eat out because I live in a dormitory and do not have a proper kitchen. There are many good and cheap places near the academy where I can find healthy food quickly. It also saves me a lot of time that I can use for studying.

Câu 6Do you have any food that you dislike or cannot eat?

ANêu tên món ăn hoặc loại thực phẩm bạn không thích hoặc không ăn được.

RGiải thích lý do (dị ứng, mùi vị, kết cấu...).

EKể một tình huống cụ thể liên quan đến món đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I really dislike eating bitter melon, even though I know it is very nutritious. The taste is too bitter for me and I find it hard to swallow. Once my aunt cooked it for me and I tried very hard to eat it politely, but I could not finish the dish.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I cannot eat very spicy food because it upsets my stomach. Whenever I accidentally order a dish that is too spicy, I feel uncomfortable for hours afterwards. I always check the menu carefully now and ask for mild versions of street food dishes.

Câu 7Do you think you have a healthy diet? Why or why not?

AĐánh giá chế độ ăn của bạn có lành mạnh không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn nghĩ vậy (ăn gì, bỏ gì...).

ENêu ví dụ cụ thể về thói quen ăn uống tốt hoặc chưa tốt của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think I have a fairly balanced diet because I try to eat vegetables and protein at every meal. As a medical student, I understand the importance of healthy food for both physical and mental performance. For example, I always include a portion of greens even when I eat out.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Honestly, my diet is not very healthy at the moment. I often skip a meal|skip breakfast when I am late for rounds, and I rely too much on snack|snacks and fast food. I know I need to improve my food habits, and I am trying to do meal prep on Sundays to fix this.

Câu 8Have you ever tried food from another country? What did you think of it?

ANêu tên món ăn nước ngoài bạn đã thử.

RNói cảm nhận của bạn về món đó (ngon, lạ, khó ăn...).

EMô tả cụ thể lần bạn ăn món đó ở đâu, với ai.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have tried Korean food, specifically kimchi and bibimbap. I was surprised by how flavourful and colourful the dish was. I tried it at a foreign cuisine restaurant in Ho Chi Minh City with my classmates, and we all enjoyed it very much.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I once tried Japanese sushi when a friend brought some back from a trip. At first I was nervous about eating raw fish, but I was glad I decided to try new dishes because the taste was really fresh and delicious. Now I want to explore more foreign cuisine when I have the chance.

Câu 9Do you often eat with your family or do you usually eat alone?

ANói bạn thường ăn cùng gia đình hay một mình.

RGiải thích lý do (sống xa nhà, lịch học bận...).

EMô tả một bữa ăn gia đình hoặc bữa ăn một mình điển hình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually eat alone or with my classmates because I live far from my family. I only go home once or twice a month, but when I do, we always have home-cooked meals together at the dinner table. Those shared meals feel very special and I always look forward to them.

Trả lời mẫu 2

During the week I eat alone in the canteen or grab street food quickly between classes. However, every Sunday I try to video call my parents while eating, so it feels a little like eating together. I miss my family's comfort food a lot when I am busy at the academy.

Câu 10Is there a traditional Vietnamese dish that you love? Can you describe it?

ANêu tên một món ăn truyền thống Việt Nam bạn yêu thích.

RGiải thích vì sao bạn yêu thích món đó.

EMô tả ngắn về nguyên liệu hoặc cách ăn món đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

My favourite traditional dish is bun bo Hue, a spicy beef noodle soup from Central Vietnam. I love it because the broth is rich and full of flavour, and it always makes me feel warm and satisfied. It is one of my go-to comfort food|comfort foods whenever I feel tired or stressed.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I love banh xeo, which is a crispy Vietnamese pancake filled with shrimp, pork, and bean sprouts. I enjoy it because you wrap it in fresh herbs and lettuce, which makes it both delicious and relatively healthy food|healthy. My grandmother used to cook from scratch|make it from scratch every time I visited her.

Câu 11Do you usually have breakfast before going to work or school?

ANói rõ bạn có ăn sáng đều đặn trước khi đi học hoặc đi làm không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn ăn sáng hoặc lý do bỏ bữa sáng.

ENêu ví dụ về một buổi sáng điển hình của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I always try to have breakfast before going to the hospital because I know that skip a meal|skipping breakfast makes it hard to concentrate during rounds. I usually eat something simple like bread with eggs or a small bowl of rice. I believe a good breakfast is an important part of a balanced diet.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Unfortunately, I often skip a meal|skip breakfast on weekdays because I have to leave the dormitory very early. I just grab a quick snack like a banana or some biscuits on the way. I am aware this is a bad food habit|food habit, and I am trying to change it by doing some meal prep the night before.

Câu 12How important is food to you in your daily life?

ANêu mức độ quan trọng của ăn uống đối với cuộc sống hàng ngày của bạn.

RGiải thích vì sao thức ăn quan trọng hoặc không quá quan trọng với bạn.

EDẫn ví dụ cụ thể từ cuộc sống sinh viên y khoa của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Food is very important to me because it directly affects my energy and focus during long hospital shifts. As a medical student, I need a balanced diet to stay sharp and perform well. If I eat poorly, I can really feel the difference in my concentration and mood throughout the day.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Food is moderately important to me. I care more about it being healthy food|healthy than being fancy or expensive. I think having regular and nutritious home-cooked meals or choosing wisely when I eat out is enough to keep me going through my demanding schedule.

Câu 13Do you ever try to eat more healthily? What do you do?

ANói có hay không bạn cố gắng ăn lành mạnh hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn muốn (hoặc không muốn) thay đổi chế độ ăn.

EKể một điều cụ thể bạn làm hoặc đã thử để ăn tốt hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have been trying to eat more healthily since I started my medical programme. I now eat more vegetables and fruits and try to avoid too much fried food. I also do meal prep on Sundays so I have healthy food ready for the busy weekdays ahead.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am trying to build better food habits, but it is not easy with my current workload. I have started choosing grilled or steamed dishes instead of fried ones when I eat out. I also drink more water and try to reduce snack|snacking on chips and sweets late at night.

Câu 14Do you like trying new or unusual foods? Why or why not?

ANói bạn có thích thử món ăn mới hoặc lạ không.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích khám phá hoặc lý do bạn thích ăn quen.

ECho ví dụ về một lần bạn thử hoặc từ chối thử một món lạ.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I love to try new dishes whenever I get the chance. I think trying different foods is a great way to learn about other cultures and expand my experience. For instance, I recently tried an Indian curry dish at a foreign cuisine restaurant and I was really impressed by the complex spices.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am quite conservative about food and prefer to stick to familiar Vietnamese dishes. I worry about upsetting my stomach, especially during a busy week at the hospital. However, I do try to try new dishes occasionally when I am with friends and the environment feels safe and relaxed.

Câu 15What is a typical meal you cook or buy when you are very busy?

AMô tả một bữa ăn đơn giản bạn thường làm hoặc mua khi bận rộn.

RGiải thích vì sao đó là lựa chọn phù hợp khi bận.

ENêu tình huống cụ thể khi bạn chọn bữa ăn đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

When I am very busy, I usually buy a banh mi or a box of rice with one dish from a nearby street food stall. It is fast, cheap, and filling enough to get me through the afternoon. During exam weeks, this becomes almost my daily lunch because I cannot afford to waste time.

Trả lời mẫu 2

When time is short, I rely on meal prep that I did the weekend before, like boiled eggs and pre-cooked rice with vegetables. I store them in small containers so I can just reheat and eat in five minutes. It is not the most exciting meal, but it keeps my balanced diet on track even on the hardest days.

Chủ đề

Part 1 · Travel

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • travel abroaddu lịch nước ngoài
  • local cuisineẩm thực địa phương
  • tourist attractionscác điểm du lịch hấp dẫn
  • book accommodationđặt chỗ ở
  • travel by planedi chuyển bằng máy bay
  • go sightseeingđi tham quan
  • pack lightđóng hành lý gọn nhẹ
  • travel on a budgetdu lịch tiết kiệm
  • cultural experiencetrải nghiệm văn hóa
  • take a day tripđi chơi trong ngày
  • scenic viewscảnh quan đẹp
  • travel companionbạn đồng hành
  • road tripchuyến đi bằng đường bộ
  • plan an itinerarylên kế hoạch lịch trình

Câu 1Do you like travelling?

ANêu thẳng bạn có thích du lịch không.

RGiải thích lý do (thư giãn, khám phá, học hỏi...).

ECho ví dụ về một chuyến đi gần đây hoặc nơi bạn đã đến.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I really enjoy travelling very much. It helps me relax and escape from my busy schedule as a medical student. I also love learning about new cultures and trying local cuisine. Last year I visited Da Nang and it was a wonderful experience.

Trả lời mẫu 2

To be honest, I like the idea of travelling, but I do not get many chances because of my studies. When I do travel, I feel refreshed and more motivated. I went on a short road trip to Ninh Binh with my classmates and I really enjoyed the scenic views there.

Câu 2What places would you like to visit?

ANêu tên địa điểm bạn muốn đến.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn muốn đến nơi đó.

EMô tả bạn sẽ làm gì hoặc xem gì ở đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I would really love to travel abroad to Japan one day. I am fascinated by Japanese culture and their advanced medical system. I would like to go sightseeing in Tokyo and visit some famous hospitals there. I think it would be an amazing cultural experience for me.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I would love to visit Europe, especially France. The history and architecture there look incredible in photos. I dream of seeing the famous tourist attractions like the Eiffel Tower. I would also enjoy trying the French local cuisine which I have heard is wonderful.

Câu 3Do you prefer travelling alone or with others? Why?

ANêu rõ bạn thích đi một mình hay đi cùng người khác.

RĐưa ra lý do cụ thể cho sự lựa chọn đó.

EKể về một lần đi cùng người khác hoặc đi một mình.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer travelling with others, especially with close friends or family. When I have a good travel companion, everything feels more fun and safe. We can share the costs and book accommodation together more easily. Last summer I went to Hoi An with three friends and we had a great time go sightseeing around the old town.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Personally, I think I prefer travelling alone sometimes. It gives me the freedom to plan an itinerary the way I want without compromise. I can spend as much time as I like at places that interest me. However, I always make sure to stay safe and book accommodation in well-known areas.

Câu 4How often do you travel?

ANêu tần suất bạn đi du lịch (thường xuyên hay hiếm khi).

RGiải thích lý do tại sao bạn đi nhiều hoặc ít.

EĐề cập đến chuyến đi gần nhất của bạn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not travel very often because I have a very busy timetable at the military medical academy. I usually manage one or two short trips a year during holidays. I like to take a day trip to nearby provinces when I have a free weekend. My last trip was to Tam Dao mountain just a few months ago.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I try to travel at least once every few months if possible. Even a short road trip within the country helps me recharge. I believe that travelling regularly makes me a better and more open-minded person. Last month I took a quick trip to Ha Long Bay with my family.

Câu 5What kind of transport do you prefer when you travel?

ANêu phương tiện bạn thích dùng khi đi du lịch.

RGiải thích ưu điểm của phương tiện đó.

ECho ví dụ về một chuyến đi bạn đã dùng phương tiện đó.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer to travel by plane when the destination is far away. It saves a lot of time, which is very important for me as a busy student. The flight experience is also quite comfortable if I choose a good airline. I flew to Ho Chi Minh City last year and it only took two hours.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually enjoy road trips by bus or car more than flying. I love watching the countryside and the scenic views along the way. It feels more like a real adventure compared to sitting on a plane. Last year I took an overnight bus to Hue and I really enjoyed the journey.

Câu 6Do you prefer beach holidays or mountain trips?

ANêu rõ bạn thích biển hay núi hơn.

RGiải thích lý do bạn thích loại hình đó.

EKể về một kỳ nghỉ ở biển hoặc núi bạn nhớ nhất.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer beach holidays because I find the sea very relaxing. I love swimming and enjoying the scenic views of the ocean. After weeks of studying medicine, a beach holiday helps me feel completely refreshed. My most memorable trip was to Phu Quoc island where the water was absolutely beautiful.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually prefer mountain trips over beach holidays. The cool air and quiet scenic views in the mountains help me think clearly. I enjoy go sightseeing in highland areas and learning about local ethnic communities. Last year I visited Sa Pa and had a wonderful cultural experience there.

Câu 7Have you ever travelled to another country?

ATrả lời có hoặc chưa đi nước ngoài.

RNếu có, chia sẻ cảm nhận; nếu chưa, giải thích lý do.

EKể về nước bạn đã đến hoặc nước bạn muốn đến đầu tiên.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I have had the chance to travel abroad once. I visited Thailand two years ago for a short medical conference trip. It was my first cultural experience outside Vietnam and I was amazed by the differences in food and lifestyle. I hope to travel abroad again after I finish my degree.

Trả lời mẫu 2

No, I have not yet had the opportunity to travel abroad. My studies at the military medical academy keep me very busy most of the year. However, I am planning to travel abroad to Singapore after I graduate. I have already started to plan an itinerary for that trip.

Câu 8What do you usually do to prepare before a trip?

ANêu các bước chuẩn bị bạn thường làm trước chuyến đi.

RGiải thích tại sao những bước đó quan trọng.

EĐưa ra ví dụ cụ thể từ một chuyến đi bạn đã chuẩn bị.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Before any trip, I always plan an itinerary carefully to make the most of my time. I research the tourist attractions and decide which ones are the most important to visit. I also book accommodation in advance to get better prices. For my trip to Da Nang, I planned everything two weeks ahead and it went very smoothly.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I usually start by searching for information about the destination online. Then I book accommodation and buy transport tickets early. I also try to pack light so I can move around easily without heavy bags. I learned this lesson after struggling with heavy luggage on my first long trip.

Câu 9Do you like trying local food when you travel?

ANêu bạn có thích thử đồ ăn địa phương khi đi du lịch không.

RGiải thích tại sao ăn uống là một phần quan trọng của du lịch.

EKể về một món ăn địa phương đáng nhớ bạn đã thử.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, trying local cuisine is one of my favourite parts of travelling. I believe food is the best way to understand a new culture and the people there. When I visited Hoi An, I tried the famous Cao Lau noodles and they were delicious. I always make sure to eat at small local restaurants rather than tourist places.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I enjoy trying local cuisine, but I am sometimes a little careful because of health concerns as a medical student. I know that food hygiene can vary a lot between different places. However, I still try to taste at least one or two special dishes at each destination. The local cuisine in Hue was particularly impressive with its rich and spicy flavours.

Câu 10Do you think travelling is important? Why?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về tầm quan trọng của du lịch.

RĐưa ra lý do cụ thể (mở rộng hiểu biết, giảm stress, ...).

EChia sẻ điều bạn học được từ một chuyến đi thực tế.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe travelling is very important for personal development. It opens your mind to different cultures and ways of thinking. As a future doctor, understanding diversity helps me work better with patients from different backgrounds. My cultural experience in different Vietnamese regions has already made me more empathetic.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think travelling is important but not the most important thing in life. It certainly helps to reduce stress and gain new perspectives. However, for medical students like us, time and money are limited. Even short domestic trips can provide a valuable cultural experience if we approach them with an open mind.

Câu 11Do you prefer a relaxing holiday or an adventurous one?

ANêu bạn thích kỳ nghỉ thư giãn hay phiêu lưu hơn.

RGiải thích lý do phù hợp với tính cách hoặc lối sống của bạn.

EMô tả một kỳ nghỉ lý tưởng của bạn trông như thế nào.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I prefer a relaxing holiday because my life as a medical student is already very stressful. I enjoy sitting on the beach, reading books, and enjoying the scenic views. I do not need a packed schedule full of activities to feel satisfied. My ideal holiday would be a quiet week at a coastal resort with good local cuisine.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer adventurous holidays because they make me feel truly alive. I love hiking, exploring new places, and having unexpected cultural experiences. I once went on a challenging trek to Fan Si Pan and it was the most rewarding trip of my life. I think adventure travelling builds both physical and mental strength.

Câu 12How do you usually find information about places to visit?

ANêu cách bạn tìm hiểu thông tin về điểm đến du lịch.

RGiải thích tại sao bạn tin tưởng nguồn thông tin đó.

ECho ví dụ về lần bạn tìm hiểu trước một chuyến đi cụ thể.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I usually search for information about tourist attractions and local cuisine on travel websites and social media. YouTube travel vlogs are especially helpful because I can see the places before I go. I also ask friends who have visited the destination for personal advice. Before my trip to Nha Trang, I spent a week reading reviews and blogs to plan an itinerary.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I prefer to ask friends or family who have already visited a place. Personal recommendations feel more reliable than online reviews. I also check if there are any famous tourist attractions that I should not miss. Once I have a list of places, I plan an itinerary and decide how many days to spend at each location.

Câu 13Do you prefer travelling to new places or revisiting places you have been before?

ANêu bạn thích khám phá nơi mới hay quay lại chỗ cũ.

RGiải thích lý do cho sự lựa chọn đó.

EKể về một nơi bạn muốn quay lại hoặc một nơi mới bạn muốn khám phá.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I always prefer travelling to new places rather than revisiting old ones. There are so many incredible tourist attractions in Vietnam and around the world that I have not seen yet. Every new destination brings a fresh cultural experience that I find exciting. I would rather discover something unknown than repeat the same trip.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I actually enjoy revisiting places I love. Some destinations like Hoi An have so much to offer that one trip is never enough. Going back allows me to go sightseeing in areas I missed the first time. I also feel more comfortable when I already know how to book accommodation and find the best local cuisine in a familiar city.

Câu 14Would you like to travel for work or study in the future?

ANêu bạn có muốn đi du lịch vì mục đích công việc hay học tập không.

RGiải thích lợi ích của việc đi công tác hoặc du học.

EĐề cập đến lĩnh vực hoặc quốc gia bạn quan tâm.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I would absolutely love to travel abroad for further medical training. I am interested in visiting hospitals and research centres in developed countries like Germany or Japan. It would be an incredible cultural experience as well as a professional development opportunity. I believe such a trip would make me a much better doctor when I return to Vietnam.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am a bit unsure about travelling long-term for work or study. I value being close to my family and my community here in Vietnam. However, if there was a short medical exchange programme, I would definitely consider it. Even a few weeks abroad could provide a valuable cultural experience and expose me to new medical technologies.

Câu 15Do you think it is better to travel on a budget or to spend more for comfort?

ANêu quan điểm của bạn về du lịch tiết kiệm hay du lịch sang trọng.

RGiải thích lý do bạn chọn cách đó.

EKể về một trải nghiệm liên quan đến chi phí khi đi du lịch.

Trả lời mẫu 1

As a student, I almost always travel on a budget. I try to pack light and stay in affordable guesthouses rather than expensive hotels. I find that travelling on a budget actually pushes me to interact more with local people and discover hidden gems. My most memorable cultural experience happened in a small family-run homestay that cost very little.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think it depends on the purpose of the trip. For short weekend breaks, I am happy to travel on a budget. But for a special trip like a honeymoon or a once-in-a-lifetime journey travel abroad, I would invest in more comfort. Good accommodation and comfortable travel by plane can make the whole experience much more enjoyable and stress-free.

Part 2

TOPIC DEVELOPMENT (Long turn)

Topic

Part 2 · Living in a city has many benefits

Từ vựng cho bài nói (học thuộc)

  • job opportunitiescơ hội việc làm
  • career developmentphát triển nghề nghiệp
  • competitive salarymức lương cạnh tranh
  • public servicesdịch vụ công cộng
  • entertainment optionscác lựa chọn giải trí
  • public transportationgiao thông công cộng
  • quality educationgiáo dục chất lượng cao
  • well-equipped schoolstrường học được trang bị tốt
  • advanced medical carechăm sóc y tế tiên tiến
  • specialist doctorsbác sĩ chuyên khoa
  • modern facilitiescơ sở vật chất hiện đại
  • networkingxây dựng mạng lưới quan hệ
  • cultural activitiescác hoạt động văn hóa
  • higher standard of livingmức sống cao hơn

Bài nói mẫu (≈2 phút)

I would like to talk about why living in a city has many benefits. In my opinion, cities offer people a much better quality of life compared to rural areas, and there are several reasons for this. First of all, cities provide far more job opportunities than the countryside. There are many companies and businesses in urban areas, so people can find work more easily and enjoy better career development. For example, in Ho Chi Minh City, graduates can apply to hundreds of firms and often earn a competitive salary. Another reason is that cities have more public services and entertainment options. People can use public transportation, visit shopping malls, parks, cinemas, and restaurants. For instance, in Hanoi, there are dozens of museums and cultural centres that make life more enjoyable. In addition, cities usually offer quality education. There are many well-equipped schools and universities with experienced teachers and modern facilities, which help students achieve better results. On top of that, I personally think that advanced medical care is a huge advantage. Cities have large hospitals with specialist doctors, so people can receive treatment quickly and effectively. This is especially important for serious illnesses. To sum up, living in a city brings many benefits including better jobs, services, education, and healthcare. That is why more and more people are choosing a higher standard of living in urban areas.

Part 3

EXTENDED QUESTIONS (Two-way discussion)

Chủ đề mở rộng

Part 3 · City life (extended discussion)

Từ vựng chủ đề (học thuộc)

  • urban areakhu vực đô thị
  • job opportunitiescơ hội việc làm
  • traffic congestiontắc nghẽn giao thông
  • cost of livingchi phí sinh hoạt
  • public transportphương tiện giao thông công cộng
  • pollution levelsmức độ ô nhiễm
  • quality of lifechất lượng cuộc sống
  • modern facilitiescơ sở vật chất hiện đại
  • green spaceskhông gian xanh
  • rural areakhu vực nông thôn
  • overcrowdingtình trạng đông đúc quá mức
  • social servicesdịch vụ xã hội
  • standard of livingmức sống
  • fast-paced lifestylelối sống nhịp độ nhanh

Câu 1Why do many people move to big cities?

ANêu lý do chính: tìm kiếm cơ hội tốt hơn.

RGiải thích: thành phố có nhiều việc làm, trường học và dịch vụ hơn.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa lên thành phố học chuyên sâu hoặc làm bệnh viện lớn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people move to big cities mainly because of job opportunities. Cities have more hospitals, companies, and universities, so people can find better careers and higher salaries there. For example, many young doctors move to Hanoi or Ho Chi Minh City to work in large hospitals with modern facilities and advanced equipment. Without these opportunities, it would be very hard to build a successful career.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, people move to cities because they want a higher standard of living. Cities offer better schools, hospitals, and entertainment options that are not available in smaller towns. For instance, a family might move to the city so their children can attend good schools and have access to social services like libraries and sports centers. The chance to improve their lives is the main reason for this move.

Câu 2What problems do people face in cities?

ANêu vấn đề lớn nhất: ô nhiễm và tắc đường.

RGiải thích: dân số đông gây ra nhiều áp lực về hạ tầng và môi trường.

EVí dụ: mất nhiều giờ đồng hồ đi lại mỗi ngày hoặc chi phí thuê nhà rất cao.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I believe the biggest problem in cities is traffic congestion. Because so many people live and work in the same area, the roads become very crowded, especially during rush hour. For example, in Hanoi, some people spend two hours every day just commuting to work. This wastes time and also increases pollution levels, which is bad for everyone's health.

Trả lời mẫu 2

One serious problem that city residents face is the high cost of living. Rent, food, and transport are all more expensive in cities than in the countryside. For instance, a young nurse working in a city hospital may spend more than half her salary on rent alone. This financial pressure can cause a lot of stress and make it difficult for people to save money or enjoy a good quality of life.

Câu 3Is city life better than life in the countryside?

AĐưa ra quan điểm rõ ràng: thành phố hoặc nông thôn tốt hơn tùy hoàn cảnh.

RGiải thích: thành phố có nhiều tiện ích nhưng nông thôn có môi trường trong lành hơn.

EVí dụ: người trẻ muốn phát triển sự nghiệp thường thích thành phố, người cao tuổi thích nông thôn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think city life can be better for young people who want to develop their careers. Cities provide job opportunities, good hospitals, and many educational choices that are simply not available in rural areas. For example, a medical student needs to be near a large teaching hospital to get the best training. However, I also believe that life in the countryside is healthier and more peaceful for families with children.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, life in the countryside is actually better in some ways. Rural areas have cleaner air, less noise, and more green spaces, which are very important for physical and mental health. For instance, people who live in villages tend to have lower stress levels because they are not dealing with traffic congestion or overcrowding every day. That said, cities do offer more opportunities, so the best choice really depends on a person's goals and lifestyle.

Câu 4How do you think cities can reduce traffic congestion?

AĐề xuất giải pháp: cải thiện giao thông công cộng.
