RGiải thích: nếu xe buýt và tàu điện tốt hơn, người dân sẽ bỏ xe cá nhân.

EVí dụ: tuyến tàu điện ngầm ở Hà Nội đang giúp giảm lượng xe máy.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think the most effective way to reduce traffic congestion is to improve public transport. If buses and trains are fast, clean, and affordable, more people will choose to leave their motorbikes at home. For example, the new metro line in Hanoi has already encouraged many commuters to use the train instead of driving. Investing in better transport infrastructure is the key to solving this problem long-term.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I believe cities should encourage people to work from home more often to reduce traffic congestion. During the pandemic, many offices allowed remote work and the streets became much less crowded. For instance, if hospitals allowed administrative staff to work online for two or three days a week, fewer people would need to travel during peak hours. Combining remote work with better public transport could make a real difference.

Câu 5Do you think governments should do more to control the growth of big cities?

AĐưa ra lập luận: có hoặc không, chính phủ cần can thiệp.

RGiải thích: tăng trưởng không kiểm soát dẫn đến quá tải hạ tầng và ô nhiễm.

EVí dụ: xây dựng các thành phố vệ tinh hoặc chuyển cơ quan nhà nước ra ngoại ô.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I strongly believe governments should do more to control urban area growth. When cities expand too quickly without planning, the result is overcrowding, poor housing, and overloaded hospitals and schools. For example, the Vietnamese government has discussed moving some ministries out of Hanoi to reduce pressure on the city center. Careful planning can help spread the population more evenly and improve quality of life for everyone.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I am not sure that strict government control is always the right answer. People should have the freedom to live where they choose, and cities grow because they offer real benefits like job opportunities and social services. Instead of trying to stop growth, governments should focus on making cities more sustainable by building better infrastructure. For instance, creating satellite towns around major cities can absorb new residents without increasing pollution levels in the center.

Câu 6How does living in a city affect people's health?

ANêu tác động: cả tích cực lẫn tiêu cực đến sức khỏe.

RGiải thích: ô nhiễm và stress làm hại sức khỏe, nhưng bệnh viện tốt hơn là lợi thế.

EVí dụ: bệnh hô hấp phổ biến hơn ở thành phố do ô nhiễm không khí.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think living in a city has a mostly negative effect on physical health. High pollution levels from vehicles and factories can cause serious respiratory problems, especially for children and the elderly. For example, studies show that people living in large cities like Hanoi have higher rates of asthma and lung disease than those in rural areas. The fast-paced lifestyle also causes stress, which can lead to heart disease and mental health issues.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, city life can actually benefit health in some ways. Cities have better hospitals with specialist doctors, advanced equipment, and faster emergency services. As a medical professional, I know that early diagnosis and treatment are critical, and these are easier to access in urban areas. For instance, a patient in the city can see a cardiologist within days, while someone in the countryside might wait months. So despite the pollution levels, access to healthcare is a major health advantage of city living.

Câu 7Should cities invest more in green spaces and parks?

AĐưa ra ý kiến ủng hộ: thành phố cần đầu tư vào không gian xanh.

RGiải thích: không gian xanh giúp cải thiện sức khỏe tâm thần và giảm ô nhiễm.

EVí dụ: công viên Thống Nhất ở Hà Nội là nơi nghỉ ngơi quan trọng cho người dân.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Absolutely, I think cities should invest much more in green spaces and parks. Research shows that spending time in nature reduces stress and improves mental health, which is very important in a stressful urban area. For example, Thong Nhat Park in Hanoi gives thousands of residents a place to exercise, relax, and breathe cleaner air every day. Without enough parks, city life becomes too stressful and unhealthy.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I agree that green spaces are important, but cities also need to balance this with housing and transport needs. Land in city centers is very expensive, so it is hard to create large parks in every neighborhood. Perhaps a better approach is to add small gardens on rooftops and along streets, which can still reduce pollution levels and give people some connection with nature. For instance, Singapore has done this very successfully with its 'City in a Garden' vision, and other cities could follow this model.

Câu 8Why do you think some people prefer to live in the suburbs rather than the city center?

ANêu lý do: muốn không gian rộng hơn và môi trường yên tĩnh hơn.

RGiải thích: trung tâm thành phố đắt đỏ, ồn ào và ô nhiễm hơn vùng ngoại ô.

EVí dụ: gia đình có con nhỏ thường chọn ngoại ô để có nhà rộng và trường học tốt.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think many people choose the suburbs because the cost of living is much lower than in the city center. For the same price as a small apartment downtown, a family can rent or buy a larger house with a garden in the suburbs. For instance, many young families in Hanoi are moving to districts like Ha Dong or Long Bien where they can have more space and a quieter environment, while still commuting to work by public transport.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my view, people prefer the suburbs because they offer a better quality of life for families. There is less noise, less traffic congestion, and more green spaces compared to the busy city center. For example, children can play outside more safely in suburban neighborhoods. At the same time, modern suburbs still have shopping centers, schools, and hospitals, so residents do not have to sacrifice access to modern facilities.

Câu 9How has technology changed life in modern cities?

ANêu thay đổi lớn nhất: công nghệ giúp cuộc sống tiện lợi và hiệu quả hơn.

RGiải thích: ứng dụng điện thoại và internet giúp giải quyết nhiều vấn đề đô thị.

EVí dụ: đặt xe công nghệ, mua hàng online và khám bệnh từ xa.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think technology has made city life much more convenient. People can now use apps to book taxis, order food, or pay bills without leaving their homes, which saves a lot of time in a fast-paced lifestyle. For example, during the pandemic, many patients in Vietnamese cities used telemedicine apps to consult doctors online instead of going to crowded hospitals. This kind of innovation is transforming how urban areas function.

Trả lời mẫu 2

Technology has certainly improved city life, but I think it has also created new problems. For instance, more people working from home means they use more electricity and generate more digital waste, which can increase a city's environmental footprint. Also, not everyone can afford smart devices or fast internet, so technology can sometimes increase inequality in urban areas. We need to make sure that the benefits of technology improve quality of life for all city residents, not just the wealthy.

Câu 10Do you think it is important for cities to have good public transport systems?

AKhẳng định tầm quan trọng: giao thông công cộng tốt là thiết yếu cho đô thị.

RGiải thích: giảm ùn tắc, ô nhiễm và chi phí đi lại cho người dân.

EVí dụ: tuyến metro Cát Linh - Hà Đông đang thay đổi thói quen di chuyển ở Hà Nội.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think good public transport is absolutely essential for any modern city. When people have access to reliable buses, trains, and metros, they are less likely to use private vehicles, which reduces traffic congestion and pollution levels. For example, cities like Tokyo and Singapore have world-class transport networks that allow millions of people to travel efficiently every day. Vietnamese cities like Hanoi should continue to develop their metro systems to achieve similar results.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I strongly agree that public transport is vital, especially for people who cannot afford cars or motorbikes. Good transport systems help reduce social inequality by giving everyone equal access to job opportunities, hospitals, and schools. For instance, a hospital worker who lives far from the city center can only afford to commute if public transport is cheap and reliable. Without this, the cost of living becomes too high for low-income residents to stay in the city.

Câu 11How can cities make life better for elderly people?

AĐề xuất giải pháp: cải thiện dịch vụ y tế, giao thông và không gian công cộng cho người cao tuổi.

RGiải thích: người già cần môi trường an toàn, dễ tiếp cận và có cộng đồng hỗ trợ.

EVí dụ: lắp thêm ghế nghỉ, thang máy ở ga tàu và tổ chức câu lạc bộ sức khỏe.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think cities can do a lot to improve life for elderly people, starting with better public transport that is easier to use. Many older people find it difficult to navigate crowded buses or walk long distances to transport stops. For example, cities could install more seating areas, add ramps and lifts in metro stations, and offer discounted transport passes for seniors. These simple changes would allow elderly residents to remain urban area independent for longer.

Trả lời mẫu 2

As a medical professional, I believe cities must prioritize access to social services and healthcare for the elderly. Many older people live alone in cities and can feel very isolated, which is bad for their mental and physical health. For instance, community centers that offer exercise classes, health check-ups, and social activities can greatly improve their quality of life. Cities that invest in elderly care now will be much better prepared for an aging population in the future.

Câu 12Should cities limit the number of private cars to reduce pollution?

AĐưa ra lập luận: có nên hạn chế ô tô cá nhân hay không.

RGiải thích: ô tô cá nhân là nguồn gây ô nhiễm lớn nhưng nhiều người vẫn cần chúng.

EVí dụ: một số thành phố châu Âu cấm xe vào trung tâm và kết quả rất tích cực.

Trả lời mẫu 1

Yes, I think cities should take strong steps to limit private cars in order to reduce pollution levels. Too many cars on the roads are a major cause of both traffic congestion and air pollution, which damages public health. For example, cities like Amsterdam and Oslo have successfully reduced car use by creating car-free zones in the center and making public transport much more attractive. Vietnamese cities could adopt similar policies gradually.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think limiting cars is a good idea in theory, but it is very difficult to do in practice without a strong alternative transport system first. Many people in urban areas rely on their cars or motorbikes because buses and trains are not yet reliable enough. For instance, a doctor who works night shifts may have no safe way to get home without a private vehicle. Therefore, I believe the government should first invest heavily in public transport before restricting private car use.

Câu 13Why do you think air pollution is a serious problem in many cities today?

AXác định nguyên nhân: quá nhiều phương tiện cơ giới và hoạt động công nghiệp.

RGiải thích: ô nhiễm không khí gây ra nhiều bệnh nguy hiểm cho người dân đô thị.

EVí dụ: Hà Nội thường xuyên có chỉ số AQI ở mức nguy hại, đặc biệt vào mùa đông.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think air pollution is so serious in cities today mainly because of the huge number of vehicles on the roads. Millions of motorbikes, cars, and trucks burn fuel every day and release harmful gases into the air. For example, Hanoi regularly records very high pollution levels, especially in winter when the weather traps dust and exhaust fumes near the ground. This is directly linked to the high rates of respiratory disease that we see in city hospitals.

Trả lời mẫu 2

In my opinion, air pollution in cities is caused by a combination of factors, not just traffic congestion. Industrial factories located near urban areas, construction dust, and the burning of waste all contribute significantly to poor air quality. For instance, many cities in Vietnam still have factories and power plants that do not have strict pollution controls. Until governments enforce stronger environmental regulations, pollution levels will remain dangerously high in many cities.

Câu 14How do you think city life affects young people compared to older generations?

ASo sánh tác động: người trẻ hưởng lợi nhiều từ cơ hội thành phố, người lớn tuổi có thể cảm thấy khó thích nghi.

RGiải thích: người trẻ linh hoạt hơn và quen với nhịp sống nhanh, người già cần sự ổn định.

EVí dụ: sinh viên y khoa tận dụng cơ sở đào tạo hiện đại, nhưng người cao tuổi có thể cảm thấy cô đơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I think city life generally benefits young people more than older generations. Young people can take advantage of job opportunities, universities, and the exciting fast-paced lifestyle that cities offer. For example, young medical students in Hanoi can access world-class teaching hospitals, specialist libraries, and international conferences that simply do not exist in the countryside. This gives them a huge advantage in building their careers.

Trả lời mẫu 2

While cities are exciting for the young, I think the fast-paced lifestyle and high cost of living can actually be harder for older generations to cope with. Elderly people often struggle with overcrowding, noisy environments, and complex digital systems that modern cities rely on. For instance, an older resident who has lived in a village all their life may find it very stressful to navigate a busy city hospital or use an online payment app. Cities need to ensure that their social services are designed to support all age groups, not just the young.

Câu 15Do you think people who live in cities are happier than those who live in rural areas?

AĐưa ra quan điểm: hạnh phúc phụ thuộc vào cá nhân, không phải nơi sống.

RGiải thích: thành phố có tiện ích nhưng nông thôn có cộng đồng gắn kết và ít căng thẳng hơn.

EVí dụ: nhiều người từ nông thôn nói họ hạnh phúc hơn dù thu nhập thấp hơn.

Trả lời mẫu 1

I do not think city people are necessarily happier. While cities offer more job opportunities and modern facilities, they also bring high cost of living, stress, and social isolation. Research suggests that people in rural areas often report higher levels of life satisfaction because they have stronger community ties and a slower, more relaxed lifestyle. For example, many Vietnamese who move from villages to cities say they miss the closeness of their home communities, even if their income has improved.

Trả lời mẫu 2

I think happiness in cities depends on a person's personality and priorities. For someone who loves excitement, variety, and career growth, the fast-paced lifestyle of an urban area can bring great satisfaction. For example, a young doctor who enjoys working with the latest technology in a city hospital may feel very fulfilled. But for someone who values peace, nature, and strong family connections, rural areas will always feel more like home. There is no single answer, because quality of life means different things to different people.
